+-- Exact source partial charges initialize the existing timed owner at mint.
+-- The immutable claim receipt proves initialization; later timed writes retain
+-- their existing revision, cause, custody and mutation proofs.
+CREATE FUNCTION game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(p_plan JSONB, p_contents JSONB)
+RETURNS BOOLEAN LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$
+DECLARE entry JSONB; previous INTEGER := -1; ordinal INTEGER;
+BEGIN
+    IF p_plan IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p_plan) <> 'array'
+       OR jsonb_array_length(p_plan)>21 THEN RETURN FALSE; END IF;
+    FOR entry IN SELECT value FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_plan) LOOP
+        IF jsonb_typeof(entry)<>'object'
+           OR NOT entry ?& ARRAY['ordinal','charges_remaining']
+           OR (SELECT count(*) FROM jsonb_object_keys(entry))<>2
+           OR jsonb_typeof(entry->'ordinal')<>'number'
+           OR jsonb_typeof(entry->'charges_remaining')<>'number'
+           OR (entry->>'charges_remaining')::NUMERIC<>1 THEN RETURN FALSE; END IF;
+        ordinal:=(entry->>'ordinal')::INTEGER;
+        IF (entry->>'ordinal')::NUMERIC<>ordinal OR ordinal<=previous
+           OR ordinal>jsonb_array_length(p_contents) THEN RETURN FALSE; END IF;
+        IF ordinal>0 AND ((p_contents->(ordinal-1)->>'quantity')::BIGINT<>1
+           OR (p_contents->(ordinal-1)->>'key_number')::INTEGER<>0
+           OR p_contents->(ordinal-1)->'text'<>'null'::jsonb) THEN RETURN FALSE; END IF;
+        previous:=ordinal;
+    END LOOP;
+    RETURN TRUE;
+EXCEPTION WHEN invalid_text_representation OR numeric_value_out_of_range THEN RETURN FALSE;
+END $$;
+
+ALTER TABLE game_reward_claim_mint_reservations ADD COLUMN charges_plan JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
+    ADD CONSTRAINT game_reward_claim_reserved_charges_shape CHECK (
+        game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(charges_plan,contents_plan)
+        AND (NOT charges_plan @> '[{"ordinal":0}]'::jsonb OR (root_text IS NULL AND contents_capacity IS NULL)));
+ALTER TABLE game_reward_claim_mint_receipts ADD COLUMN charges_plan JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
+    ADD CONSTRAINT game_reward_claim_committed_charges_shape CHECK (
+        game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(charges_plan,contents_plan)
+        AND (NOT charges_plan @> '[{"ordinal":0}]'::jsonb OR (quantity=1 AND root_text IS NULL AND contents_capacity IS NULL)));
+
+CREATE FUNCTION game_reward_claim_charge_initialization_proven(p_item UUID, p_charges INTEGER)
+RETURNS BOOLEAN LANGUAGE sql SECURITY DEFINER AS $$
+    SELECT EXISTS (
+        SELECT 1 FROM game_reward_claim_mint_receipts r
+        JOIN game_reward_claim_mint_reservations v USING(game_session_id,command_id)
+        JOIN game_item_instances i ON i.item_instance_id=p_item
+        CROSS JOIN LATERAL jsonb_array_elements(r.charges_plan) f
+        WHERE r.created_xact_id=pg_current_xact_id() AND r.charges_plan=v.charges_plan
+          AND r.contents_plan=v.contents_plan AND r.intent_binding=v.intent_binding
+          AND r.transaction_id=v.transaction_id AND r.item_instance_id=v.item_instance_id
+          AND r.character_id=v.character_id AND i.world_id=v.world_id
+          AND i.lifecycle=1 AND i.quantity=1 AND i.state_revision=1 AND i.last_transaction_id IS NULL
+          AND i.definition_family='Item' AND i.definition_revision_ref='definition-r1'
+          AND i.definition_production_key IN ('oteryn:item.tibia.i3048','oteryn:item.tibia.i3054',
+              'oteryn:item.tibia.i3081','oteryn:item.tibia.i3082','oteryn:item.tibia.i3083','oteryn:item.tibia.i3085')
+          AND p_charges=1 AND (f->>'charges_remaining')::INTEGER=p_charges
+          AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM game_item_keys WHERE item_instance_id=p_item)
+          AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM game_item_texts WHERE item_instance_id=p_item)
+          AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM game_item_fluid_states WHERE item_instance_id=p_item)
+          AND CASE WHEN (f->>'ordinal')::INTEGER=0 THEN i.item_instance_id=r.item_instance_id
+                AND i.minted_transaction_id=r.transaction_id
+              ELSE i.item_instance_id=(r.contents_plan->((f->>'ordinal')::INTEGER-1)->>'item_instance_id')::UUID
+                AND i.minted_transaction_id=(r.contents_plan->((f->>'ordinal')::INTEGER-1)->>'transaction_id')::UUID END
+          AND game_reward_claim_container_complete(r.transaction_id)
+    );
+$$;
+
+CREATE FUNCTION game_reward_claim_charges_complete(p_transaction UUID)
+RETURNS BOOLEAN LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER AS $$
+DECLARE r game_reward_claim_mint_receipts%ROWTYPE; v game_reward_claim_mint_reservations%ROWTYPE;
+        item UUID; ordinal INTEGER; expected INTEGER; actual game_item_timed_states%ROWTYPE;
+BEGIN
+    SELECT * INTO r FROM game_reward_claim_mint_receipts
+     WHERE transaction_id=p_transaction AND created_xact_id=pg_current_xact_id();
+    IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN FALSE; END IF;
+    SELECT * INTO v FROM game_reward_claim_mint_reservations
+     WHERE game_session_id=r.game_session_id AND command_id=r.command_id;
+    IF NOT FOUND OR r.charges_plan IS DISTINCT FROM v.charges_plan THEN RETURN FALSE; END IF;
+    FOR ordinal IN 0..jsonb_array_length(r.contents_plan) LOOP
+        IF ordinal=0 THEN item:=r.item_instance_id;
+        ELSE item:=(r.contents_plan->(ordinal-1)->>'item_instance_id')::UUID; END IF;
+        SELECT (f->>'charges_remaining')::INTEGER INTO expected
+          FROM jsonb_array_elements(r.charges_plan) f WHERE (f->>'ordinal')::INTEGER=ordinal;
+        SELECT * INTO actual FROM game_item_timed_states WHERE item_instance_id=item;
+        IF expected IS NULL THEN
+            IF FOUND THEN RETURN FALSE; END IF;
+        ELSIF NOT FOUND OR actual.charges IS DISTINCT FROM expected OR actual.state_revision<>1
+           OR actual.remaining_ms IS NOT NULL OR actual.deadline_at IS NOT NULL
+           OR NOT game_reward_claim_charge_initialization_proven(item,expected)
+        THEN RETURN FALSE; END IF;
+    END LOOP;
+    RETURN TRUE;
+END $$;
+
+DO $migration$
+DECLARE name TEXT; body TEXT; arm TEXT; insert_at INTEGER;
+BEGIN
+    FOREACH name IN ARRAY ARRAY['game_item_timed_state_row_guard','game_item_timed_state_row_proven',
+                                'game_reward_claim_mint_consistency_guard'] LOOP
+        body:=pg_get_functiondef((name||'()')::regprocedure);
+        insert_at:=regexp_instr(body,E'\\mBEGIN\\M',1,1,1,'i');
+        IF insert_at=0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'unexpected charge initialization guard: %',name; END IF;
+        IF name='game_reward_claim_mint_consistency_guard' THEN arm:=$arm$
+    IF NOT game_reward_claim_charges_complete(NEW.transaction_id) THEN
+        RAISE EXCEPTION 'reward claim must initialize its complete frozen charge plan' USING ERRCODE='23514';
+    END IF;
+$arm$;
+        ELSE
+            arm:=$arm$
+    IF TG_OP='INSERT' AND NEW.state_revision=1 AND NEW.remaining_ms IS NULL AND NEW.deadline_at IS NULL
+       AND game_reward_claim_charge_initialization_proven(NEW.item_instance_id,NEW.charges)
+    THEN $arm$ || CASE WHEN name='game_item_timed_state_row_guard' THEN 'RETURN NEW;' ELSE 'RETURN NULL;' END || ' END IF;';
+        END IF;
+        EXECUTE overlay(body placing arm from insert_at for 0);
+    END LOOP;
+END $migration$;
+DO $$ DECLARE signature TEXT; BEGIN
+    FOREACH signature IN ARRAY ARRAY['game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(JSONB,JSONB)',
+        'game_reward_claim_charge_initialization_proven(UUID,INTEGER)',
+        'game_reward_claim_charges_complete(UUID)'] LOOP
+        EXECUTE format('ALTER FUNCTION %s SET search_path=pg_catalog,%I,pg_temp',signature,current_schema());
+        EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION %s FROM PUBLIC',signature);
+    END LOOP;
+END $$;
+GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(JSONB,JSONB),
+    game_reward_claim_charge_initialization_proven(UUID,INTEGER),game_reward_claim_charges_complete(UUID)
+    TO oteryn_game_runtime,oteryn_game_control;

+raise SystemExit(any(results))

+-- Complete Falcon section for root-owned migration0969; not a separate migration.
+CREATE TABLE game_character_falcon_chest_cycles (
+ character_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES game_character_roots(character_id),
+ chest_key TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (chest_key IN (
+ 'oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u11_80.the_secret_library.falcon_bastion.chests_timer.coast',
+ 'oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u11_80.the_secret_library.falcon_bastion.chests_timer.island',
+ 'oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u11_80.the_secret_library.falcon_bastion.chests_timer.throne_hall',
+ 'oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u11_80.the_secret_library.falcon_bastion.chests_timer.shortcut',
+ 'oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u11_80.the_secret_library.falcon_bastion.chests_timer.lower_bastion',
+ 'oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u11_80.the_secret_library.falcon_bastion.chests_timer.underground_bastion')),
+ cycle BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK(cycle>0),
+ entropy BYTEA NOT NULL CHECK(octet_length(entropy)=32),
+ selected_index SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK(selected_index BETWEEN 0 AND 6),
+ drawn_at BIGINT NOT NULL,
+ claimed_at BIGINT NULL,
+ transaction_id UUID NULL REFERENCES game_reward_claim_mint_receipts(transaction_id),
+ CHECK((claimed_at IS NULL) = (transaction_id IS NULL)),
+ CHECK(claimed_at IS NULL OR claimed_at>=drawn_at),
+ PRIMARY KEY(character_id,chest_key,cycle)
+);
+CREATE UNIQUE INDEX game_falcon_cycle_pending ON game_character_falcon_chest_cycles(character_id,chest_key) WHERE claimed_at IS NULL;
+CREATE UNIQUE INDEX game_falcon_cycle_transaction ON game_character_falcon_chest_cycles(transaction_id) WHERE transaction_id IS NOT NULL;
+REVOKE ALL ON game_character_falcon_chest_cycles FROM PUBLIC;
+GRANT SELECT,INSERT,UPDATE ON game_character_falcon_chest_cycles TO oteryn_game_runtime;
+GRANT SELECT ON game_character_falcon_chest_cycles TO oteryn_game_control;
+CREATE FUNCTION game_falcon_now_ms() RETURNS BIGINT
+LANGUAGE SQL VOLATILE AS $$
+    SELECT floor(extract(epoch FROM statement_timestamp())*1000)::bigint
+$$;
+
+CREATE FUNCTION game_falcon_draw_index(p_entropy BYTEA) RETURNS SMALLINT
+LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE STRICT AS $$
+DECLARE seed_block BYTEA := p_entropy; ordinal INTEGER; sample_byte INTEGER;
+BEGIN
+    IF octet_length(seed_block) <> 32 THEN
+        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Falcon entropy must be 32 bytes' USING ERRCODE='23514';
+    END IF;
+    LOOP
+        FOR ordinal IN 0..31 LOOP
+            sample_byte := get_byte(seed_block,ordinal);
+            IF sample_byte < 252 THEN RETURN (sample_byte % 7)::smallint; END IF;
+        END LOOP;
+        seed_block := sha256(seed_block);
+    END LOOP;
+END;
+$$;
+
+CREATE FUNCTION game_falcon_cycle_guard() RETURNS TRIGGER
+LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
+DECLARE previous_cycle BIGINT; previous_claimed BIGINT;
+BEGIN
+    IF TG_OP='DELETE' THEN
+        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Falcon cycle receipts are retained' USING ERRCODE='23514';
+    END IF;
+    -- Serialize the six per-character cycle owners even for direct SQL calls.
+    PERFORM 1 FROM game_character_roots WHERE character_id=NEW.character_id FOR UPDATE;
+    IF NEW.selected_index <> game_falcon_draw_index(NEW.entropy) THEN
+        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Falcon draw differs from frozen entropy' USING ERRCODE='23514';
+    END IF;
+    IF TG_OP='INSERT' THEN
+        SELECT cycle,claimed_at INTO previous_cycle,previous_claimed
+          FROM game_character_falcon_chest_cycles
+         WHERE character_id=NEW.character_id AND chest_key=NEW.chest_key
+         ORDER BY cycle DESC LIMIT 1;
+        IF NEW.claimed_at IS NOT NULL OR NEW.transaction_id IS NOT NULL
+           OR NEW.cycle <> COALESCE(previous_cycle,0)+1
+           OR NEW.drawn_at NOT BETWEEN game_falcon_now_ms()-10000 AND game_falcon_now_ms()
+           OR (previous_cycle IS NOT NULL AND
+               (previous_claimed IS NULL OR
+                NEW.drawn_at::numeric < previous_claimed::numeric+86400000)) THEN
+            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Falcon cycle must follow successful 24h eligibility' USING ERRCODE='23514';
+        END IF;
+    ELSIF TG_OP='UPDATE' THEN
+        IF OLD.claimed_at IS NOT NULL OR NEW.claimed_at IS NULL OR NEW.transaction_id IS NULL
+           OR ROW(NEW.character_id,NEW.chest_key,NEW.cycle,NEW.entropy,NEW.selected_index,NEW.drawn_at)
+              IS DISTINCT FROM ROW(OLD.character_id,OLD.chest_key,OLD.cycle,OLD.entropy,OLD.selected_index,OLD.drawn_at)
+           OR NOT EXISTS (
+                SELECT 1 FROM game_reward_claim_mint_receipts r
+                 WHERE r.transaction_id=NEW.transaction_id
+                   AND r.character_id=NEW.character_id
+                   AND r.claim_family='RewardClaim'
+                   AND r.claim_production_key=NEW.chest_key||':cycle:'||NEW.cycle::text
+                   AND r.occurred_at=NEW.claimed_at
+                   AND r.created_xact_id=pg_current_xact_id()) THEN
+            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Falcon cycle may only acknowledge its atomic mint' USING ERRCODE='23514';
+        END IF;
+    END IF;
+    RETURN NEW;
+END;
+$$;
+CREATE TRIGGER game_falcon_cycle_guard BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE
+ON game_character_falcon_chest_cycles FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION game_falcon_cycle_guard();
+CREATE TRIGGER game_falcon_cycles_no_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE
+ON game_character_falcon_chest_cycles FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION game_item_immutable();
+
+CREATE FUNCTION game_falcon_receipt_guard() RETURNS TRIGGER
+LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
+BEGIN
+    -- Internal operation identities remain subject to every existing generic
+    -- mint proof. This additional deferred proof closes direct once-owner calls.
+    IF NOT starts_with(NEW.claim_production_key,'oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u11_80.the_secret_library.falcon_bastion.chests_timer.') THEN
+        RETURN NULL;
+    END IF;
+    IF NEW.claim_production_key !~ '^oteryn:reward-claim[.]quest[.]u11_80[.]the_secret_library[.]falcon_bastion[.]chests_timer[.](coast|island|throne_hall|shortcut|lower_bastion|underground_bastion):cycle:[1-9][0-9]*$' THEN
+        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Falcon public claims require the repeat owner' USING ERRCODE='23514';
+    END IF;
+    IF NOT EXISTS (
+        SELECT 1 FROM game_character_falcon_chest_cycles c
+        JOIN game_item_instances i ON i.item_instance_id=NEW.item_instance_id
+        WHERE c.character_id=NEW.character_id AND c.transaction_id=NEW.transaction_id
+          AND NEW.claim_family='RewardClaim'
+          AND NEW.claim_production_key=c.chest_key||':cycle:'||c.cycle::text
+          AND c.claimed_at=NEW.occurred_at
+          AND NEW.quantity=(ARRAY[1,1,1,1,1,1,10])[c.selected_index+1]
+          AND i.definition_family='Item'
+          AND i.definition_production_key='oteryn:item.tibia.i'||(ARRAY[9081,28821,28823,9058,6299,3052,3035])[c.selected_index+1]::text
+          AND c.selected_index=game_falcon_draw_index(c.entropy)) THEN
+        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Falcon mint requires its exact cycle and selected pool item' USING ERRCODE='23514';
+    END IF;
+    RETURN NULL;
+END;
+$$;
+CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER game_falcon_receipt_guard AFTER INSERT
+ON game_reward_claim_mint_receipts DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED
+FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION game_falcon_receipt_guard();
+
+DO $$
+DECLARE signature TEXT;
+BEGIN
+    FOREACH signature IN ARRAY ARRAY['game_falcon_now_ms()','game_falcon_draw_index(BYTEA)',
+        'game_falcon_cycle_guard()','game_falcon_receipt_guard()'] LOOP
+        EXECUTE format('ALTER FUNCTION %s SET search_path = %I, pg_temp',signature,current_schema());
+    END LOOP;
+END;
+$$;
+REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION game_falcon_now_ms(),game_falcon_draw_index(BYTEA),game_falcon_cycle_guard(),game_falcon_receipt_guard() FROM PUBLIC;
+GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION game_falcon_now_ms(),game_falcon_draw_index(BYTEA),game_falcon_cycle_guard(),game_falcon_receipt_guard() TO oteryn_game_runtime;
+GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION game_falcon_now_ms(),game_falcon_draw_index(BYTEA) TO oteryn_game_control;

+-- NPC storage-only responses share the exact physical quest receipt transaction.
+CREATE TABLE game_quest_npc_program_receipts (
+    character_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES game_character_roots(character_id),
+    game_session_id uuid NOT NULL,
+    command_id numeric(20,0) NOT NULL CHECK(command_id>=1),
+    transition_key text NOT NULL CHECK(transition_key LIKE 'oteryn:quest-npc/%'),
+    reply text[] NOT NULL,
+    next_topic integer NOT NULL CHECK(next_topic>=0),
+    PRIMARY KEY(character_id,game_session_id,command_id,transition_key),
+    FOREIGN KEY(character_id,game_session_id,command_id,transition_key)
+        REFERENCES game_character_quest_receipts(character_id,cause_id,cause_ordinal,transition_key)
+);
+REVOKE ALL ON game_quest_npc_program_receipts FROM PUBLIC;
+GRANT SELECT,INSERT ON game_quest_npc_program_receipts TO oteryn_game_runtime;
+GRANT SELECT ON game_quest_npc_program_receipts TO oteryn_game_control;
+
+CREATE FUNCTION game_quest_npc_program_response_guard() RETURNS trigger
+LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
+BEGIN
+    IF cardinality(NEW.reply)>32 OR EXISTS (
+        SELECT 1 FROM unnest(NEW.reply) line
+        WHERE line IS NULL OR octet_length(line)>2048
+    ) OR NOT EXISTS (
+        SELECT 1 FROM game_character_quest_receipts r
+        WHERE r.character_id=NEW.character_id AND r.cause_id=NEW.game_session_id
+          AND r.cause_ordinal=NEW.command_id AND r.transition_key=NEW.transition_key
+          AND r.cause_kind='command' AND r.created_xact_id=pg_current_xact_id()
+    ) THEN
+        RAISE EXCEPTION 'NPC response requires bounded lines and its same-transaction quest receipt'
+            USING ERRCODE='23514';
+    END IF;
+    RETURN NEW;
+END $$;
+DO $$ BEGIN
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER FUNCTION game_quest_npc_program_response_guard() SET search_path = %I, pg_temp',current_schema());
+END $$;
+REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION game_quest_npc_program_response_guard() FROM PUBLIC;
+CREATE TRIGGER game_quest_npc_program_response_proven
+BEFORE INSERT ON game_quest_npc_program_receipts
+FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION game_quest_npc_program_response_guard();

+import json, re
+from pathlib import Path
+
+rows=[]
+for path in sorted(Path(".questpc").glob("r3-*.result.json")):
+    result=json.loads(path.read_text())
+    log=path.with_name(path.name.replace(".result.json", ".log"))
+    text=log.read_text(errors="replace") if log.exists() else ""
+    summaries=re.findall(r"test result: .+|Ran \d+ tests? in .+|Quest unit tests failed: .+|ValueError: stale donor Source capture: .+", text)
+    rows.append({**result,"log":str(log),"summaries":summaries,"postgres_completed": "POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED" in text})
+Path(".questpc/r3-validation.json").write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2)+"\n")
+lines=["| Command | Exit | Seconds including queue/build | Evidence |", "| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |"]
+for row in rows:
+    evidence="; ".join(row["summaries"]) or "See log"
+    lines.append(f"| `{row['command']}` | {row['exit']} | {row['duration_seconds']} | {evidence} (`{row['log']}`) |")
+Path(".questpc/r3-validation.md").write_text("\n".join(lines)+"\n")

+#[test]
+fn quest_catalogue_chests_a_m_unqualified_source_placements_use_replay_and_refusal() -> TestResult {
+    let coverage: Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
+        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
+        "/../../../docs/repository/QUEST_CALLBACK_COVERAGE.json"
+    )))?;
+    // Lane ownership comes from the coverage quest title, including its leading
+    // article. Claim slugs can describe an unrelated item or donor storage key.
+    let claims: BTreeSet<_> = coverage["quests"]
+        .as_array()
+        .ok_or("coverage quests")?
+        .iter()
+        .filter(|quest| {
+            quest["quest"]
+                .as_str()
+                .and_then(|title| title.rsplit('/').next())
+                .and_then(|title| title.bytes().next())
+                .is_some_and(|initial| (b'a'..=b'm').contains(&initial))
+        })
+        .flat_map(|quest| quest["chests"].as_array().into_iter().flatten())
+        .filter(|chest| chest["status"] == "supported_unqualified")
+        .map(|chest| string(&chest["claim"]))
+        .collect::<TestResult<_>>()?;
+    assert!(!claims.is_empty(), "A..M qualification requires source claims");
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases()?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| claims.contains(&case.claim))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected.iter().map(|case| &case.claim).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>(),
+        claims.iter().collect(),
+        "every A..M unqualified claim needs every source placement exercised"
+    );
+    execute(selected)
+}
+

+"""Verify exact run-8 A..M PG markers and individual test success in a shared log."""
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import re
+from pathlib import Path
+
+PREFIX = 'gameplay_transport::monk_save::postgres_tests::quest_catalogue_chests::'
+BATCH_TEST = PREFIX + 'quest_catalogue_chests_a_m_unqualified_source_placements_use_replay_and_refusal'
+KEY_TEST = PREFIX + 'quest_catalogue_chests_mintwallin_prison_key3620_use_replay_and_refusal'
+SOURCE_PATH = 'crates/gameplay/dispatch/src/gameplay_transport/postgres_tests/quest_catalogue_chests.rs'
+SNAPSHOTS = [
+    (Path('.questpc/r8-catalogue-claims.json'), BATCH_TEST),
+    (Path('.questpc/r8-catalogue-key3620-claims.json'), KEY_TEST),
+]
+
+parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+parser.add_argument('log', type=Path)
+parser.add_argument('--require-complete', action='store_true')
+args = parser.parse_args()
+source = {}
+source_hashes = {}
+for path, test in SNAPSHOTS:
+    source_hashes[str(path)] = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    for key, row in json.loads(path.read_text()).items():
+        if key in source:
+            raise ValueError(f'duplicate source claim: {key}')
+        source[key] = {**row, 'fully_qualified_name': test}
+if len(source) != 133:
+    raise ValueError(f'expected frozen132 plus Mintwallin key3620, got {len(source)}')
+
+raw = args.log.read_bytes()
+text = raw.decode()
+pattern = re.compile(
+    r'QUEST_CATALOGUE_CHEST POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED (\S+) '
+    r'\((\d+), (\d+), (-?\d+)\) native_source=(true|false) duration=(\S+)'
+)
+matches = list(pattern.finditer(text))
+if text.count('QUEST_CATALOGUE_CHEST POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED ') != len(matches):
+    raise ValueError('malformed catalogue completion marker')
+completed = {}
+for match in matches:
+    claim, x, y, floor, native, duration = match.groups()
+    if claim not in source:
+        raise ValueError(f'out-of-scope completion marker: {claim}')
+    parsed_duration = re.fullmatch(r'([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)(s|ms|µs|ns)', duration)
+    if native != 'true' or parsed_duration is None:
+        raise ValueError(f'invalid completion: {match.group()}')
+    magnitude, unit = parsed_duration.groups()
+    seconds = float(magnitude) * {'s': 1, 'ms': 1e-3, 'µs': 1e-6, 'ns': 1e-9}[unit]
+    if seconds <= 0:
+        raise ValueError(f'nonpositive completion duration: {match.group()}')
+    position = (int(x), int(y), -int(floor))
+    expected = {tuple(p['position'][axis] for axis in ('x', 'y', 'z'))
+                for p in source[claim]['placements']}
+    if position not in expected:
+        raise ValueError(f'unknown source placement: {claim} {position}')
+    key = (claim, position)
+    if key in completed:
+        raise ValueError(f'duplicate source completion: {key}')
+    completed[key] = {
+        'position': dict(zip(('x', 'y', 'z'), position)),
+        'native_source': True,
+        'duration': duration,
+        'duration_seconds': seconds,
+        'marker': match.group(),
+    }
+
+individual_results = {}
+for name in (BATCH_TEST, KEY_TEST):
+    matches = re.findall(r'^test ' + re.escape(name) + r' \.\.\. (ok|FAILED)$', text, re.MULTILINE)
+    if len(matches) > 1:
+        raise ValueError(f'ambiguous individual test result: {name} {matches}')
+    individual_results[name] = matches[0] if matches else 'pending'
+
+claims = {}
+ledger_candidates = {}
+for key, row in source.items():
+    positions = [tuple(p['position'][axis] for axis in ('x', 'y', 'z')) for p in row['placements']]
+    observed = [completed[(key, p)] for p in positions if (key, p) in completed]
+    all_placements = len(observed) == len(positions)
+    test = row['fully_qualified_name']
+    qualified = all_placements and individual_results[test] == 'ok'
+    claims[key] = {
+        'quest': row['quest'],
+        'fully_qualified_name': test,
+        'expected_placements': len(positions),
+        'completed_placements': observed,
+        'all_placements_completed': all_placements,
+        'individual_test_result': individual_results[test],
+        'qualified': qualified,
+    }
+    if qualified:
+        ledger_candidates[key] = {
+            'disposition': 'test',
+            'evidence': {
+                'kind': 'test',
+                'path': SOURCE_PATH,
+                'fully_qualified_name': test,
+                'crate_or_target': 'oteryn-gameplay-dispatch --all-features --lib',
+                'asserted_scenario': (
+                    'Exact authored World source selectors; literal production USE dispatch; '
+                    'native reward mint and complete custody/attributes; once-only receipt; '
+                    'retained duplicate replay; cold-owner replay with identical item identity; '
+                    'second new-command refusal; stale-generation refusal without reservation. '
+                    'Reward proof does not qualify quest access callbacks.'
+                ),
+                'observed_result': (
+                    f'PASS individual test; {len(observed)}/{len(positions)} exact native-source '
+                    f'POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED markers with positive elapsed durations in {args.log}'
+                ),
+                'postgres_completion_markers': observed,
+            },
+        }
+
+result = {
+    'log': str(args.log),
+    'log_sha256': hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
+    'source_snapshots_sha256': source_hashes,
+    'expected_claims': len(source),
+    'expected_placements': sum(len(row['placements']) for row in source.values()),
+    'completed_markers': len(completed),
+    'qualified_claims': len(ledger_candidates),
+    'individual_test_results': individual_results,
+    'aggregate_test_result_lines': re.findall(r'^test result:.*$', text, re.MULTILINE),
+    'claims': claims,
+}
+complete = len(ledger_candidates) == len(source)
+candidate_path = Path('.questpc/r8-catalogue-ledger-candidate.json')
+if not complete:
+    candidate_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
+result['complete'] = complete
+Path('.questpc/r8-catalogue-root.evidence.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + '\n')
+if complete:
+    candidate_path.write_text(
+        json.dumps({'log': str(args.log), 'log_sha256': result['log_sha256'],
+                    'claims': ledger_candidates}, indent=2) + '\n'
+    )
+print(json.dumps({key: value for key, value in result.items() if key != 'claims'}, indent=2))
+if args.require_complete and not complete:
+    raise SystemExit(1)

+"""Read-only finite native carrying-policy census for every run-8 reward item."""
+import collections
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+
+keys = set(json.loads(Path('.questpc/r8-catalogue-claims.json').read_text()))
+keys.update(json.loads(Path('.questpc/r8-catalogue-key3620-claims.json').read_text()))
+native_rows = json.loads(Path('content/items/definitions/spell-native-profiles.json').read_text())['records']
+native = collections.defaultdict(list)
+for row in native_rows:
+    d=row['production_definition']
+    native[(d['family'],d['production_key'],d['revision_ref'])].append(row)
+
+def known(value):
+    return value.get('value') if isinstance(value,dict) and value.get('state')=='KNOWN' else None
+
+uses=collections.defaultdict(list)
+claims={}
+issues=[]
+for path in sorted(Path('content/interactions/reward_claims').glob('reward-claim*.json')):
+    for row in json.loads(path.read_text())['records']:
+        d=row['definition'];key=d['identity']['key']
+        if key not in keys:continue
+        claims[key]=d
+        for i,p in enumerate(d['placements']):
+            n=d.get('native_contents',{}).get('placements',[])
+            n=n[i] if i<len(n) else None
+            items=[(n['root'],1,0,n.get('capacity')),*[(c['item'],c['count'],c.get('key_number',0),None) for c in n['contents']]] if n else [(c['item'],c['count'],int(p['reward']['key_binding'].rsplit('/',1)[-1]) if p['reward'].get('key_binding') and not p['reward'].get('container') else 0,None) for c in p['reward']['items']]
+            for j,(item,count,number,capacity) in enumerate(items):
+                use={'claim':key,'placement':i,'role':'root' if j==0 else 'child','count':count,'key_number':number,'recipe_capacity':capacity,'reward_claim_path':str(path)}
+                uses[(item['family'],item['key'],item['revision'])].append(use)
+
+for definition,refs in sorted(uses.items()):
+    rows=native.get(definition,[]);reasons=[]
+    facts={}
+    if len(rows)!=1:reasons.append('NATIVE_PROFILE_MISSING' if not rows else 'NATIVE_PROFILE_DUPLICATE')
+    else:
+        row=rows[0];a=row['admission'];s=row['semantics'];physical=known(s.get('physical',{})) or {};stack=known(s.get('stack',{})) or {};container=known(s.get('container',{})) or {}
+        facts={'materializable':a['materializable'],'destinations':a['legal_destinations'],'stack_class':a['stack_class'],'weight':known(physical.get('weight',{})),'movable':known(physical.get('movable',{})),'pickupable':known(physical.get('pickupable',{})),'stack_max':known(stack.get('stack_max',{})),'container_capacity':known(container.get('capacity',{}))}
+        if not a['materializable'] or 'CharacterInventory' not in a['legal_destinations']:reasons.append('CHARACTER_INVENTORY_NOT_ADMITTED')
+        if facts['weight'] is None:reasons.append('PORTABLE_WEIGHT_UNKNOWN')
+        if facts['movable'] is not True or facts['pickupable'] is not True:reasons.append('PHYSICAL_PORTABILITY_NOT_PROVEN')
+        for use in refs:
+            if not 0<use['count']<=100:reasons.append('REWARD_QUANTITY_OUTSIDE_OWNER_RANGE')
+            if a['stack_class']=='NonStackable' and use['count']!=1:reasons.append('NONSTACKABLE_REWARD_QUANTITY')
+            if a['stack_class']=='StackCapable' and (not facts['stack_max'] or use['count']>facts['stack_max']):reasons.append('STACK_MAX_NOT_PROVEN')
+            if use['key_number'] and not 1<=use['key_number']<=65535:reasons.append('KEY_NUMBER_OUT_OF_RANGE')
+            if use['recipe_capacity'] is not None and facts['container_capacity']!=use['recipe_capacity']:reasons.append('ROOT_CONTAINER_CAPACITY_MISMATCH')
+    if reasons:issues.append({'definition':{'family':definition[0],'production_key':definition[1],'revision_ref':definition[2]},'reasons':sorted(set(reasons)),'facts':facts,'uses':refs})
+result={'selected_claims':len(keys),'definitions_found':len(claims),'distinct_items':len(uses),'native_profile_rows':len(native_rows),'issues':issues,'items':[{'definition':{'family':d[0],'production_key':d[1],'revision_ref':d[2]},'uses':r} for d,r in sorted(uses.items())]}
+Path('.questpc/r8-catalogue-native-audit.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')
+print('claims',len(keys),'definitions',len(claims),'items',len(uses),'issue definitions',len(issues))
+for row in issues:print(row['definition']['production_key'],','.join(row['reasons']),'claims',','.join(sorted({u['claim'] for u in row['uses']})))

+  if key in bindings:row['source_bindings']=bindings[key]
+  extra={k:v for k,v in authoring.get(key,{}).items() if k not in {'item','taxonomy'}}
+  if extra:row['authoring']=extra
+  rows.append(row)
+ end=start+len(rows)-1

+print(f'Generated {len(items)} canonical Items using existing source materializers')

+"""Retain real cohort results and checkpoint each five qualified reward sets."""
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import re
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+
+manifest_path = Path(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else '.questpc/restart-phase-a-cohorts-all.json')
+manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())
+source = Path('crates/gameplay/dispatch/src/gameplay_transport/postgres_tests/quest_catalogue_chests.rs')
+source_hash = hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+command = ['/home/mole/oteryn-cache/codex-bin/cargo', 'test', '--locked', '--offline',
+           '-p', 'oteryn-gameplay-dispatch', '--all-features', '--lib',
+           '--', *manifest, '--test-threads=2', '--nocapture']
+marker_pattern = re.compile(r'QUEST_CATALOGUE_CHEST POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED (\S+) '
+                            r'\((\d+), (\d+), (-?\d+)\) native_source=(true|false) duration=(\S+)')
+result_pattern = re.compile(r'^test \S+::(quest_catalogue_chests_phase_a_\d+_use_replay_and_refusal) \.\.\. (ok|FAILED)$')
+observed = {}
+results = {}
+qualified = {}
+start = time.monotonic()
+# Preserve every attempt: a rerun must not replace a log referenced by an
+# earlier QUALIFIED checkpoint, even if the rerun fails before its first case.
+attempt = 1
+label = 'restart-phase-a-pg'
+while Path(f'.questpc/{label}.log').exists() or Path(f'.questpc/{label}.result.json').exists():
+    attempt += 1
+    label = f'restart-phase-a-pg-{attempt}'
+log_path = Path(f'.questpc/{label}.log')
+result_path = Path(f'.questpc/{label}.result.json')
+qualified_path = Path(f'.questpc/{label}.qualified.json')
+print('Retaining attempt at ' + str(log_path), flush=True)
+environment = dict(os.environ, CARGO_BUILD_JOBS='4')
+with log_path.open('w') as log:
+    process = subprocess.Popen(command, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+                               text=True, env=environment)
+    for line in process.stdout:
+        log.write(line)
+        log.flush()
+        match = marker_pattern.search(line)
+        if match:
+            claim, x, y, floor, native, duration = match.groups()
+            elapsed = re.fullmatch(r'([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)(s|ms|µs|ns)', duration)
+            if native != 'true' or not elapsed or float(elapsed[1]) <= 0:
+                raise ValueError('invalid placement completion: ' + match[0])
+            position = (int(x), int(y), -int(floor))
+            key = (claim, position)
+            if key in observed:
+                raise ValueError('duplicate placement completion: ' + str(key))
+            observed[key] = {'marker': match[0], 'duration': duration, 'position': position}
+        match = result_pattern.match(line.rstrip())
+        if not match:
+            continue
+        name, result = match.groups()
+        results[name] = result
+        if result != 'ok':
+            continue
+        cohort = manifest[name]
+        evidence = {}
+        for claim, record in cohort['claims'].items():
+            placements = [tuple(p['position'][axis] for axis in ('x', 'y', 'z'))
+                          for p in record['placements']]
+            evidence[claim] = [observed[(claim, p)] for p in placements]
+        if hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != source_hash:
+            raise ValueError('test source changed during qualification')
+        qualified[name] = {'quests': cohort['quests'], 'claims': evidence}
+        qualified_path.write_text(json.dumps({
+            'log': str(log_path),
+            'test_source_sha256': source_hash, 'manifest_sha256': hashlib.sha256(manifest_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+            'command': command, 'qualified': qualified}, indent=2) + '\n')
+        titles = [quest.rsplit('/', 1)[-1] for quest in cohort['quests']]
+        with Path('.questpc/RESUME.md').open('a') as resume:
+            resume.write('QUALIFIED: ' + ', '.join(titles) + ' | test: ' + name +
+                         ' | reward-source USE/replay/refusal only; all placements and durations: ' +
+                         str(qualified_path) + '\n')
+        print('QUALIFIED ' + name + ': ' + str(len(titles)) + ' quests, ' + str(len(evidence)) + ' claims', flush=True)
+    code = process.wait()
+if code == 0 and set(results) != set(manifest):
+    code = 1
+    print('Missing individual test results: no aggregate success claim', flush=True)
+result_path.write_text(json.dumps({
+    'log': str(log_path), 'log_sha256': hashlib.sha256(log_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+    'command': command, 'exit': code, 'duration_seconds': round(time.monotonic() - start, 3),
+    'test_results': results, 'qualified_cohorts': len(qualified),
+    'qualified_quests': sum(len(c['quests']) for c in qualified.values())}, indent=2) + '\n')
+print('exit=' + str(code) + ' elapsed=' + str(round(time.monotonic()-start, 3)), flush=True)
+raise SystemExit(code)

+//! Catalogue-wide reward qualification through the production USE and durable owners.
+//! This proves the reward at its source selector, not the quest's access path.
+use super::TestResult;
+use serde_json::Value;
+use std::collections::BTreeSet;
+
+pub(super) type CatalogueActivation = (
+    crate::content::ContentActivationController,
+    crate::content::NativeEntryContentPin,
+);
+
+pub(super) struct CatalogueChest {
+    pub claim: String,
+    pub position: (u16, u16, i8),
+    pub unique: u16,
+    pub servable: bool,
+    pub appearance: u16,
+    pub root: String,
+    pub quantity: u32,
+    pub contents: Vec<(String, i64, i32)>,
+    pub texts: Vec<(String, String)>,
+    pub key_number: Option<i32>,
+    pub achievement: bool,
+    pub charges: Vec<(String, i32)>,
+    pub fluids: Vec<(String, i32)>,
+}
+
+fn string(value: &Value) -> TestResult<String> {
+    Ok(value.as_str().ok_or("catalogue string")?.into())
+}
+
+fn cases() -> TestResult<Vec<CatalogueChest>> {
+    cases_including(|_| false)
+}
+
+fn cases_including(include: impl Fn(&str) -> bool) -> TestResult<Vec<CatalogueChest>> {
+    let coverage: Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
+        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
+        "/../../../docs/repository/QUEST_CALLBACK_COVERAGE.json"
+    )))?;
+    let mut supported: BTreeSet<_> = coverage["quests"]
+        .as_array()
+        .ok_or("coverage quests")?
+        .iter()
+        .flat_map(|quest| quest["chests"].as_array().into_iter().flatten())
+        .filter(|chest| chest["status"] != "not_playable")
+        .map(|chest| string(&chest["claim"]))
+        .collect::<TestResult<_>>()?;
+    // Random repeat sources have their own complete production USE scenarios;
+    // the generic once-only recipe harness cannot represent their cycle owner.
+    for chest in oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::falcon::CHESTS {
+        assert!(
+            supported.remove(&chest.key()),
+            "Falcon source needs cycle coverage"
+        );
+    }
+    let mut found = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut served = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut cases = Vec::new();
+    for shard in crate::content::world_reward_claims::REWARD_CLAIM_SHARDS {
+        let shard: Value = serde_json::from_str(shard)?;
+        for row in shard["records"].as_array().ok_or("claim records")? {
+            let definition = &row["definition"];
+            let claim = string(&definition["identity"]["key"])?;
+            // A new source slice must reach the production binder before its
+            // coverage report can claim qualification. The broad sweep still
+            // requires every previously supported claim exactly as before.
+            if include(&claim) {
+                supported.insert(claim.clone());
+            }
+            if !supported.contains(&claim) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            assert!(found.insert(claim.clone()), "duplicate claim {claim}");
+            for (index, placement) in definition["placements"]
+                .as_array()
+                .ok_or("claim placements")?
+                .iter()
+                .enumerate()
+            {
+                let binding = &placement["source_binding"];
+                let position = &binding["project_position"];
+                let ids = binding["legacy_unique_ids"]
+                    .as_array()
+                    .ok_or("source unique ids")?;
+                let crystal = ids
+                    .iter()
+                    .find(|source| source["server"] == "crystalserver");
+                let unique = crystal.or_else(|| ids.first()).ok_or("source selector")?["unique_id"]
+                    .as_u64()
+                    .ok_or("source unique id")?;
+                if crystal.is_some() {
+                    served.insert(claim.clone());
+                }
+                let reward = &placement["reward"];
+                let native = definition["native_contents"]["placements"].get(index);
+                let (root, quantity, contents) = if let Some(native) = native {
+                    let children = native["contents"]
+                        .as_array()
+                        .ok_or("native contents")?
+                        .iter()
+                        .map(|child| {
+                            Ok((
+                                string(&child["item"]["key"])?,
+                                child["count"].as_i64().ok_or("child count")?,
+                                i32::try_from(child["key_number"].as_i64().unwrap_or(0))?,
+                            ))
+                        })
+                        .collect::<TestResult<Vec<_>>>()?;
+                    (string(&native["root"]["key"])?, 1, children)
+                } else {
+                    let items = reward["items"].as_array().ok_or("leaf items")?;
+                    assert_eq!(items.len(), 1, "unsupported leaf shape {claim}");
+                    (
+                        string(&items[0]["item"]["key"])?,
+                        u32::try_from(items[0]["count"].as_u64().ok_or("leaf count")?)?,
+                        Vec::new(),
+                    )
+                };
+                let mut texts = Vec::new();
+                if let Some(native) = native {
+                    if let Some(text) = native["root_text"].as_str() {
+                        texts.push((root.clone(), text.into()));
+                    }
+                    for child in native["contents"].as_array().ok_or("native contents")? {
+                        if let Some(text) = child["text"].as_str() {
+                            texts.push((string(&child["item"]["key"])?, text.into()));
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                let key_number = reward["key_binding"]
+                    .as_str()
+                    .filter(|_| reward.get("container").is_none())
+                    .map(|binding| -> TestResult<i32> {
+                        Ok(binding
+                            .rsplit('/')
+                            .next()
+                            .ok_or("key binding")?
+                            .parse::<i32>()?)
+                    })
+                    .transpose()?;
+                cases.push(CatalogueChest {
+                    claim: claim.clone(),
+                    position: (
+                        u16::try_from(position["x"].as_u64().ok_or("source x")?)?,
+                        u16::try_from(position["y"].as_u64().ok_or("source y")?)?,
+                        -i8::try_from(position["z"].as_u64().ok_or("source z")?)?,
+                    ),
+                    unique: u16::try_from(unique)?,
+                    servable: crystal.is_some(),
+                    appearance: u16::try_from(
+                        placement["appearance_tibia_id"]
+                            .as_u64()
+                            .ok_or("source appearance")?,
+                    )?,
+                    root,
+                    quantity,
+                    contents,
+                    texts,
+                    key_number,
+                    achievement: native.is_some_and(|native| !native["achievement"].is_null()),
+                    charges: expected_charges(&claim),
+                    fluids: expected_fluids(&claim),
+                });
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    assert_eq!(found, supported, "every supported claim needs a scenario");
+    assert_eq!(
+        served, supported,
+        "every supported claim needs a native-source placement"
+    );
+    Ok(cases)
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn quest_catalogue_chests_every_supported_source_placement_use_replay_and_refusal() -> TestResult {
+    execute(cases()?)?;
+    super::falcon_chests::falcon_chests_postgres_real_use_frozen_draw_refusal_restart_and_fences()
+}
+

+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_0.shardsof_ancient_winters.helheim",

+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_1.koshei_the_deathless.koshei_the_deathless_gold",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_1.koshei_the_deathless.koshei_the_deathless_legs",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_1.koshei_the_deathless.koshei_the_deathless_platinum",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_5.darashia_dragon",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_5.lone_medusa",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_54.children_of_the_revolution.chest_tome_of_knowledge1",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_54.children_of_the_revolution.chest_tome_of_knowledge2",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_54.children_of_the_revolution.flask_of_extra_greasy_oil",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_6.an_interest_in_botany.botany_almanach",
+    ]
+    .into_iter()
+    .collect();
+    let coverage: Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
+        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
+        "/../../../docs/repository/QUEST_CALLBACK_COVERAGE.json"
+    )))?;
+    // Lane ownership comes from the coverage quest title, including its leading
+    // article. Claim slugs can describe an unrelated item or donor storage key.
+    let owned_claims: BTreeSet<_> = coverage["quests"]
+        .as_array()
+        .ok_or("coverage quests")?
+        .iter()
+        .filter(|quest| {
+            quest["quest"]
+                .as_str()
+                .and_then(|title| title.rsplit('/').next())
+                .and_then(|title| title.bytes().next())
+                .is_some_and(|initial| (b'a'..=b'm').contains(&initial))
+        })
+        .flat_map(|quest| quest["chests"].as_array().into_iter().flatten())
+        .filter(|chest| chest["status"] != "not_playable")
+        .map(|chest| string(&chest["claim"]))
+        .collect::<TestResult<_>>()?;
+    assert!(
+        claims.iter().all(|claim| owned_claims.contains(*claim)),
+        "every frozen A..M claim must retain supported title ownership"
+    );
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases()?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| claims.contains(case.claim.as_str()))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected
+            .iter()
+            .map(|case| case.claim.as_str())
+            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>(),
+        claims,
+        "every A..M run-8 claim needs every source placement exercised"
+    );
+    execute(selected)
+}
+
+// Small cohorts use the same production dispatch scenario as the catalogue
+// sweep. Keep every placement and every already-qualified sibling so an early
+// checkpoint describes a whole reward set, rather than a partial failed sweep.
+// Access puzzles, NPC exchanges and outfits remain separate qualification work.
+fn phase_a_cohort(quests: &[&str]) -> TestResult {
+    let coverage: Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
+        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
+        "/../../../docs/repository/QUEST_CALLBACK_COVERAGE.json"
+    )))?;
+    let mut found = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut claims = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut placements = 0;
+    for quest in coverage["quests"].as_array().ok_or("coverage quests")? {
+        let title = quest["quest"]
+            .as_str()
+            .ok_or("quest title")?
+            .rsplit('/')
+            .next()
+            .ok_or("quest slug")?;
+        if !quests.contains(&title) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        assert!(found.insert(title), "duplicate quest {title}");
+        for chest in quest["chests"].as_array().ok_or("quest chests")? {
+            assert_ne!(chest["status"], "not_playable", "held reward in {title}");
+            assert!(
+                claims.insert(string(&chest["claim"])?),
+                "duplicate cohort claim"
+            );
+            placements += chest["placements"]
+                .as_array()
+                .ok_or("coverage placements")?
+                .len();
+        }
+    }
+    assert_eq!(
+        found,
+        quests.iter().copied().collect(),
+        "every cohort quest must be authored"
+    );
+    assert!(!claims.is_empty(), "a cohort must exercise actual rewards");
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases()?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| claims.contains(&case.claim))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected.len(),
+        placements,
+        "every source placement must execute"
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected
+            .iter()
+            .map(|case| &case.claim)
+            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>(),
+        claims.iter().collect(),
+        "every reward must execute"
+    );
+    execute(selected)
+}
+

+    phase_a_cohort(&[
+        "adorned_uh_rune_quest",
+        "alawar_s_vault_quest",
+        "barbarian_axe_quest",
+        "battle_axe_quest",
+        "bear_room_quest",
+    ])
+}

+#[test]
+fn quest_catalogue_chests_mintwallin_prison_key3620_use_replay_and_refusal() -> TestResult {
+    // TibiaWiki Key 3620 identifies the Mintwallin barracks drawer; the donor's
+    // inherited Black Knight section label is not this source's quest identity.
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases()?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| case.claim == "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.key.id3620")
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(selected.len(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(selected[0].position, (32411, 32155, -15));
+    assert_eq!(selected[0].unique, 5009);
+    assert_eq!(selected[0].appearance, 2434);
+    assert_eq!(selected[0].root, "oteryn:item.tibia.i2969");
+    assert_eq!(selected[0].key_number, Some(3620));
+    execute(selected)
+}

+#[test]
+fn quest_catalogue_chests_full_charge_rewards_use_replay_and_refusal() -> TestResult {
+    let claims: BTreeSet<_> = [
+        "desert2",
+        "draconia1",
+        "edrongoblin1",
+        "fireaxe1",
+        "ghoulroom1",
+        "lifering",
+        "powerring2",
+        "silveramulet",
+        "stealhring1",
+        "timering3",
+        "triangletower",
+        "trollcave1",
+        "weddingring1",
+    ]
+    .into_iter()
+    .map(|suffix| format!("oteryn:reward-claim.kv.{suffix}"))
+    .collect();
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases()?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| claims.contains(&case.claim))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected
+            .iter()
+            .map(|case| &case.claim)
+            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>(),
+        claims.iter().collect(),
+        "every newly admitted full-charge claim needs a real USE scenario"
+    );
+    execute(selected)
+}
+
+fn expected_charges(claim: &str) -> Vec<(String, i32)> {
+    let ids: &[u16] = match claim.strip_prefix("oteryn:reward-claim.") {
+        Some("kv.deeperfibula4") => &[3082],
+        Some("quest.u7_2.the_queen_of_the_banshees.reward.stone_skin_amulet") => &[3081],
+        Some("quest.u15_24.targuna.silver_amulet_chest") => &[3054],
+        Some("quest.pre_u6_0.ornamented_shield.rewards.ornamented_shield") => &[3048, 3085],
+        Some("kv.ornamentedshield") => &[3083],
+        _ => &[],
+    };
+    ids.iter()
+        .map(|id| (format!("oteryn:item.tibia.i{id}"), 1))
+        .collect()
+}
+
+fn expected_fluids(claim: &str) -> Vec<(String, i32)> {
+    match claim.strip_prefix("oteryn:reward-claim.") {
+        Some("kv.goblintemple2") => vec![("oteryn:item.tibia.i2874".into(), 9)],
+        Some("quest.u7_9.dreamers_challenge.chests_wine") => {
+            vec![("oteryn:item.tibia.i2874".into(), 2)]
+        }
+        Some("kv.present_box") => vec![
+            ("oteryn:item.tibia.i2881".into(), 1),
+            ("oteryn:item.tibia.i2882".into(), 1),
+        ],
+        _ => Vec::new(),
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn quest_catalogue_chests_exact_partial_charges_use_replay_and_refusal() -> TestResult {
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases_including(|claim| !expected_charges(claim).is_empty())?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| !case.charges.is_empty())
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected.len(),
+        5,
+        "all five exact partial-charge source claims need USE proof"
+    );
+    execute(selected)
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn quest_catalogue_chests_exact_fluid_subtypes_use_replay_and_refusal() -> TestResult {
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases_including(|claim| !expected_fluids(claim).is_empty())?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| !case.fluids.is_empty())
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected.len(),
+        6,
+        "milk, four wine bags and water present need USE proof"
+    );
+    execute(selected)
+}

+pub(super) async fn assert_charges(
+    pool: &sqlx::PgPool,
+    root: [u8; 16],
+    chest: &CatalogueChest,
+) -> TestResult {
+    let actual: Vec<(String, i32, i64, bool, bool, bool)> = sqlx::query_as(
+        "SELECT i.definition_production_key,t.charges,t.state_revision::bigint, \
+            t.remaining_ms IS NULL,t.deadline_at IS NULL,t.xmin=r.xmin \
+         FROM game_item_timed_states t JOIN game_item_instances i USING(item_instance_id) \
+         JOIN game_reward_claim_mint_receipts r ON r.item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid \
+         WHERE i.item_instance_id=r.item_instance_id OR i.item_instance_id IN \
+            (SELECT item_instance_id FROM game_item_nested_entries WHERE parent_item_instance_id=r.item_instance_id) \
+         ORDER BY i.definition_production_key",
+    )
+    .bind(root.as_slice())
+    .fetch_all(pool)
+    .await?;
+    let expected: Vec<_> = chest
+        .charges
+        .iter()
+        .map(|(key, count)| (key.clone(), *count, 1, true, true, true))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        actual, expected,
+        "exact source charge state, minted atomically and stable on replay"
+    );
+    let (reserved, committed): (Value, Value) = sqlx::query_as(
+        "SELECT v.charges_plan,r.charges_plan FROM game_reward_claim_mint_receipts r \
+         JOIN game_reward_claim_mint_reservations v USING(game_session_id,command_id) \
+         WHERE r.item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+    )
+    .bind(root.as_slice())
+    .fetch_one(pool)
+    .await?;
+    let mut expected_plan = Vec::new();
+    for (key, charges) in &chest.charges {
+        let ordinal = if *key == chest.root {
+            0
+        } else {
+            chest
+                .contents
+                .iter()
+                .position(|(child, _, _)| child == key)
+                .ok_or("charged child absent")?
+                + 1
+        };
+        expected_plan.push(serde_json::json!({"ordinal": ordinal, "charges_remaining": charges}));
+    }
+    expected_plan.sort_by_key(|value| value["ordinal"].as_u64());
+    assert_eq!(committed, Value::Array(expected_plan));
+    assert_eq!(
+        reserved, committed,
+        "reservation binds the exact complete charge plan"
+    );
+    if !chest.charges.is_empty() {
+        let shape: (bool, bool, bool, bool, bool) = sqlx::query_as(
+            "SELECT game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(r.charges_plan,r.contents_plan), \
+                game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(r.charges_plan#-'{0,charges_remaining}',r.contents_plan), \
+                game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(jsonb_set(r.charges_plan,'{0,charges_remaining}','2'),r.contents_plan), \
+                game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(jsonb_set(r.charges_plan,'{0,ordinal}','-1'),r.contents_plan), \
+                game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(r.charges_plan||r.charges_plan,r.contents_plan) \
+             FROM game_reward_claim_mint_receipts r WHERE r.item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+        )
+        .bind(root.as_slice())
+        .fetch_one(pool)
+        .await?;
+        assert_eq!(shape, (true, false, false, false, false));
+        for mutation in [
+            "UPDATE game_reward_claim_mint_reservations SET charges_plan='[]'::jsonb WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+            "UPDATE game_item_timed_states SET charges=2,state_revision=state_revision+1 WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid OR item_instance_id IN (SELECT item_instance_id FROM game_item_nested_entries WHERE parent_item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid)",
+        ] {
+            let changed = sqlx::query(mutation)
+                .bind(root.as_slice())
+                .execute(pool)
+                .await;
+            assert!(
+                matches!(changed, Err(sqlx::Error::Database(ref error)) if error.code().as_deref() == Some("23514")),
+                "charge initialization never permits later plan substitution or unfenced inflation"
+            );
+        }
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+/// The binder admits a claim only when every source placement is present.
+/// Preserve all sibling selectors even while exercising one clicked chest.
+pub(super) fn catalogue_source_world(
+    chest: &CatalogueChest,
+) -> TestResult<crate::map::boot::BundleWorld> {
+    use oteryn_world_bundle::{bundle, sector};
+    use std::collections::BTreeMap;
+    let mut placements = Vec::new();
+    for shard in crate::content::world_reward_claims::REWARD_CLAIM_SHARDS {
+        let shard: Value = serde_json::from_str(shard)?;
+        for row in shard["records"].as_array().ok_or("claim records")? {
+            let definition = &row["definition"];
+            if definition["identity"]["key"] != chest.claim {
+                continue;
+            }
+            for source in definition["placements"]
+                .as_array()
+                .ok_or("claim placements")?
+            {
+                let binding = &source["source_binding"];
+                let unique = binding["legacy_unique_ids"]
+                    .as_array()
+                    .ok_or("source ids")?
+                    .iter()
+                    .find(|id| id["server"] == "crystalserver")
+                    .ok_or("native source")?;
+                let position = &binding["project_position"];
+                placements.push((
+                    u16::try_from(position["x"].as_u64().ok_or("source x")?)?,
+                    u16::try_from(position["y"].as_u64().ok_or("source y")?)?,
+                    -i8::try_from(position["z"].as_u64().ok_or("source z")?)?,
+                    u16::try_from(unique["unique_id"].as_u64().ok_or("source uid")?)?,
+                    u16::try_from(
+                        source["appearance_tibia_id"]
+                            .as_u64()
+                            .ok_or("source appearance")?,
+                    )?,
+                ));
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    assert!(
+        !placements.is_empty(),
+        "every catalogue claim has placed sources"
+    );
+    let (bytes, mut pins) = crate::map::boot::tests::bundle();
+    let mut data = bundle::read(&bytes)?;
+    data.manifest.palette.truncate(1);
+    let mut appearances = BTreeMap::new();
+    let mut tiles = BTreeMap::<(i8, u16, u16), sector::Tile>::new();
+    for (x, y, floor, unique, appearance) in placements {
+        let palette = if let Some(index) = appearances.get(&appearance) {
+            *index
+        } else {
+            let index = u32::try_from(data.manifest.palette.len())?;
+            data.manifest.palette.push(bundle::PaletteEntry {
+                key: format!("oteryn:terrain.tibia.i{appearance}"),
+                family: bundle::Family::Terrain,
+                id: index,
+                terrain: Some(bundle::Terrain {
+                    kind: bundle::TerrainKind::Common,
+                    walkable: None,
+                    ground_speed: None,
+                }),
+            });
+            appearances.insert(appearance, index);
+            index
+        };
+        for tile_x in x.saturating_sub(2)..=x {
+            tiles
+                .entry((floor, y, tile_x))
+                .or_insert_with(|| sector::Tile {
+                    x: tile_x,
+                    y,
+                    flags: 0,
+                    house: 0,
+                    zones: Vec::new(),
+                    items: vec![sector::Item {
+                        palette: 0,
+                        depth: 0,
+                        attrs: sector::Attrs::default(),
+                    }],
+                });
+        }
+        tiles
+            .get_mut(&(floor, y, x))
+            .ok_or("source tile")?
+            .items
+            .push(sector::Item {
+                palette,
+                depth: 0,
+                attrs: sector::Attrs {
+                    unique: Some(unique),
+                    ..Default::default()
+                },
+            });
+    }
+    let coordinates: Vec<_> = tiles.keys().copied().collect();
+    data.manifest.world = bundle::Extent {
+        min_x: coordinates
+            .iter()
+            .map(|(_, _, x)| *x)
+            .min()
+            .ok_or("source minimum x")?,
+        min_y: coordinates
+            .iter()
+            .map(|(_, y, _)| *y)
+            .min()
+            .ok_or("source minimum y")?,
+        max_x: coordinates
+            .iter()
+            .map(|(_, _, x)| *x)
+            .max()
+            .ok_or("source maximum x")?
+            + 1,
+        max_y: coordinates
+            .iter()
+            .map(|(_, y, _)| *y)
+            .max()
+            .ok_or("source maximum y")?
+            + 1,
+        floors: coordinates
+            .iter()
+            .map(|(floor, _, _)| *floor)
+            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()
+            .into_iter()
+            .collect(),
+    };
+    let mut grouped = BTreeMap::<(i8, u16, u16), Vec<sector::Tile>>::new();
+    for ((floor, y, x), tile) in tiles {
+        grouped
+            .entry((floor, x / 32, y / 32))
+            .or_default()
+            .push(tile);
+    }
+    let sectors: Vec<_> = grouped
+        .into_iter()
+        .map(|((floor, sx, sy), tiles)| bundle::Sector {
+            floor,
+            sx,
+            sy,
+            tiles,
+        })
+        .collect();
+    let bytes = oteryn_world_bundle::writer::write(&data.manifest, &sectors, &Default::default())?;
+    pins.digest = bundle::read(&bytes)?.digest;
+    let base = crate::map::load(&bytes, &pins).map_err(super::fixture::debug)?;
+    crate::map::boot::boot(
+        &bytes,
+        &crate::map::boot::BootPins {
+            map_revision: crate::map::overlay::map_revision(&base),
+            bundle: pins,
+            start: crate::map::overlay::TilePos {
+                x: chest.position.0 - 1,
+                y: chest.position.1,
+                floor: chest.position.2,
+            },
+        },
+        crate::foundation::WorldId::decode(&super::fixture::id(42))
+            .map_err(super::fixture::debug)?,
+        crate::foundation::ChannelId::decode(&super::fixture::id(43))
+            .map_err(super::fixture::debug)?,
+        |_| None,
+    )
+    .map_err(super::fixture::debug)
+}
+
+fn execute(cases: Vec<CatalogueChest>) -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() {
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    std::thread::Builder::new()
+        .stack_size(256 << 20)
+        .spawn(move || -> Result<(), String> {
+            // Every case has an isolated durable world/session, using the same
+            // immutable production content generation. Qualify that content once;
+            // no mutable character, command or receipt state is shared.
+            let world = crate::foundation::WorldId::decode(&super::fixture::id(42))
+                .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
+            let activation =
+                super::death_ml_loss_composed::production_generation_with_pin(world, "content-1")
+                    .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
+            let generation = activation.0.active().ok_or("active generation")?;
+            let native = generation.native_gameplay().ok_or("native generation")?;
+            for case in cases {
+                for key in std::iter::once(case.root.as_str())
+                    .chain(case.contents.iter().map(|(key, _, _)| key.as_str()))
+                {
+                    let definition = crate::durability::item_mint::TypedDefinitionRef {
+                        family: "Item".into(), production_key: key.into(), revision_ref: "definition-r1".into(),
+                    };
+                    let facts = crate::gameplay_transport::item_ref_admission::generation_item_facts(native, &definition)
+                        .map_err(|error| format!("{} {key}: {error:?}", case.claim))?;
+                    if matches!(facts.stack, crate::durability::item_transfer::ItemStackClass::Unknown) {
+                        return Err(format!("{} {key}: native Item admission missing", case.claim));
+                    }
+                }
+                let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+                super::quest_container_chests::catalogue_scenario(&case, &activation)
+                    .map_err(|error| format!("{} {:?}: {error}", case.claim, case.position))?;
+                println!(
+                    "QUEST_CATALOGUE_CHEST POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED {} {:?} native_source={} duration={:?}",
+                    case.claim,
+                    case.position,
+                    case.servable,
+                    started.elapsed()
+                );
+            }
+            Ok(())
+        })?
+        .join()
+        .map_err(|_| "catalogue chest scenario panicked")??;
+    Ok(())
+}

+//! Newhaven Corruptor gear travels through the actual kill, corpse, take and equip owners.
+use super::fixture::{Harness, debug, id, run};
+use super::{Dependencies, TestResult, prepare_current_session};

+#[test]
+fn newhaven_corruptor_all_five_vocations_kill_loot_take_equip() -> TestResult {

+        return Ok(());

+        for vocation in ["knight", "paladin", "sorcerer", "druid", "monk"] {
+            let started = Instant::now();
+            scenario(vocation, false, false, vocation == "knight", false)?;
+            println!(
+                "NEWHAVEN_{vocation} POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED {:?}",
+                started.elapsed()
+            );
+        }
+        Ok(())
+    })
