+//! Ordered source NPC storage branches on the established talk and receipt owners.
+use super::super::{ComposedFreshAdmission, connection::UseCommand};
+use super::*;
+use crate::domain::CharacterId;
+use crate::durability::quest_state::{
+    QuestTransitionOutcome,
+    npc_program::{self, NpcProgramRequest},
+};
+use crate::foundation::{CommandId, CommandRef};
+use oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::npc_rules::{NpcRuleCatalog, NpcRuleTrigger};
+use std::sync::Arc;
+
+type Context = (u64, u64, String);
+#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub(super) struct Conversation {
+    context: Context,
+    actor: ExactActorRef,
+    npc: EntityRef,
+    topic: i32,
+}
+#[derive(Clone)]
+pub(super) enum Pending {
+    Topic(Conversation),
+    Commit {
+        character: CharacterId,
+        request: NpcProgramRequest,
+        intent: NpcTalkIntent,
+        next: Conversation,
+        fallback_lines: Vec<String>,
+    },
+}
+pub(super) enum Step {
+    Reply(Vec<String>),
+    Refused,
+    Held,
+    Commit(Box<CommitStep>),
+}
+pub(super) struct CommitStep {
+    context: Context,
+    previous: Option<Conversation>,
+    next: Conversation,
+    request: NpcProgramRequest,
+    fallback_lines: Vec<String>,
+}
+
+pub(super) fn prepare(
+    admission: &ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    actor: ExactActorRef,
+    command: UseCommand,
+    intent: &NpcTalkIntent,
+    runtime: &ChannelRuntimeV1,
+    ordinary: &NpcTalkOutcome,
+) -> Option<Step> {
+    let context = admission.states.npc_conversations.current_npc(
+        &admission.states.chat,
+        runtime,
+        actor,
+        command.game_session_id,
+        intent,
+    )?;
+    let source = NpcRuleCatalog::runtime().ok()?;
+    if !source
+        .programs()
+        .iter()
+        .any(|p| p.document.rules.iter().any(|r| r.npc == context.2))
+    {
+        return None;
+    }
+    let catalogue = admission.quest_catalogue.as_ref()?;
+    let tracks = {
+        let sessions = admission.states.quest_sessions.lock().ok()?;
+        let copy = sessions.get(&command.game_session_id)?.copy.as_ref()?;
+        let mut tracks = copy.tracks().clone();
+        for (quest, state) in copy.states() {
+            if let Some(defaults) = oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::npc_rules::legacy_defaults(
+                catalogue,
+                quest,
+                &state.definition_hash,
+            ) {
+                for (key, value) in defaults {
+                    tracks.entry(key.clone()).or_insert(*value);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        tracks
+    };
+    let mut sessions = admission.states.npc_conversations.sessions.lock().ok()?;
+    let state = sessions.get_mut(&command.game_session_id)?;
+    if matches!(state.catalogue, Some(Pending::Commit { .. })) {
+        return Some(Step::Held);
+    }
+    let previous = match &state.catalogue {
+        Some(Pending::Topic(p))
+            if p.context == context && p.actor == actor && p.npc == intent.npc_actor =>
+        {
+            Some(p.clone())
+        }
+        _ => None,
+    };
+    if previous.is_none() {
+        state.catalogue = None;
+    }
+    let greeting = intent
+        .text
+        .split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_alphabetic())
+        .any(|word| {
+            ["hi", "hello", "hail"]
+                .iter()
+                .any(|g| word.eq_ignore_ascii_case(g))
+        });
+    let mut request = NpcProgramRequest {
+        command: CommandRef::new(
+            command.game_session_id,
+            CommandId::new(command.command_id).ok()?,
+        ),
+        npc: context.2.clone(),
+        trigger: if greeting {
+            NpcRuleTrigger::Greet
+        } else {
+            NpcRuleTrigger::Say
+        },
+        message: intent.text.clone(),
+        topic: if greeting {
+            0
+        } else {
+            previous.as_ref().map_or(0, |p| p.topic)
+        },
+        transition_key: String::new(),
+    };
+    // StorageTime is checked again at database transaction time before committing.
+    let now = std::time::SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .ok()
+        .and_then(|d| i64::try_from(d.as_secs()).ok())?;
+    let plan = match npc_program::preview(&request, &tracks, catalogue, now) {
+        Ok(Some(plan)) => plan,
+        Ok(None) => {
+            if greeting {
+                state.catalogue = None;
+            }
+            return None;
+        }
+        Err(_) => return Some(Step::Refused),
+    };
+    let next = Conversation {
+        context: context.clone(),
+        actor,
+        npc: intent.npc_actor,
+        topic: plan.next_topic,
+    };
+    if plan.track_changes.is_empty() {
+        state.catalogue = Some(Pending::Topic(next));
+        return if plan.reply.is_empty() {
+            None
+        } else {
+            Some(Step::Reply(plan.reply))
+        };
+    }
+    request.transition_key = plan.selected_rule_keys.first()?.clone();
+    let fallback_lines = if greeting && plan.reply.is_empty() {
+        ordinary
+            .conversation
+            .as_ref()
+            .map(|conversation| conversation.lines.clone())
+            .unwrap_or_default()
+    } else {
+        Vec::new()
+    };
+    Some(Step::Commit(Box::new(CommitStep {
+        context,
+        previous,
+        next,
+        request,
+        fallback_lines,
+    })))
+}
+
+fn response(
+    admission: &ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    session: GameSessionId,
+    intent: &NpcTalkIntent,
+    mut lines: Vec<String>,
+) -> NpcTalkOutcome {
+    if let Some(name) = admission.states.chat.speaker_name(session) {
+        for line in &mut lines {
+            *line = oteryn_gameplay_npc::quest_greetings::render_player_name(line, &name);
+        }
+    }
+    NpcTalkOutcome {
+        unknown: false,
+        inventory_changed: false,
+        disposition: NpcIntentDisposition::Ok,
+        conversation: Some(NpcConversation {
+            open: Some((intent.npc_actor, NpcConversationState::Open)),
+            lines,
+        }),
+        window: None,
+    }
+}
+fn install(
+    admission: &ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    session: GameSessionId,
+    next: Option<Conversation>,
+) {
+    if let Ok(mut sessions) = admission.states.npc_conversations.sessions.lock()
+        && let Some(state) = sessions.get_mut(&session)
+    {
+        state.catalogue = next.map(Pending::Topic);
+    }
+}
+
+pub(super) async fn apply(
+    admission: &ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    actor: ExactActorRef,
+    command: UseCommand,
+    intent: NpcTalkIntent,
+    step: Step,
+) -> NpcTalkOutcome {
+    let rejected = || NpcTalkOutcome::only(NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected);
+    let unknown = || NpcTalkOutcome {
+        unknown: true,
+        ..rejected()
+    };
+    let CommitStep {
+        context,
+        previous,
+        next,
+        request,
+        fallback_lines,
+    } = match step {
+        Step::Reply(lines) => return response(admission, command.game_session_id, &intent, lines),
+        Step::Refused => return rejected(),
+        Step::Held => return unknown(),
+        Step::Commit(c) => *c,
+    };
+    let (Some(item_fence), Some(catalogue)) =
+        (command.item_fence, admission.quest_catalogue.as_ref())
+    else {
+        return rejected();
+    };
+    let mut slot = admission
+        .revision_sequencer
+        .acquire(item_fence.character_id)
+        .await;
+    let Ok(Some(fence)) = admission.current_quest_fence(command.game_session_id).await else {
+        return unknown();
+    };
+    if fence.item_fence() != item_fence {
+        return rejected();
+    }
+    {
+        let runtime = admission.runtime.lock().await;
+        let Ok(states) = admission.states.spell_states.try_lock() else {
+            return rejected();
+        };
+        if states.is_dead(actor)
+            || !states.spell_idle(&runtime, actor, command.game_session_id)
+            || admission.states.npc_conversations.current_npc(
+                &admission.states.chat,
+                &runtime,
+                actor,
+                command.game_session_id,
+                &intent,
+            ) != Some(context)
+            || !runtime
+                .bound_attacker_lease(actor, command.game_session_id)
+                .is_ok_and(|lease| {
+                    lease.character_id().as_bytes() == fence.character_id.as_bytes()
+                        && lease.generation() == fence.character_lease_generation
+                })
+        {
+            return rejected();
+        }
+    }
+    {
+        let Ok(mut sessions) = admission.states.npc_conversations.sessions.lock() else {
+            return rejected();
+        };
+        let Some(state) = sessions.get_mut(&command.game_session_id) else {
+            return rejected();
+        };
+        if matches!(state.catalogue, Some(Pending::Commit { .. })) {
+            return unknown();
+        }
+        let current = match &state.catalogue {
+            Some(Pending::Topic(p)) => Some(p),
+            _ => None,
+        };
+        if current != previous.as_ref() {
+            return rejected();
+        }
+        state.catalogue = Some(Pending::Commit {
+            character: fence.character_id,
+            request: request.clone(),
+            intent: intent.clone(),
+            next: next.clone(),
+            fallback_lines: fallback_lines.clone(),
+        });
+    }
+    let outcome = slot
+        .commit_npc_program(
+            admission.root,
+            admission.character,
+            admission.holder,
+            fence,
+            request,
+            Arc::clone(catalogue),
+        )
+        .await;
+    drop(slot);
+    match outcome {
+        Ok(commit)
+            if matches!(
+                commit.transition,
+                QuestTransitionOutcome::Committed(_) | QuestTransitionOutcome::AlreadyCommitted(_)
+            ) =>
+        {
+            install(
+                admission,
+                command.game_session_id,
+                Some(Conversation {
+                    topic: commit.next_topic,
+                    ..next
+                }),
+            );
+            admission
+                .refresh_quest_session(command.game_session_id, false)
+                .await;
+            response(
+                admission,
+                command.game_session_id,
+                &intent,
+                if commit.reply.is_empty() {
+                    fallback_lines
+                } else {
+                    commit.reply
+                },
+            )
+        }
+        Err(crate::durability::character_progression::CharacterProgressionError::Unavailable(
+            _,
+        )) => unknown(),
+        _ => {
+            install(admission, command.game_session_id, None);
+            rejected()
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+pub(in crate::gameplay_transport) async fn resume(
+    admission: &ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    command: UseCommand,
+) -> Option<NpcTalkOutcome> {
+    let pending = admission
+        .states
+        .npc_conversations
+        .sessions
+        .lock()
+        .ok()?
+        .get(&command.game_session_id)?
+        .catalogue
+        .clone()?;
+    let Pending::Commit {
+        character,
+        request,
+        intent,
+        next,
+        fallback_lines,
+    } = pending
+    else {
+        return None;
+    };
+    if request.command
+        != CommandRef::new(
+            command.game_session_id,
+            CommandId::new(command.command_id).ok()?,
+        )
+    {
+        return None;
+    }
+    Some(
+        match admission
+            .root
+            .reconcile_npc_program(admission.character, character, request)
+            .await
+        {
+            Ok(Some(commit)) => {
+                install(
+                    admission,
+                    command.game_session_id,
+                    Some(Conversation {
+                        topic: commit.next_topic,
+                        ..next
+                    }),
+                );
+                admission
+                    .refresh_quest_session(command.game_session_id, false)
+                    .await;
+                response(
+                    admission,
+                    command.game_session_id,
+                    &intent,
+                    if commit.reply.is_empty() {
+                        fallback_lines
+                    } else {
+                        commit.reply
+                    },
+                )
+            }
+            Ok(None) => {
+                install(admission, command.game_session_id, None);
+                NpcTalkOutcome::only(NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected)
+            }
+            Err(_) => NpcTalkOutcome {
+                unknown: true,
+                ..NpcTalkOutcome::only(NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected)
+            },
+        },
+    )
+}

+//! Placed Viola uses command 7; current Wiki retires her spell-teaching service.
+use super::super::{ComposedFreshAdmission, connection::UseCommand};
+use super::*;
+use crate::durability::tutorial_viola::{ViolaAction, ViolaReply};
+use crate::foundation::{CommandId, CommandRef};
+
+type Context = (u64, u64, String);
+#[derive(Clone)]
+pub(super) enum Pending {
+    Offer {
+        context: Context,
+        npc: EntityRef,
+        fence: crate::durability::item_transfer::CurrentCharacterItemFence,
+        opened: u64,
+    },
+    Commit {
+        character: crate::domain::CharacterId,
+        command: CommandRef,
+        intent: NpcTalkIntent,
+    },
+}
+pub(super) struct Step {
+    context: Context,
+    action: Option<ViolaAction>,
+    reply: Option<&'static str>,
+}
+
+pub(super) fn prepare(
+    admission: &ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    actor: ExactActorRef,
+    command: UseCommand,
+    intent: &NpcTalkIntent,
+    runtime: &ChannelRuntimeV1,
+) -> Option<Step> {
+    let context = admission.states.npc_conversations.current_npc(
+        &admission.states.chat,
+        runtime,
+        actor,
+        command.game_session_id,
+        intent,
+    )?;
+    if context.2 != "oteryn:npc.viola" {
+        return None;
+    }
+    let text = intent.text.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
+    let mut sessions = admission.states.npc_conversations.sessions.lock().ok()?;
+    let state = sessions.get_mut(&command.game_session_id)?;
+    if matches!(state.viola, Some(Pending::Commit { .. })) {
+        return Some(Step {
+            context,
+            action: None,
+            reply: None,
+        });
+    }
+    let pending = state.viola.take();
+    let mut reply = None;
+    let action = match text.as_str() {
+        "mission" | "task" | "book" => Some(ViolaAction::Mission),
+        "yes" => match pending {
+            Some(Pending::Offer {
+                context: prior,
+                npc,
+                fence,
+                opened,
+            }) if prior == context
+                && npc == intent.npc_actor
+                && Some(fence) == command.item_fence
+                && admission
+                    .owner_now()
+                    .get()
+                    .checked_sub(opened)
+                    .is_some_and(|age| age < 30_000_000) =>
+            {
+                Some(ViolaAction::Accept)
+            }
+            _ => {
+                reply = Some("Ask me about a mission first.");
+                None
+            }
+        },
+        "no" => {
+            reply = Some("Please come back if you change your mind.");
+            None
+        }
+        "learn" | "spells" | "teach" => {
+            reply = Some("I have retired from teaching spells and now devote my time to reading.");
+            None
+        }
+        _ => return None,
+    };
+    Some(Step {
+        context,
+        action,
+        reply,
+    })
+}
+fn line(reply: ViolaReply) -> &'static str {
+    match reply {
+        ViolaReply::ReturnOffer => "Glad to see you are back. Did you find my book? Say yes or no.",
+        ViolaReply::Offer => {
+            "A ghost took my Book of Wisdom into the mines to the northeast. Would you retrieve it for me? Say yes or no."
+        }
+        ViolaReply::Accepted => {
+            "Thank you! The book should be in a chest in the corrupted mines to the northeast. Please be careful."
+        }
+        ViolaReply::MissingBook => {
+            "Have you found my Book of Wisdom? Look in the mines to the northeast."
+        }
+        ViolaReply::Completed => {
+            "You found my Book of Wisdom! Please accept this scarf and 800 experience as thanks."
+        }
+        ViolaReply::AlreadyCompleted => {
+            "You have already helped me recover my book. Thank you again!"
+        }
+        ViolaReply::NoRoom => {
+            "I cannot give you the scarf. Please make room and return with my book."
+        }
+    }
+}
+fn complete(
+    admission: &ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    command: UseCommand,
+    intent: &NpcTalkIntent,
+    context: Option<Context>,
+    reply: ViolaReply,
+) -> NpcTalkOutcome {
+    if let Ok(mut sessions) = admission.states.npc_conversations.sessions.lock()
+        && let Some(state) = sessions.get_mut(&command.game_session_id)
+    {
+        state.viola = if matches!(reply, ViolaReply::Offer | ViolaReply::ReturnOffer) {
+            context
+                .zip(command.item_fence)
+                .map(|(context, fence)| Pending::Offer {
+                    context,
+                    npc: intent.npc_actor,
+                    fence,
+                    opened: admission.owner_now().get(),
+                })
+        } else {
+            None
+        };
+    }
+    if reply == ViolaReply::Completed
+        && let Some(fence) = command.item_fence
+    {
+        admission.states.level_refresh.mark([fence.character_id]);
+    }
+    NpcTalkOutcome {
+        inventory_changed: reply == ViolaReply::Completed,
+        ..NpcConversations::bank_reply(intent, line(reply).into())
+    }
+}
+pub(super) async fn apply(
+    admission: &ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    actor: ExactActorRef,
+    command: UseCommand,
+    intent: NpcTalkIntent,
+    step: Step,
+) -> NpcTalkOutcome {
+    let rejected = || NpcTalkOutcome::only(NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected);
+    let unknown = || NpcTalkOutcome {
+        unknown: true,
+        ..rejected()
+    };
+    let Some(action) = step.action else {
+        return step.reply.map_or_else(unknown, |line| {
+            NpcConversations::bank_reply(&intent, line.into())
+        });
+    };
+    let (Some(item_fence), Ok(id), Some(binding), Some(native)) = (
+        command.item_fence,
+        CommandId::new(command.command_id),
+        admission.progression_binding(command.game_session_id),
+        admission
+            .active_generation
+            .and_then(crate::content::ActiveGeneration::native_gameplay),
+    ) else {
+        return rejected();
+    };
+    let Some(revision) = native
+        .item_index()
+        .and_then(|index| index.revision("oteryn:item.tibia.i3572"))
+    else {
+        return rejected();
+    };
+    let output = crate::durability::item_mint::TypedDefinitionRef {
+        family: "Item".into(),
+        production_key: "oteryn:item.tibia.i3572".into(),
+        revision_ref: revision.into(),
+    };
+    let mut slot = admission
+        .revision_sequencer
+        .acquire(item_fence.character_id)
+        .await;
+    let Ok(Some(mut fence)) = admission.current_quest_fence(command.game_session_id).await else {
+        return unknown();
+    };
+    if fence.item_fence() != item_fence {
+        return rejected();
+    }
+    {
+        let runtime = admission.runtime.lock().await;
+        let Ok(states) = admission.states.spell_states.try_lock() else {
+            return rejected();
+        };
+        if states.is_dead(actor)
+            || !states.spell_idle(&runtime, actor, command.game_session_id)
+            || admission.states.npc_conversations.current_npc(
+                &admission.states.chat,
+                &runtime,
+                actor,
+                command.game_session_id,
+                &intent,
+            ) != Some(step.context.clone())
+            || !runtime
+                .bound_attacker_lease(actor, command.game_session_id)
+                .is_ok_and(|lease| {
+                    lease.character_id().as_bytes() == fence.character_id.as_bytes()
+                        && lease.generation() == fence.character_lease_generation
+                })
+        {
+            return rejected();
+        }
+    }
+    let Ok(cursor) = slot.cursor(admission.root, admission.character).await else {
+        return unknown();
+    };
+    fence.expected_character_revision = cursor;
+    let reference = CommandRef::new(command.game_session_id, id);
+    if let Ok(mut sessions) = admission.states.npc_conversations.sessions.lock() {
+        sessions.entry(command.game_session_id).or_default().viola = Some(Pending::Commit {
+            character: fence.character_id,
+            command: reference,
+            intent: intent.clone(),
+        });
+    } else {
+        return unknown();
+    }
+    let result = admission
+        .root
+        .commit_viola(
+            admission.character,
+            admission.holder,
+            fence,
+            reference,
+            action,
+            binding.policy.clone(),
+            &super::super::item_ref_admission::NativeItemWeights(native),
+            output,
+            native.source_digest(),
+        )
+        .await;
+    slot.observe_endgame_progression();
+    match result {
+        Ok(reply) => complete(admission, command, &intent, Some(step.context), reply),
+        Err(crate::durability::character_progression::CharacterProgressionError::Unavailable(
+            _,
+        )) => unknown(),
+        Err(_) => {
+            if let Ok(mut sessions) = admission.states.npc_conversations.sessions.lock()
+                && let Some(state) = sessions.get_mut(&command.game_session_id)
+            {
+                state.viola = None;
+            }
+            rejected()
+        }
+    }
+}
+pub(super) async fn resume(
+    admission: &ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    command: UseCommand,
+) -> Option<NpcTalkOutcome> {
+    let (character, reference, intent) = {
+        let sessions = admission.states.npc_conversations.sessions.lock().ok()?;
+        match sessions.get(&command.game_session_id)?.viola.as_ref()? {
+            Pending::Commit {
+                character,
+                command: reference,
+                intent,
+            } if reference.command_id().get() == command.command_id => {
+                (*character, *reference, intent.clone())
+            }
+            _ => return None,
+        }
+    };
+    match admission
+        .root
+        .reconcile_viola(admission.character, character, reference)
+        .await
+    {
+        Ok(Some(reply)) => Some(complete(admission, command, &intent, None, reply)),
+        Ok(None) => {
+            if let Ok(mut sessions) = admission.states.npc_conversations.sessions.lock()
+                && let Some(state) = sessions.get_mut(&command.game_session_id)
+            {
+                state.viola = None;
+            }
+            Some(NpcTalkOutcome::only(NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected))
+        }
+        Err(_) => Some(NpcTalkOutcome {
+            unknown: true,
+            ..NpcTalkOutcome::only(NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected)
+        }),
+    }
+}

+//! Six repeat chests must use the dedicated durable cycle owner, never once claims.
+use super::TestResult;
+
+type DeliveredCycle = (i64, Vec<u8>, i16, Option<i64>, String, String, i64);
+
+#[test]
+fn falcon_chests_exact_six_sources_and_seven_rewards() -> TestResult {
+    use oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::falcon::{CHESTS, REWARDS, claim_key, draw_index};
+    assert_eq!(CHESTS.len(), 6);
+    assert_eq!(
+        REWARDS,
+        [
+            (9081, 1),
+            (28821, 1),
+            (28823, 1),
+            (9058, 1),
+            (6299, 1),
+            (3052, 1),
+            (3035, 10)
+        ]
+    );
+    for chest in CHESTS {
+        assert!(claim_key(&chest.key()).is_some());
+    }
+    let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+    for value in 0..=255 {
+        let entropy = [value; 32];
+        let index = draw_index(&entropy);
+        assert!(index < 7);
+        assert_eq!(index, draw_index(&entropy));
+        seen.insert(index);
+    }
+    assert_eq!(seen.len(), 7);
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+use super::fixture::{Harness, debug, id, run};
+use super::quest_callbacks::{source_world, wire_uses};
+use super::{Dependencies, prepare_current_session};
+use crate::content::channel_content_pin::ChannelContentPin;

+#[test]
+pub(super) fn falcon_chests_postgres_real_use_frozen_draw_refusal_restart_and_fences() -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() {
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    std::thread::Builder::new()
+        .stack_size(256 << 20)
+        .spawn(|| -> Result<(), String> {
+            let world = crate::foundation::WorldId::decode(&id(42))
+                .map_err(debug)
+                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+            let activation =
+                super::death_ml_loss_composed::production_generation_with_pin(world, "content-1")
+                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+            for chest in CHESTS {
+                let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+                scenario(chest, &activation).map_err(|e| format!("{}: {e}", chest.suffix))?;
+                println!(
+                    "FALCON_CHEST POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED {} duration={:?}",
+                    chest.suffix,
+                    started.elapsed()
+                );
+            }
+            Ok(())
+        })?
+        .join()
+        .map_err(|_| "Falcon scenario panicked")??;
+    Ok(())
+}
+fn definition(id: u16) -> TypedDefinitionRef {
+    TypedDefinitionRef {
+        family: "Item".into(),
+        production_key: format!("oteryn:item.tibia.i{id}"),
+        revision_ref: "definition-r1".into(),
+    }
+}
+fn scenario(
+    chest: Chest,
+    activation: &super::quest_catalogue_chests::CatalogueActivation,
+) -> TestResult {
+    run(async |admin| {
+        let h = Harness::create_with_admission_seed(admin, "falcon_chests", false, false).await?;
+        prepare_current_session(&h).await?;
+        let seal = h.recovery.seal_current().map_err(debug)?;
+        let authority = h
+            .root
+            .open_character_authority(&seal)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let mut deps = Dependencies::new()?;
+        let (controller, activated_pin) = activation;
+        let generation = controller.active().ok_or("active generation")?;
+        let native = generation.native_gameplay().ok_or("native generation")?;
+        for (id, _) in REWARDS {
+            generation_item_facts(native, &definition(id)).map_err(debug)?;
+        }
+        let world = source_world(chest.position, chest.unique, chest.appearance, 0)?;
+        let cells = world
+            .movement_cells(deps.room.movement_cells())
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let pin = ChannelContentPin::from_activation(
+            deps.admitted.world_id,
+            1,
+            generation.identity().server_artifact_digest(),
+            generation.identity().client_artifact_digest(),
+            activated_pin.frame_binding().digest(),
+            world.map_revision_digest(),
+            (
+                i32::from(chest.position.0) - 1,
+                i32::from(chest.position.1),
+                -i16::from(chest.position.2),
+            ),
+        );
+        let mut runtime = ChannelRuntimeV1::from_committed_assignment(
+            deps.admitted.world_id,
+            deps.admitted.channel_id,
+            crate::foundation::NodeId::decode(&id(1)).map_err(debug)?,
+            1,
+            1,
+            1,
+            "runtime-scope-assignment:1",
+            2,
+            pin,
+        )
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+        let reservation = runtime
+            .reserve_fresh_session(deps.admitted.game_session_id)
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        deps.actor = runtime.commit_fresh_session(reservation).map_err(debug)?;
+        runtime
+            .initialize_pinned_test_position(
+                deps.actor,
+                MovementLocalPosition {
+                    x: i32::from(chest.position.0) - 1,
+                    y: i32::from(chest.position.1),
+                    floor: -i16::from(chest.position.2),
+                },
+            )
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        deps.runtime = tokio::sync::Mutex::new(runtime);
+        let current =
+            crate::durability::fresh_admission::FreshAdmissionStore::from_root(h.root.clone())
+                .current_session_at(deps.admitted.game_session_id)
+                .await?
+                .0;
+        let fence = crate::gameplay_transport::item_fence_of(current).ok_or("item fence")?;
+        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_control_scope_grants VALUES(session_user,encode($1,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid,4)").bind(id(42).as_slice()).bind(id(43).as_slice()).execute(&h.pool).await?;
+        sqlx::query("SELECT game_content_record_activation(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid,1,NULL,$3,$4,$5)")
+            .bind(id(42).as_slice()).bind(id(43).as_slice()).bind(generation.identity().server_artifact_digest().as_slice())
+            .bind(generation.identity().client_artifact_digest().as_slice()).bind(activated_pin.frame_binding().digest().as_slice()).execute(&h.pool).await?;
+        let weights = NativeItemWeights(native);
+        let fresh = super::fresh_fixture::fresh::Source::new(0)?.request()?;
+        h.root
+            .initialize_admission_equipment(
+                &authority,
+                &h.node,
+                &fence,
+                generation.identity().server_artifact_digest(),
+                fresh.operation(),
+            )
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let template = crate::durability::starter_grant::StarterTemplate {
+            definition: definition(2854),
+            ..crate::durability::starter_grant::StarterTemplate::main()
+        };
+        h.root
+            .grant_starter_items(&authority, &h.node, &fence, &weights, &template)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let mut admitted = deps.admitted;
+        admitted.first_entry = FirstEntryOutcome::Positioned;
+        admitted.item_fence = Some(fence);
+        let key = oteryn_world_bundle::bundle::placement_key(
+            chest.position.2,
+            chest.position.0,
+            chest.position.1,
+            1,
+        )
+        .ok_or("placement")?;
+        let mut placement = world.base().digest().to_vec();
+        placement.extend_from_slice(&key.to_be_bytes());
+        let target = WorldObjectTarget {
+            placement,
+            expected_revision: 0,
+        };
+        let full = chest.unique == 6105;
+        if full {
+            for index in 0..20 {
+                let request = RewardClaimMintRequest {
+                    command: CommandRef::new(
+                        admitted.game_session_id,
+                        CommandId::new(100 + index).map_err(debug)?,
+                    ),
+                    claim: TypedDefinitionRef {
+                        family: "RewardClaim".into(),
+                        production_key: format!("fixture:falcon-burden.{index}"),
+                        revision_ref: "definition-r1".into(),
+                    },
+                    source_placement: "fixture:falcon-burden".into(),
+                    item: generation_item_facts(native, &definition(3031)).map_err(debug)?,
+                    quantity: 1,
+                    contents: Vec::new(),
+                    root_text: None,
+                    root_fluid_subtype: None,
+                    root_charges_remaining: None,
+                    backpack: generation_item_facts(native, &template.definition).map_err(debug)?,
+                    content_revision: "content-1".into(),
+                    ruleset_revision: "ruleset-1".into(),
+                    sim_revision: "sim-1".into(),
+                    achievement: None,
+                    quest_transition: None,
+                    key_number: None,
+                };
+                let mut candidate = h
+                    .root
+                    .freeze_reward_claim_mint(&authority, &h.node, fence, request)
+                    .await
+                    .map_err(debug)?;
+                h.root
+                    .commit_reward_claim_mint(&authority, &h.node, fence, &mut candidate)
+                    .await
+                    .map_err(debug)?;
+            }
+        }
+        let mut frozen_draw = None;
+        {
+            let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+            owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+            owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+            owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+            deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+            let mut stale = admitted;
+            let mut stale_fence = fence;
+            stale_fence.character_lease_generation += 1;
+            stale.item_fence = Some(stale_fence);
+            assert_eq!(
+                wire_uses(&owner, stale, &[1], &target).await?,
+                [UseDisposition::Rejected]
+            );
+            let count: i64 =
+                sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_falcon_chest_cycles")
+                    .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+                    .await?;
+            assert_eq!(count, 0, "stale authority must not freeze a draw");
+        }
+        if full {
+            let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+            owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+            owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+            owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+            deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+            assert_eq!(
+                wire_uses(&owner, admitted, &[1, 2], &target).await?,
+                [UseDisposition::Rejected, UseDisposition::Rejected]
+            );
+            let draw: (Vec<u8>, i16, Option<i64>) = sqlx::query_as(
+                "SELECT entropy,selected_index,claimed_at FROM game_character_falcon_chest_cycles",
+            )
+            .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+            .await?;
+            assert!(draw.2.is_none());
+            frozen_draw = Some((draw.0, draw.1));
+            let count:i64=sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_reward_claim_mint_reservations WHERE claim_production_key LIKE '%falcon_bastion%'").fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+            assert_eq!(count, 0, "refusal writes only the cycle draw");
+            free_one(&owner, fence, chest, 500).await?;
+        }
+        // Recreate the composed owner: neither refusal draw nor mint replay can
+        // depend on an in-memory RNG value or a connection's terminal-result cache.
+        for cold in [false, true] {
+            let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+            owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+            owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+            owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+            deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+            assert_eq!(
+                wire_uses(&owner, admitted, &[1, 1, 2], &target).await?,
+                [
+                    UseDisposition::Committed,
+                    UseDisposition::Committed,
+                    UseDisposition::NothingToUse
+                ],
+                "cold={cold}"
+            );
+        }
+        let rows:Vec<DeliveredCycle>=sqlx::query_as("SELECT c.cycle,c.entropy,c.selected_index,c.claimed_at,r.claim_production_key,i.definition_production_key,r.quantity FROM game_character_falcon_chest_cycles c JOIN game_reward_claim_mint_receipts r ON r.transaction_id=c.transaction_id JOIN game_item_instances i ON i.item_instance_id=r.item_instance_id WHERE c.chest_key=$1")
+            .bind(chest.key()).fetch_all(&h.pool).await?;
+        assert_eq!(rows.len(), 1);
+        let row = &rows[0];
+        assert_eq!(row.0, 1);
+        assert!(row.3.is_some());
+        let entropy: [u8; 32] = row.1.as_slice().try_into()?;
+        assert_eq!(usize::try_from(row.2)?, draw_index(&entropy));
+        if let Some(draw) = frozen_draw {
+            assert_eq!(
+                (&row.1, row.2),
+                (&draw.0, draw.1),
+                "refusal must never reroll"
+            );
+        }
+        let (reward, count) = REWARDS[draw_index(&entropy)];
+        assert_eq!(row.4, format!("{}:cycle:1", chest.key()));
+        assert_eq!(row.5, format!("oteryn:item.tibia.i{reward}"));
+        assert_eq!(row.6, i64::from(count));
+        let count: i64 =
+            sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_falcon_chest_cycles")
+                .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+                .await?;
+        assert_eq!(count, 1);
+        // This isolated database owns its helper definition. Advance only the
+        // server DB-clock seam: receipts and all guards remain active and unchanged.
+        let claimed = row.3.ok_or("claimed time")?;
+        for (now, expected) in [
+            (claimed + 86_400_000 - 1, UseDisposition::NothingToUse),
+            (claimed + 86_400_000, UseDisposition::Committed),
+        ] {
+            // The only interpolation is an i64 obtained from this fixture receipt.
+            sqlx::query(sqlx::AssertSqlSafe(format!("CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION game_falcon_now_ms() RETURNS BIGINT LANGUAGE SQL VOLATILE AS 'SELECT {now}::bigint'")))
+                .execute(&h.pool).await?;
+            let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+            owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+            owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+            owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+            deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+            if full && expected == UseDisposition::Committed {
+                free_one(&owner, fence, chest, 501).await?;
+            }
+            assert_eq!(
+                wire_uses(&owner, admitted, &[1, 2], &target).await?,
+                [UseDisposition::Committed, expected],
+                "repeat boundary time={now}"
+            );
+        }
+        {
+            // Replaying the old command after the second cycle must still
+            // return its original receipt, never consume another cycle.
+            let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+            owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+            owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+            owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+            deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+            assert_eq!(
+                wire_uses(&owner, admitted, &[1], &target).await?,
+                [UseDisposition::Committed]
+            );
+        }
+        let cycles: Vec<(i64, i64)> = sqlx::query_as(
+            "SELECT cycle,claimed_at FROM game_character_falcon_chest_cycles ORDER BY cycle",
+        )
+        .fetch_all(&h.pool)
+        .await?;
+        assert_eq!(cycles, [(1, claimed), (2, claimed + 86_400_000)]);
+        let minted:i64=sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_reward_claim_mint_receipts WHERE claim_production_key LIKE '%falcon_bastion%'").fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+        assert_eq!(
+            minted, 2,
+            "new cycle mints once, old command replays its original cycle"
+        );
+        drop(authority);
+        drop(seal);
+        h.cleanup().await
+    })
+}
+
+async fn free_one(
+    owner: &crate::gameplay_transport::ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    fence: crate::durability::item_transfer::CurrentCharacterItemFence,
+    chest: Chest,
+    command_id: u64,
+) -> TestResult {
+    let native = owner
+        .active_generation
+        .and_then(|generation| generation.native_gameplay())
+        .ok_or("native")?;
+    let bag = owner
+        .root
+        .read_character_backpack(owner.character, fence.character_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?
+        .ok_or("bag")?;
+    let filler = bag
+        .entries
+        .iter()
+        .find(|entry| entry.item.definition.production_key == "oteryn:item.tibia.i3031")
+        .ok_or("filler")?;
+    let (digest, frame) = {
+        let runtime = owner.runtime.lock().await;
+        (
+            runtime.content_pin().server_artifact_digest(),
+            runtime.content_pin().frame_binding_digest(),
+        )
+    };
+    let revision = format!(
+        "sha256:{}",
+        digest
+            .iter()
+            .map(|b| format!("{b:02x}"))
+            .collect::<String>()
+    );
+    let permit = owner.spell_lane_permit().await.ok_or("lane permit")?;
+    let moved = owner
+        .root
+        .commit_item_loop_move(
+            &permit,
+            owner.character,
+            owner.holder,
+            &fence,
+            CommandRef::new(
+                fence.game_session_id,
+                CommandId::new(command_id).map_err(debug)?,
+            ),
+            &NativeItemWeights(native),
+            crate::durability::item_loop_move::LoopMoveRequest {
+                item: filler.item.item_instance_id,
+                count: None,
+                pickup_cell: None,
+                destination: crate::durability::item_loop_move::LoopDestination::Ground(
+                    crate::durability::item_loop_move::LoopGround {
+                        spatial_position:
+                            crate::durability::map_item_mint_audit::map_item_spatial_position(
+                                chest.position.0 - 1,
+                                chest.position.1,
+                                chest.position.2,
+                            ),
+                        map_revision: crate::map::overlay::map_revision(
+                            owner.bundle_map().ok_or("bundle map")?.overlay.base(),
+                        ),
+                        content_revision: revision,
+                        placement_context: frame.to_vec(),
+                    },
+                ),
+            },
+        )
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    assert!(moved.is_ok(), "free capacity: {moved:?}");
+    Ok(())
+}

+//! Newhaven Corruptor gear travels through the actual kill, corpse, take and equip owners.
+use super::fixture::{Harness, debug, id, run};
+use super::{Dependencies, TestResult, prepare_current_session};

+#[test]
+fn newhaven_corruptor_all_five_vocations_kill_loot_take_equip() -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() {
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    super::kill_reward_e2e::scenario_thread::run("newhaven-corruptor", || {
+        for vocation in ["knight", "paladin", "sorcerer", "druid", "monk"] {
+            let started = Instant::now();
+            scenario(vocation, false, false, vocation == "knight", false)?;
+            println!(
+                "NEWHAVEN_{vocation} POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED {:?}",
+                started.elapsed()
+            );
+        }
+        Ok(())
+    })
+}
