+fn scenario(
+    vocation: &'static str,
+    low_stamina: bool,
+    double_loot: bool,
+    fail_commit: bool,
+    lifecycle: bool,
+) -> TestResult {
+    use oteryn_gameplay_foundation::scheduled_events::{Schedule, test_clock};
+    let schedule = Schedule::parse(if double_loot {
+        r#"{"events":[{"name":"newhaven-double","startdate":"01/01/2030","enddate":"01/02/2030","ingame":{"lootrate":200}}]}"#
+    } else {r#"{"events":[]}"#}).map_err(debug)?;
+    let _clock = test_clock::set(schedule, 1_893_456_000);
+
+    run(async move |admin| {
+        let h =
+            Harness::create_with_admission_seed(admin, "newhaven_corruptor", false, false).await?;
+        prepare_current_session(&h).await?;
+        let seal = h.recovery.seal_current().map_err(debug)?;
+        let authority = h
+            .root
+            .open_character_authority(&seal)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let world = crate::foundation::WorldId::decode(&id(42)).map_err(debug)?;
+        let (controller, pin) = super::death_ml_loss_composed::production_generation_with_source(
+            world,
+            "content-1",
+            Some(include_bytes!(
+                "../../../../../../tools/content-schema/native-gameplay/canary-thalom-world.json"
+            )),
+        )?;
+        let active = controller.active().ok_or("native generation")?;
+        let native = active.native_gameplay().ok_or("native gameplay")?;
+        native
+            .reward_table()
+            .row("oteryn:creature.the_corruptor")
+            .map_err(|reason| {
+                format!(
+                    "Newhaven production kill reward admission: {}",
+                    reason.as_str()
+                )
+            })?;
+        let room = pin.qualified_room().clone();
+        super::tutorial_vitals_fixture::publish_native_activation(&h, &pin).await?;
+        let channel_pin = ChannelContentPin::from_activation(
+            world,
+            pin.activation_sequence(),
+            pin.identity().server_artifact_digest(),
+            pin.identity().client_artifact_digest(),
+            pin.frame_binding().digest(),
+            room.map_revision_digest(),
+            (32534, 32513, 7),
+        );
+        let deps = Dependencies::with_content(
+            channel_pin.clone(),
+            room.clone(),
+            crate::spell::cast::v1_spell_book().map_err(debug)?,
+        )?;
+        let actor = deps.actor;
+        let session = deps.admitted.game_session_id;
+        let target = {
+            let mut runtime = deps.runtime.lock().await;
+            native
+                .install_companion_policies(&mut runtime)
+                .map_err(debug)?;
+            runtime
+                .bind_attacker_lease(
+                    actor,
+                    session,
+                    crate::foundation::CharacterLease::new(
+                        crate::foundation::CharacterId::decode(&id(41)).map_err(debug)?,
+                        2,
+                    )
+                    .map_err(debug)?,
+                )
+                .map_err(debug)?;
+            runtime
+                .initialize_pinned_test_position(
+                    actor,
+                    MovementLocalPosition {
+                        x: 2,
+                        y: 11,
+                        floor: -7,
+                    },
+                )
+                .map_err(debug)?;
+            if fail_commit || double_loot {
+                // Pick an actual actor occurrence with an ordinary Brass Armor
+                // roll. Probabilities stay untouched; the special set must not
+                // replace that independently caused output on the retry. The
+                // event case selects a base gold miss that200% loot restores.
+                // Knight also rolls a real mushroom for the native take route.
+                let reward = native
+                    .reward_table()
+                    .row("oteryn:creature.the_corruptor")
+                    .map_err(debug)?;
+                let mut witness = None;
+                for generation in 1..=20_000 {
+                    let death = oteryn_gameplay_items::loot_plan::LootPlanDeathKey::new(
+                        id(42),
+                        id(43),
+                        1,
+                        2,
+                        generation,
+                    );
+                    let plan = oteryn_gameplay_items::loot_plan::plan_creature_loot(
+                        death,
+                        &reward.loot_table_ref,
+                        &reward.loot_table,
+                    )
+                    .map_err(debug)?;
+                    let selected = if double_loot {
+                        "oteryn:item.tibia.i3031"
+                    } else {
+                        "oteryn:item.tibia.i3359"
+                    };
+                    let present = plan
+                        .entries
+                        .iter()
+                        .any(|entry| entry.item.production_key == selected);
+                    let mushroom = plan
+                        .entries
+                        .iter()
+                        .any(|entry| entry.item.production_key == "oteryn:item.tibia.i3723");
+                    if present != double_loot && (!fail_commit || mushroom) {
+                        witness = Some(generation);
+                        break;
+                    }
+                }
+                for _ in 1..witness.ok_or("ordinary Corruptor occurrence")? {
+                    let unused = runtime
+                        .reserve_fresh_session(GameSessionId::decode(&id(245)).map_err(debug)?)
+                        .map_err(debug)?;
+                    runtime
+                        .rollback_definitely_uncommitted(unused)
+                        .map_err(debug)?;
+                }
+            }
+            runtime
+                .realize_native_qualification_spawn(
+                    channel_pin,
+                    "oteryn:creature.the_corruptor",
+                    MovementLocalPosition {
+                        x: 1,
+                        y: 11,
+                        floor: -7,
+                    },
+                )
+                .map_err(debug)?;
+            let creature = runtime
+                .visible_entities()
+                .creatures
+                .first()
+                .copied()
+                .ok_or("Corruptor spawn")?;
+            EntityRef {
+                identity: creature.actor.placement_identity(),
+                generation: creature.generation,
+            }
+        };
+        let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+        owner.active_generation = Some(active);
+        owner.rooms = crate::gameplay_transport::SeamRooms::new(None, Some(&room));
+        owner.clock_origin = Instant::now()
+            .checked_sub(Duration::from_secs(60))
+            .ok_or("clock")?;
+        {
+            let runtime = owner.runtime.lock().await;
+            assert!(
+                owner
+                    .states
+                    .spell_states
+                    .lock()
+                    .await
+                    .initialize(
+                        &runtime,
+                        actor,
+                        session,
+                        crate::spell::cast::CharacterCastFacts {
+                            vocation: crate::spell::Vocation::Monk,
+                            level: 200,
+                            max_health: 3000,
+                            ..crate::gameplay_transport::actor_spell::tests::FACTS
+                        },
+                        (3, 7_000_000),
+                        owner.owner_now()
+                    )
+                    .is_some()
+            );
+        }
+        let mut admitted = deps.admitted;
+        owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+        // Newhaven weapon rewards retain their actual level-six restrictions.
+        // This character seed represents an eligible tutorial participant.
+        {
+            let mut tx = h.pool.begin().await?;
+            sqlx::query("SET LOCAL session_replication_role=replica")
+                .execute(&mut *tx)
+                .await?;
+            sqlx::query("UPDATE game_character_progression_state SET level=6,total_experience=1500 WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid")
+                .bind(id(41).as_slice()).execute(&mut *tx).await?;
+            tx.commit().await?;
+        }
+        let current =
+            crate::gameplay_transport::character_gameplay_fence::current(&owner, session, false)
+                .await
+                .ok_or("gameplay fence")?;
+        let formula = native.training_formula().ok_or("training formula")?;
+        let choice = crate::gameplay_transport::vocation_choice::vocation_choice_request(
+            crate::durability::character_build::BuildOccurrence::from_bytes(id(94))
+                .map_err(debug)?,
+            crate::durability::character_build::DurableBuildState::default(),
+            vocation,
+            formula,
+            None,
+        )
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+        owner
+            .revision_sequencer
+            .acquire(current.character_id)
+            .await
+            .commit_build(
+                owner.root,
+                owner.character,
+                owner.holder,
+                current,
+                choice,
+                formula,
+                None,
+            )
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let current =
+            crate::gameplay_transport::character_gameplay_fence::current(&owner, session, false)
+                .await
+                .ok_or("vocation fence")?;
+        h.root
+            .initialize_familiar_group(&authority, &h.node, current)
+            .await
+            .map_err(|error| format!("Newhaven ordinary group admission: {error:?}"))?;
+        let armed = owner
+            .tutorial_event(current, TutorialEvent::Forcefield)
+            .await
+            .map_err(|_| "arm Corruptor tutorial")?
+            .ok_or("forcefield arms reward")?;
+        assert!(armed.progress.corruptor);
+        if low_stamina || fail_commit {
+            let runtime = owner.runtime.lock().await;
+            owner.states.spell_states.lock().await.load_stamina(
+                &runtime,
+                actor,
+                session,
+                // This fixture's kill occurs over 60s after admission, so its
+                // first real XP award spends two minutes before loot freezes.
+                if fail_commit { 843 } else { 0 },
+            );
+        }
+        admitted.item_fence = Some(current.item_fence());
+        admitted.first_entry = FirstEntryOutcome::Positioned;
+        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_character_equipment_state(character_id,revision) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,1)")
+            .bind(id(41).as_slice()).execute(&h.pool).await?;
+        // Only the empty backpack is a fixture. Every reward below must come from the kill.
+        {
+            let index = native.item_index().ok_or("item index")?;
+            let mut tx = h.pool.begin().await?;
+            sqlx::query("SET LOCAL session_replication_role=replica")
+                .execute(&mut *tx)
+                .await?;
+            sqlx::raw_sql(sqlx::AssertSqlSafe(
+                crate::durability::item_fee_burn::test_support::item(
+                    210,
+                    "oteryn:item.tibia.i2854",
+                    index
+                        .revision("oteryn:item.tibia.i2854")
+                        .ok_or("backpack revision")?,
+                    1,
+                ),
+            ))
+            .execute(&mut *tx)
+            .await?;
+            sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_item_container_slots(character_id,item_instance_id,world_id,placed_transaction_id) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid,encode($3,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid)")
+                .bind(id(41).as_slice()).bind(id(210).as_slice()).bind(id(42).as_slice()).execute(&mut *tx).await?;
+            tx.commit().await?;
+        }
+        {
+            let runtime = owner.runtime.lock().await;
+            assert_eq!(
+                owner.states.attack.lock().await.set_target(
+                    &runtime,
+                    Some(&room),
+                    actor,
+                    session,
+                    owner.owner_now(),
+                    Some(target),
+                    1
+                ),
+                AttackIntentDisposition::Ok
+            );
+        }
+        let mut swings = 0;
+        while !owner
+            .states
+            .attack
+            .lock()
+            .await
+            .kills()
+            .has_session(session)
+        {
+            owner.refresh_attack_weapon(actor, session).await;
+            owner.drain_auto_attacks().await;
+            swings += 1;
+            assert!(swings < 1024, "Corruptor dies to actual auto-attacks");
+            owner.clock_origin = owner
+                .clock_origin
+                .checked_sub(Duration::from_secs(3))
+                .ok_or("attack clock")?;
+        }
+        let (facts, killed_target, observed, frozen_table) = {
+            let attacks = owner.states.attack.lock().await;
+            let TakeOutcome::Taken(taken) = attacks.kills().take(session, 2) else {
+                return Err("actual Corruptor kill queued".into());
+            };
+            let entry = taken.entry().ok_or("Corruptor death facts")?;
+            let replay = (
+                entry.facts,
+                entry.target,
+                entry.activity_observed_at,
+                std::sync::Arc::clone(&entry.tutorial_loot),
+            );
+            taken.requeue();
+            replay
+        };
+        let prepared = h
+            .root
+            .prepare_tutorial_corruptor(&authority, &h.node, current, facts.death)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?
+            .ok_or("prepared tutorial gear")?;
+        assert_eq!(prepared.items(), corruptor_items(vocation));
+        assert!(
+            owner
+                .tutorial_progress(current)
+                .await
+                .map_err(|_| "retained arm")?
+                .ok_or("armed progress")?
+                .progress
+                .corruptor,
+            "preparation never consumes the reward"
+        );
+        if fail_commit {
+            sqlx::raw_sql(sqlx::AssertSqlSafe(
+                "CREATE FUNCTION newhaven_reject_claim() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$ BEGIN RAISE EXCEPTION 'newhaven simulated commit failure'; END $$; CREATE TRIGGER newhaven_reject_claim BEFORE INSERT ON game_tutorial_corruptor_claims FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION newhaven_reject_claim();".to_owned()
+            )).execute(&h.pool).await?;
+            assert_ne!(
+                owner.drain_kill_rewards(session).await,
+                DrainOutcome::Drained
+            );
+            h.root.maintain_ready_once().await.map_err(debug)?;
+            let current = crate::gameplay_transport::character_gameplay_fence::current(
+                &owner, session, false,
+            )
+            .await
+            .ok_or("failed batch current fence")?;
+            assert!(
+                owner
+                    .tutorial_progress(current)
+                    .await
+                    .map_err(|_| "failed batch arm")?
+                    .ok_or("failed batch progress")?
+                    .progress
+                    .corruptor
+            );
+            let committed:i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_item_mint_receipts WHERE loot_purpose_key='CORPSE_MATERIALIZATION'")
+                .fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+            assert_eq!(
+                committed, 0,
+                "corpse and every gear item roll back with the claim"
+            );
+            sqlx::raw_sql(sqlx::AssertSqlSafe("DROP TRIGGER newhaven_reject_claim ON game_tutorial_corruptor_claims; DROP FUNCTION newhaven_reject_claim();".to_owned()))
+                .execute(&h.pool).await?;
+            h.root.maintain_ready_once().await.map_err(debug)?;
+            let runtime = owner.runtime.lock().await;
+            let mut states = owner.states.spell_states.lock().await;
+            assert_eq!(states.live_stamina(actor, session), Some(841));
+            // Admission reloads preserve hunting progress. Drive another hunting
+            // event one second after the first award's two-minute deadline.
+            let next_award = observed
+                .ok_or("captured hunting time")?
+                .get()
+                .checked_add(121_000_000)
+                .ok_or("next hunting time")?;
+            states.hunting_experience(&runtime, actor, session, next_award);
+            assert_eq!(states.live_stamina(actor, session), Some(840));
+            assert!(
+                frozen_table.get().is_some(),
+                "failed atomic attempt froze ordinary loot"
+            );
+        }
+        drain(&owner, session).await?;
+        let corpse: Vec<u8> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT uuid_send(item_instance_id) FROM game_item_mint_receipts WHERE loot_purpose_key=$1 AND materialized_at IS NOT NULL")
+            .bind(crate::durability::item_mint::CORPSE_MATERIALIZATION_PURPOSE_KEY).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+        let corpse: [u8; 16] = corpse.try_into().map_err(|_| "corpse identity")?;
+        let expected: Vec<String> = corruptor_items(vocation)
+            .iter()
+            .map(|id| format!("oteryn:item.tibia.i{id}"))
+            .collect();
+        let actual: Vec<String> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT i.definition_production_key FROM game_item_corpse_container_entries e JOIN game_item_instances i USING(item_instance_id) WHERE e.parent_item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid ORDER BY e.placement_ordinal")
+            .bind(corpse.as_slice()).fetch_all(&h.pool).await?;
+        if low_stamina {
+            assert_eq!(
+                actual.len(),
+                expected.len(),
+                "zero stamina suppresses ordinary loot while retaining exactly one guaranteed set"
+            );
+        }
+        if double_loot {
+            assert!(
+                actual.iter().any(|key| key == "oteryn:item.tibia.i3031"),
+                "the real200% event restores the selected ordinary gold miss"
+            );
+        }
+        if fail_commit {
+            assert_eq!(
+                actual
+                    .iter()
+                    .filter(|key| key.as_str() == "oteryn:item.tibia.i3359")
+                    .count(),
+                2,
+                "ordinary and guaranteed Brass Armor retain independent causes across failed commit and841->840 retry"
+            );
+            assert!(
+                actual.iter().any(|key| key == "oteryn:item.tibia.i3723"),
+                "the selected ordinary mushroom is minted by the actual kill"
+            );
+        }
+        for (id, key) in corruptor_items(vocation).iter().zip(&expected) {
+            assert!(actual.contains(key), "{vocation} corpse contains {key}");
+            let guaranteed:i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_item_mint_receipts WHERE loot_purpose_key=$1 AND item_instance_id IN (SELECT item_instance_id FROM game_item_corpse_container_entries WHERE parent_item_instance_id=encode($2,'hex')::uuid)")
+                .bind(crate::durability::tutorial_corruptor::guarantee_purpose(*id))
+                .bind(corpse.as_slice()).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+            assert_eq!(
+                guaranteed, 1,
+                "{vocation} has exactly one special {key}, independently of ordinary drops"
+            );
+        }
+        if lifecycle {
+            qualify_lifecycle(&mut owner, &h, corpse).await?;
+            drop(owner);
+            drop(authority);
+            drop(seal);
+            return h.cleanup().await;
+        }
+        let mushroom: Option<(Vec<u8>, i64)> = if fail_commit {
+            let item: (Vec<u8>, i64) = sqlx::query_as("SELECT uuid_send(i.item_instance_id),i.quantity FROM game_item_corpse_container_entries e JOIN game_item_instances i USING(item_instance_id) WHERE e.parent_item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND i.definition_production_key='oteryn:item.tibia.i3723' LIMIT 1")
+                .bind(corpse.as_slice()).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+            assert!((1..=2).contains(&item.1));
+            Some(item)
+        } else {
+            None
+        };
+        admitted.continuity.selected_capabilities =
+            crate::gameplay_transport::capabilities::SelectedCapabilities::select(
+                crate::gameplay_transport::capabilities::BUNDLE_WORLD_OFFERED_CAPABILITIES,
+                &[4, 6, 12, 14, 98, 179, 180],
+            )
+            .ok_or("Newhaven item command capabilities")?;
+        let click = QuickLootRequest {
+            action: QuickLootAction::Click,
+            corpse: Some(corpse),
+            category: None,
+            recipient: None,
+            recipient_id: None,
+            fallback_to_main: false,
+            filter_mode: None,
+            filter_definition_ref: None,
+        };
+        let payload =
+            oteryn_protocol_oteryn::quick_loot::encode_quick_loot_request(&click).map_err(debug)?;
+        let reply = wire_command(
+            &owner,
+            &mut admitted,
+            oteryn_protocol_oteryn::quick_loot::COMMAND_TYPE_QUICK_LOOT,
+            &payload,
+        )
+        .await?;
+        let result = oteryn_protocol_oteryn::quick_loot::decode_quick_loot_result(&reply)
+            .map_err(|error| format!("{vocation} Quick Loot reply: {error:?}"))?;
+        assert!(!result.items.is_empty(), "the real corpse is in reach");
+        assert!(result.items.iter().all(|item| item.accepted));
+        if let Some((item, quantity)) = mushroom {
+            let carried: (i64, bool) = sqlx::query_as("SELECT i.quantity,NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM game_item_corpse_container_entries c WHERE c.item_instance_id=i.item_instance_id) FROM game_item_instances i JOIN game_item_container_entries e USING(item_instance_id) WHERE i.item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND e.character_id=encode($2,'hex')::uuid AND e.parent_item_instance_id=encode($3,'hex')::uuid AND i.definition_production_key='oteryn:item.tibia.i3723'")
+                .bind(item.as_slice()).bind(id(41).as_slice()).bind(id(210).as_slice()).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+            assert_eq!(
+                carried,
+                (quantity, true),
+                "real wire Quick Loot moves the same mushroom and quantity from corpse into the Character backpack"
+            );
+        }
+        for key in &expected {
+            let index = native.item_index().ok_or("item index")?;
+            let definition = index
+                .definition_ref("Item", key, index.revision(key).ok_or("gear revision")?)
+                .ok_or("gear definition")?;
+            let request = oteryn_protocol_oteryn::hotkey_equip::Request { definition }.encode();
+            let reply = wire_command(
+                &owner,
+                &mut admitted,
+                oteryn_protocol_oteryn::hotkey_equip::COMMAND_TYPE_HOTKEY_EQUIP,
+                &request,
+            )
+            .await?;
+            assert_eq!(
+                oteryn_protocol_oteryn::hotkey_equip::decode_reply(&reply)
+                    .map_err(|error| format!("{vocation} equip {key} reply: {error:?}"))?,
+                ItemMoveOutcome::Moved,
+                "{vocation} equips {key}"
+            );
+            let count:i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_equipment_slots e JOIN game_item_instances i USING(item_instance_id) WHERE e.character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND i.definition_production_key=$2")
+                .bind(id(41).as_slice()).bind(key).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+            assert_eq!(count, 1, "actual durable equipment contains {key}");
+        }
+        let original_table = frozen_table.get().cloned().ok_or("frozen ordinary table")?;
+        {
+            let runtime = owner.runtime.lock().await;
+            owner.states.spell_states.lock().await.load_stamina(
+                &runtime,
+                actor,
+                session,
+                if low_stamina { 2520 } else { 0 },
+            );
+        }
+        let minted: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_item_mint_receipts")
+            .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+            .await?;
+        {
+            let mut runtime = owner.runtime.lock().await;
+            let attacks = owner.states.attack.lock().await;
+            crate::gameplay_transport::kill_reward::record_creature_kill(
+                &mut runtime,
+                KillSink {
+                    prey: None,
+                    activity: &[],
+                    queue: attacks.kills(),
+                    rewards: native.reward_table(),
+                    charms: None,
+                },
+                killed_target,
+                facts.principal,
+                facts.death_at_ms,
+                observed,
+            );
+        }
+        {
+            let attacks = owner.states.attack.lock().await;
+            let TakeOutcome::Taken(taken) = attacks.kills().take(session, 2) else {
+                return Err("same death replay queued".into());
+            };
+            let replay = taken.entry().ok_or("replay death")?;
+            assert!(
+                std::sync::Arc::ptr_eq(&frozen_table, &replay.tutorial_loot),
+                "settled corpse recapture shares the original table"
+            );
+            assert_eq!(replay.tutorial_loot.get(), Some(&original_table));
+            taken.requeue();
+        }
+        drain(&owner, session).await?;
+        assert_eq!(
+            sqlx::query_scalar::<_, i64>("SELECT count(*) FROM game_item_mint_receipts")
+                .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+                .await?,
+            minted,
+            "same death replay mints no second gear"
+        );
+        let current =
+            crate::gameplay_transport::character_gameplay_fence::current(&owner, session, false)
+                .await
+                .ok_or("post-equipment fence")?;
+        let progress = owner
+            .tutorial_progress(current)
+            .await
+            .map_err(|_| "read tutorial")?
+            .ok_or("tutorial progress")?;
+        assert!(!progress.progress.corruptor);
+        assert!(progress.progress.hunting);
+        assert_eq!(
+            progress.version, 2,
+            "one atomic consume advances the tutorial once"
+        );
+        let claims:i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_tutorial_corruptor_claims WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid")
+            .bind(id(41).as_slice()).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+        assert_eq!(claims, 1);
+        drop(owner);
+        drop(authority);
+        drop(seal);
+        h.cleanup().await
+    })
+}
+
+async fn drain(
+    owner: &crate::gameplay_transport::ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    session: GameSessionId,
+) -> TestResult {
+    let mut passes = 0;
+    while owner.drain_kill_rewards(session).await != DrainOutcome::Drained {
+        passes += 1;
+        assert!(passes < 8, "actual Corruptor settlement drains");
+        owner.root.maintain_ready_once().await.map_err(debug)?;
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+async fn qualify_lifecycle(
+    owner: &mut crate::gameplay_transport::ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    h: &Harness,
+    corpse: [u8; 16],
+) -> TestResult {
+    sqlx::raw_sql(sqlx::AssertSqlSafe(
+        include_str!("../../../../../../apps/game-server/migrations/1201_corruptor_lifecycle.sql")
+            .to_owned(),
+    ))
+    .execute(&h.pool)
+    .await?;
+    let schedules: Vec<(i32, i64, i64)> = sqlx::query_as("SELECT p.source_stage,p.duration_millis,p.expires_at_unix_ms-m.materialized_at FROM game_spell_field_temporal_schedules p JOIN game_item_mint_receipts m USING(item_instance_id) WHERE p.item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid ORDER BY source_stage")
+        .bind(corpse.as_slice()).fetch_all(&h.pool).await?;
+    assert_eq!(
+        schedules,
+        vec![
+            (1, 600_000, 600_000),
+            (2, 600_000, 1_200_000),
+            (3, 10_000, 1_210_000)
+        ]
+    );
+    let lifetime: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT game_corpse_retirement_deadline(item_instance_id)-materialized_at FROM game_item_mint_receipts WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid")
+        .bind(corpse.as_slice()).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+    assert_eq!(lifetime, 1_810_000);
+    for sql in [
+        "UPDATE game_spell_field_temporal_schedules SET duration_millis=1 WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+        "DELETE FROM game_spell_field_temporal_schedules WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+        "INSERT INTO game_spell_field_temporal_schedules SELECT item_instance_id,4,blocks_movement,blocks_projectile,immovable_block_solid,source_transaction_id,source_ordinal,duration_millis,expires_at_unix_ms,definition_family,definition_production_key,definition_revision,target_family,target_production_key,target_revision,content_digest,created_xact_id FROM game_spell_field_temporal_schedules WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND source_stage=1",
+    ] {
+        let result = sqlx::query(sql)
+            .bind(corpse.as_slice())
+            .execute(&h.pool)
+            .await;
+        let Err(error) = result else {
+            return Err("immutable corpse schedule accepted mutation".into());
+        };
+        assert_eq!(
+            error
+                .as_database_error()
+                .and_then(|error| error.code())
+                .as_deref(),
+            Some("23514")
+        );
+    }
+    let children: Vec<String> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT item_instance_id::text FROM game_item_corpse_container_entries WHERE parent_item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid ORDER BY item_instance_id")
+        .bind(corpse.as_slice()).fetch_all(&h.pool).await?;
+    assert!(
+        children.len() >= 3,
+        "real Knight guarantee remains in corpse custody"
+    );
+    owner.drain_source_item_deadlines().await;
+    let stages: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_spell_field_temporal_receipts WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid")
+        .bind(corpse.as_slice()).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+    assert_eq!(stages, 0, "no early first transform");
+    owner.drain_corpse_deadlines().await;
+
+    // Translate only the fixture's source clock. Real commit-time clocks,
+    // provenance checks, current-scope fences and custody guards remain active.
+    let mut tx = h.pool.begin().await?;
+    sqlx::query("SET LOCAL session_replication_role=replica")
+        .execute(&mut *tx)
+        .await?;
+    let delta: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT materialized_at-floor(extract(epoch FROM clock_timestamp())*1000)::bigint+1211000 FROM game_item_mint_receipts WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid")
+        .bind(corpse.as_slice()).fetch_one(&mut *tx).await?;
+    sqlx::query("UPDATE game_item_mint_receipts SET materialized_at=materialized_at-$2 WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid")
+        .bind(corpse.as_slice()).bind(delta).execute(&mut *tx).await?;
+    sqlx::query("UPDATE game_spell_field_temporal_schedules SET expires_at_unix_ms=expires_at_unix_ms-$2 WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid")
+        .bind(corpse.as_slice()).bind(delta).execute(&mut *tx).await?;
+    tx.commit().await?;
+    restart_lifecycle(h, "transforms")?;
+    let state: (String, String, i64) = sqlx::query_as("SELECT definition_production_key,state_revision::text,quantity FROM game_item_instances WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid")
+        .bind(corpse.as_slice()).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+    assert_eq!(state, ("oteryn:item.tibia.i52116".into(), "4".into(), 1));
+    let retained: Vec<String> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT item_instance_id::text FROM game_item_corpse_container_entries WHERE parent_item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid ORDER BY item_instance_id")
+        .bind(corpse.as_slice()).fetch_all(&h.pool).await?;
+    assert_eq!(
+        retained, children,
+        "every transform keeps exact child custody"
+    );
+    owner.clock_origin = owner
+        .clock_origin
+        .checked_sub(Duration::from_secs(2))
+        .ok_or("corpse clock")?;
+    owner.drain_corpse_deadlines().await;
+    let retirements: i64 =
+        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_item_decay_retire_receipts")
+            .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+            .await?;
+    assert_eq!(retirements, 0, "final stage retains the source600s window");
+    let projected = owner.runtime.lock().await.visible_corpses();
+    assert_eq!(projected.len(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(projected[0].item.item_instance_id, corpse);
+    assert_eq!(projected[0].item.production_key, "oteryn:item.tibia.i52116");
+    let mut tx = h.pool.begin().await?;
+    sqlx::query("SET LOCAL session_replication_role=replica")
+        .execute(&mut *tx)
+        .await?;
+    sqlx::query("UPDATE game_item_mint_receipts SET materialized_at=materialized_at-601000 WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid")
+        .bind(corpse.as_slice()).execute(&mut *tx).await?;
+    tx.commit().await?;
+    restart_lifecycle(h, "retire")?;
+    owner.clock_origin = owner
+        .clock_origin
+        .checked_sub(Duration::from_secs(2))
+        .ok_or("retirement clock")?;
+    owner.drain_corpse_deadlines().await;
+    assert!(owner.runtime.lock().await.visible_corpses().is_empty());
+    let retired: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_item_decay_retire_receipts")
+        .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+        .await?;
+    assert_eq!(retired, i64::try_from(children.len())? + 1);
+    let stages: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_spell_field_temporal_receipts r JOIN game_spell_field_temporal_audit a USING(transaction_id) WHERE r.item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid")
+        .bind(corpse.as_slice()).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+    assert_eq!(
+        stages, 3,
+        "exactly three durable transforms and audit receipts"
+    );
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+// The process receives only a database name and recovery-store locator. It
+// reconstructs its own root, seal, node proof and lane; no parent timer survives.
+#[test]
+fn newhaven_corruptor_lifecycle_process_restart() -> TestResult {
+    let Ok(database) = std::env::var("NEWHAVEN_RESTART_DATABASE") else {
+        return Ok(());
+    };
+    let parent = std::env::var("NEWHAVEN_RESTART_PARENT")?;
+    let mode = std::env::var("NEWHAVEN_RESTART_MODE")?;
+    super::kill_reward_e2e::scenario_thread::run("newhaven-corpse-restart", move || {
+        run(async |admin| {
+            let prefix = admin
+                .strip_suffix("/postgres")
+                .ok_or("test admin database")?;
+            let url = format!("{prefix}/{database}");
+            let root = crate::durability::DurabilityRoot::connect_test_runtime(&url)?;
+            assert!(root.maintain_ready_once().await?);
+            let retained = std::env::temp_dir()
+                .join(format!("oteryn-revision-sequencer-parent-{parent}"))
+                .join(&database);
+            let recovery = crate::character_recovery_fence::CharacterRecoveryStore::open(
+                retained,
+                "character-primary",
+                "game-ops",
+            )
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+            let seal = recovery.seal_current().map_err(debug)?;
+            let authority = root.open_character_authority(&seal).await.map_err(debug)?;
+            let world = crate::foundation::WorldId::decode(&id(42)).map_err(debug)?;
+            let channel = crate::foundation::ChannelId::decode(&id(43)).map_err(debug)?;
+            let schedule = root
+                .read_corpse_decay_schedule(&authority, world, channel)
+                .await
+                .map_err(debug)?;
+            assert_eq!(schedule.corpses.len(), 1);
+            assert_eq!(
+                schedule.corpses[0].decay_at_unix_ms - schedule.corpses[0].materialized_at_unix_ms,
+                1_810_000
+            );
+            let corpse = schedule.corpses[0].corpse_item_instance_id;
+            let node = super::fixture::registered_node(&root, 1).await?;
+            let permit = crate::durability::spell_owner_commit::SpellLanePermit::of_fresh_lane(
+                world, channel,
+            )
+            .await;
+            if mode == "transforms" {
+                assert_eq!(
+                    root.drain_spell_item_deadlines(
+                        &permit,
+                        &authority,
+                        &node,
+                        crate::foundation::RuntimeScopeRefV1::channel(world, channel),
+                        1
+                    )
+                    .await
+                    .map_err(debug)?,
+                    3
+                );
+                assert_eq!(
+                    root.drain_spell_item_deadlines(
+                        &permit,
+                        &authority,
+                        &node,
+                        crate::foundation::RuntimeScopeRefV1::channel(world, channel),
+                        1
+                    )
+                    .await
+                    .map_err(debug)?,
+                    0
+                );
+            } else {
+                assert_eq!(mode, "retire");
+                root.retire_decayed_corpse(
+                    &permit,
+                    &authority,
+                    &node,
+                    crate::durability::item_decay_retire::CorpseDecayFence {
+                        runtime_scope: crate::foundation::RuntimeScopeRefV1::channel(
+                            world, channel,
+                        ),
+                        scope_ownership_generation:
+                            crate::foundation::ScopeOwnershipGeneration::new(1).map_err(debug)?,
+                    },
+                    corpse,
+                )
+                .await
+                .map_err(debug)?;
+                assert!(
+                    root.read_corpse_decay_schedule(&authority, world, channel)
+                        .await
+                        .map_err(debug)?
+                        .corpses
+                        .is_empty()
+                );
+            }
+            Ok(())
+        })
+    })
+}
+
+fn restart_lifecycle(h: &Harness, mode: &str) -> TestResult {
+    let options = h.pool.connect_options();
+    let database = options.get_database().ok_or("test database")?;
+    let output = std::process::Command::new(std::env::current_exe()?)
+        .args([
+            "newhaven_corruptor_lifecycle_process_restart",
+            "--nocapture",
+            "--test-threads=1",
+        ])
+        .env("NEWHAVEN_RESTART_DATABASE", database)
+        .env("NEWHAVEN_RESTART_PARENT", std::process::id().to_string())
+        .env("NEWHAVEN_RESTART_MODE", mode)
+        .output()?;
+    assert!(
+        output.status.success(),
+        "Corruptor restart {mode}: {} {}",
+        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout),
+        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)
+    );
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+async fn wire_command(
+    owner: &crate::gameplay_transport::ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    admitted: &mut AdmittedSession,
+    command_type: u32,
+    payload: &[u8],
+) -> TestResult<Vec<u8>> {
+    use oteryn_protocol_oteryn as wire;
+    use tokio::io::{AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt};
+    let frame = wire::encode_client_command(
+        1,
+        &wire::ClientCommandValue {
+            command_id: admitted.continuity.next_command_id,
+            command_type,
+            payload,
+        },
+    )
+    .map_err(debug)?;
+    let (mut server, mut client) = tokio::io::duplex(1 << 20);
+    client.write_u32(u32::try_from(frame.len())?).await?;
+    client.write_all(&frame).await?;
+    client.shutdown().await?;
+    let end = crate::gameplay_transport::connection::serve_admitted(
+        &mut server,
+        *admitted,
+        owner,
+        crate::gameplay_transport::connection::IDLE_LIVENESS,
+    )
+    .await;
+    drop(server);
+    let mut output = Vec::new();
+    client.read_to_end(&mut output).await?;
+    let crate::gameplay_transport::connection::ConnectionEnd::AdmittedThenDisconnected(next) = end
+    else {
+        return Err(format!("Newhaven item command connection ended: {end:?}").into());
+    };
+    *admitted = next;
+    let mut cursor = output.as_slice();
+    let mut result = None;
+    while !cursor.is_empty() {
+        let length = usize::try_from(u32::from_be_bytes(
+            cursor.get(..4).ok_or("wire length")?.try_into()?,
+        ))?;
+        let frame = cursor.get(4..4 + length).ok_or("wire frame")?;
+        cursor = &cursor[4 + length..];
+        let envelope = wire::decode_wire_envelope(frame).map_err(debug)?;
+        if envelope.message_type() == wire::MessageType::CommandResult {
+            let reply = wire::decode_command_result(envelope.payload()).map_err(debug)?;
+            assert_eq!(reply.status, wire::CommandStatus::Accepted);
+            assert!(result.is_none(), "one result for one item command");
+            result = Some(reply.payload.to_vec());
+        }
+    }
+    result.ok_or_else(|| "Newhaven item command reply".into())
+}

+//! Production NPC command, atomic acceptance, bound replay and session fence qualification.
+use super::fixture::{Harness, debug};
+use super::{AdmittedSession, ComposedFreshAdmission, TestResult};
+use crate::durability::character_authority::ReconciledCharacterAuthority;
+use crate::durability::quest_state::{QuestTransitionOutcome, npc_program::NpcProgramRequest};
+use crate::foundation::{CommandId, CommandRef};
+use oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::{
+    npc_rules::{NpcRuleCatalog, NpcRuleTrigger},
+    world_callbacks::Position,
+};
+
+const ACCEPT: &str = "oteryn:quest-npc/the_order_of_the_stag/kesar_the_younger_day/writ-offer";
+const KESAR: &str = "oteryn:npc.kesar_the_younger_day";
+const DEMERON: &str = "oteryn:npc.second_in_command_demeron";
+const STAG: &str =
+    "oteryn:quest-progress/crystalserver/quest/u15_20/the_order_of_the_stag/questline";

+#[test]
+fn catalogue_npc_source_acceptance_is_atomic_and_replay_bound() -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() {
+        eprintln!("NPC_PROGRAM_POSTGRES_SKIPPED no_admin_url");
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+    super::kill_reward_e2e::scenario_thread::run("catalogue-npc", || {
+        super::quest_world_callbacks::scenario(
+            "catalogue-npc",
+            Position {
+                x: 33294,
+                y: 31677,
+                floor: -15,
+            },
+            false,
+        )
+    })?;
+    eprintln!(
+        "POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED catalogue_npc_source_acceptance_is_atomic_and_replay_bound {:?}",
+        started.elapsed()
+    );
+    Ok(())
+}

+pub(super) async fn exercise(
+    owner: &mut ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    admitted: &mut AdmittedSession,
+    h: &Harness,
+    authority: &ReconciledCharacterAuthority<'_, '_>,
+) -> TestResult {
+    let source = NpcRuleCatalog::load_embedded().map_err(debug)?;
+    assert_eq!(source.programs().len(), 13);
+    assert_eq!(
+        source
+            .programs()
+            .iter()
+            .map(|p| p.document.rules.len())
+            .sum::<usize>(),
+        1123
+    );
+    let unique = source
+        .programs()
+        .iter()
+        .flat_map(|p| p.document.rules.iter().map(|r| r.npc.as_str()))
+        .collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>();
+    assert_eq!(unique.len(), 78);
+    let [kesar, demeron] =
+        super::classic_quests::install(owner, admitted, [KESAR, DEMERON]).await?;
+    super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, kesar, 2400, "hi").await?;
+    // An unfinished Lion prerequisite cannot create a Stag receipt or source stage.
+    super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, kesar, 2401, "help").await?;
+    let mut fence = owner
+        .current_quest_fence(admitted.game_session_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(|()| "fence")?
+        .ok_or("fence")?;
+    let empty = owner
+        .root
+        .read_character_quest_state(authority, fence.character_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    assert!(!empty.tracks().contains_key(STAG));
+    // Establish the completed prior Lion mission using its existing native owner.
+    // This fixture does not add a route around any missing Stag item hand-in.
+    for (id, key) in [(2410, "npc_3"), (2411, "creature_event_1")] {
+        let mut slot = owner.revision_sequencer.acquire(fence.character_id).await;
+        let outcome=slot.commit_quest_transition(owner.root,authority,owner.holder,fence,
+            crate::durability::quest_state::QuestTransitionRequest {
+                transition_key:format!("oteryn:quest-transition/crystalserver/quest/u12_40/the_order_of_the_lion/kesar_mission/{key}"),
+                cause:crate::durability::quest_state::QuestCause::Command(CommandRef::new(admitted.game_session_id,CommandId::new(id).map_err(debug)?)),
+            },owner.quest_catalogue.as_ref().ok_or("catalogue")?.clone()).await.map_err(debug)?;
+        assert!(matches!(outcome, QuestTransitionOutcome::Committed(_)));
+        drop(slot);
+        fence = owner
+            .current_quest_fence(admitted.game_session_id)
+            .await
+            .map_err(|()| "fence")?
+            .ok_or("fence")?;
+    }
+    owner
+        .refresh_quest_session(admitted.game_session_id, false)
+        .await;
+    // Both the foreign Lion >=5 and Stag <1 predicates are rechecked by the durable owner.
+    let accepted = super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, kesar, 2402, "help").await?;
+    assert!(!accepted.is_empty());
+    fence = owner
+        .current_quest_fence(admitted.game_session_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(|()| "fence")?
+        .ok_or("fence")?;
+    assert_eq!(
+        super::classic_quests::progress(owner, admitted, authority, STAG).await?,
+        1
+    );
+    let request = NpcProgramRequest {
+        command: CommandRef::new(
+            admitted.game_session_id,
+            CommandId::new(2402).map_err(debug)?,
+        ),
+        npc: KESAR.into(),
+        trigger: NpcRuleTrigger::Say,
+        message: "help".into(),
+        topic: 0,
+        transition_key: ACCEPT.into(),
+    };
+    let replay = owner
+        .root
+        .reconcile_npc_program(authority, fence.character_id, request.clone())
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?
+        .ok_or("receipt")?;
+    assert_eq!(replay.reply, accepted);
+    let QuestTransitionOutcome::AlreadyCommitted(receipt) = replay.transition else {
+        return Err("replayed receipt".into());
+    };
+    assert_eq!(receipt.changes.len(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(receipt.changes[0].before, -1);
+    assert_eq!(
+        receipt.committed_character_revision.get(),
+        receipt.original_character_revision.get() + 1
+    );
+    let before = owner
+        .root
+        .read_character_quest_state(authority, fence.character_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    let mut changed = request.clone();
+    changed.message = "help different".into();
+    assert!(matches!(owner.root.reconcile_npc_program(authority,fence.character_id,changed).await,Err(crate::durability::character_progression::CharacterProgressionError::ConflictingOccurrence)));
+    let mut stale = fence;
+    stale.character_lease_generation += 1;
+    let mut fresh = request;
+    fresh.command = CommandRef::new(
+        admitted.game_session_id,
+        CommandId::new(2403).map_err(debug)?,
+    );
+    let mut slot = owner.revision_sequencer.acquire(fence.character_id).await;
+    assert!(matches!(
+        slot.commit_npc_program(
+            owner.root,
+            authority,
+            owner.holder,
+            stale,
+            fresh,
+            owner.quest_catalogue.as_ref().ok_or("catalogue")?.clone()
+        )
+        .await,
+        Err(crate::durability::character_progression::CharacterProgressionError::AuthorityRejected)
+    ));
+    drop(slot);
+    assert_eq!(
+        owner
+            .root
+            .read_character_quest_state(authority, fence.character_id)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?,
+        before
+    );
+    // Demeron's distinct <4 guard reads the same authoritative stage. These
+    // source topic changes do not invent a revision or consume a proof item.
+    super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, demeron, 2404, "hi").await?;
+    assert!(
+        !super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, demeron, 2405, "mission")
+            .await?
+            .is_empty()
+    );
+    super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, demeron, 2406, "tell").await?;
+    for id in 2420..2423 {
+        super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, demeron, id, "yes").await?;
+    }
+    assert_eq!(
+        owner
+            .root
+            .read_character_quest_state(authority, fence.character_id)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?,
+        before
+    );
+    let count: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
+        "SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_quest_receipts WHERE transition_key=$1",
+    )
+    .bind(ACCEPT)
+    .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+    .await?;
+    assert_eq!(count, 1);
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+const FATHER: &str = "oteryn:quest.a_father_s_burden_quest";
+const FATHER_ACCEPT: &str =
+    "oteryn:quest-npc/a_father_s_burden_quest/tereban_functions/mission-accept";
+
+#[test]
+fn catalogue_npc_father_fresh_acceptance_is_one_eleven_track_transaction() -> TestResult {
+    father_case(false)
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn catalogue_npc_father_legacy_hash_preserves_defaults_and_restart_receipts() -> TestResult {
+    father_case(true)
+}
+
+fn father_case(legacy: bool) -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() {
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+    let name = if legacy {
+        "catalogue-npc-legacy"
+    } else {
+        "catalogue-npc-father"
+    };
+    super::kill_reward_e2e::scenario_thread::run(name, move || {
+        super::quest_world_callbacks::scenario(
+            name,
+            Position {
+                x: 33294,
+                y: 31677,
+                floor: -15,
+            },
+            false,
+        )
+    })?;
+    eprintln!("POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED {name} {:?}", started.elapsed());
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+pub(super) async fn exercise_father(
+    owner: &mut ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    admitted: &mut AdmittedSession,
+    h: &Harness,
+    authority: &ReconciledCharacterAuthority<'_, '_>,
+    legacy: bool,
+) -> TestResult {
+    use crate::durability::quest_state::{QuestCause, QuestTransitionRequest};
+    use crate::gameplay_transport::connection::{FreshAdmissionAuthority, UseCommand};
+    use oteryn_protocol_oteryn::npc_service::{NpcIntentDisposition, NpcTalkIntent};
+    let mut fence = owner
+        .current_quest_fence(admitted.game_session_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(|()| "fence")?
+        .ok_or("fence")?;
+    let catalogue = owner.quest_catalogue.as_ref().ok_or("catalogue")?.clone();
+    let old = oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::loader::parse_quest_state(
+        include_str!("../../../../../../content/quests/missions/quest-state.json"),
+        catalogue.content_revision(),
+    )
+    .map_err(debug)?;
+    if legacy {
+        let mut slot = owner.revision_sequencer.acquire(fence.character_id).await;
+        let seeded = slot
+            .commit_quest_transition(
+                owner.root,
+                authority,
+                owner.holder,
+                fence,
+                QuestTransitionRequest {
+                    transition_key:
+                        "oteryn:quest-transition/quest/u8_6/afathers_burden/quest_log/npc_1".into(),
+                    cause: QuestCause::Command(CommandRef::new(
+                        admitted.game_session_id,
+                        CommandId::new(2500).map_err(debug)?,
+                    )),
+                },
+                std::sync::Arc::new(old.catalogue().clone()),
+            )
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        assert!(matches!(seeded, QuestTransitionOutcome::Committed(_)));
+        drop(slot);
+        owner
+            .refresh_quest_session(admitted.game_session_id, false)
+            .await;
+        fence = owner
+            .current_quest_fence(admitted.game_session_id)
+            .await
+            .map_err(|()| "fence")?
+            .ok_or("fence")?;
+    }
+    let before = owner
+        .root
+        .read_character_quest_state(authority, fence.character_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    if legacy {
+        let state = before.states().get(FATHER).ok_or("legacy state")?;
+        assert_eq!(
+            Some(state.definition_hash),
+            old.catalogue().definition_hash(FATHER)
+        );
+        assert!(
+            oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::npc_rules::legacy_defaults(
+                &catalogue,
+                FATHER,
+                &state.definition_hash
+            )
+            .is_some()
+        );
+    }
+    let [tereban] = super::classic_quests::install(owner, admitted, ["oteryn:npc.tereban"]).await?;
+    super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, tereban, 2501, "hi").await?;
+    let offer = super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, tereban, 2502, "mission").await?;
+    assert!(offer.iter().any(|line| line.contains("four sons")));
+    // Force failure after the receipt and earlier tracks were attempted. The real
+    // dispatch must expose no partial tracks, pin, response receipt or revision.
+    sqlx::query("CREATE SEQUENCE fixture_father_failure_seen")
+        .execute(&h.pool)
+        .await?;
+    sqlx::query(
+        "GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON SEQUENCE fixture_father_failure_seen TO oteryn_game_runtime",
+    )
+    .execute(&h.pool)
+    .await?;
+    sqlx::query("CREATE FUNCTION questpc_npc_fail() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$ BEGIN IF NEW.track_key = 'oteryn:quest-progress/quest/u8_6/afathers_burden/wood' THEN PERFORM nextval('fixture_father_failure_seen'); RAISE EXCEPTION 'questpc injected final-track failure'; END IF; RETURN NEW; END $$")
+        .execute(&h.pool).await?;
+    sqlx::query("CREATE TRIGGER questpc_npc_fail BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON game_character_quest_tracks FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION questpc_npc_fail()")
+        .execute(&h.pool).await?;
+    let result = owner
+        .npc_talk(
+            admitted.runtime_actor.ok_or("actor")?,
+            UseCommand {
+                game_session_id: admitted.game_session_id,
+                command_id: 2503,
+                item_fence: Some(fence.item_fence()),
+            },
+            NpcTalkIntent {
+                npc_actor: tereban,
+                text: "yes".into(),
+            },
+        )
+        .await;
+    assert_eq!(result.disposition, NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected);
+    assert!(
+        sqlx::query_scalar::<_, bool>("SELECT is_called FROM fixture_father_failure_seen")
+            .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+            .await?,
+        "the rollback must reach the final material-track write"
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        owner
+            .root
+            .read_character_quest_state(authority, fence.character_id)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?,
+        before
+    );
+    let absent: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
+        "SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_quest_receipts WHERE transition_key=$1",
+    )
+    .bind(FATHER_ACCEPT)
+    .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+    .await?;
+    assert_eq!(absent, 0);
+    sqlx::query("DROP TRIGGER questpc_npc_fail ON game_character_quest_tracks")
+        .execute(&h.pool)
+        .await?;
+    sqlx::query("DROP FUNCTION questpc_npc_fail()")
+        .execute(&h.pool)
+        .await?;
+    sqlx::query("DROP SEQUENCE fixture_father_failure_seen")
+        .execute(&h.pool)
+        .await?;
+    // Resolve a possibly unknown outcome before recreating a conversation.
+    let reconciled = owner
+        .resume_npc_bank(
+            admitted.runtime_actor.ok_or("actor")?,
+            UseCommand {
+                game_session_id: admitted.game_session_id,
+                command_id: 2503,
+                item_fence: Some(fence.item_fence()),
+            },
+        )
+        .await
+        .ok_or("retained Father acceptance")?;
+    assert!(!reconciled.unknown);
+    assert_eq!(reconciled.disposition, NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected);
+    super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, tereban, 2504, "hi").await?;
+    super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, tereban, 2505, "mission").await?;
+    let reply = super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, tereban, 2506, "yes").await?;
+    assert!(reply.iter().any(|line| line.contains("relieved")));
+    let request = NpcProgramRequest {
+        command: CommandRef::new(
+            admitted.game_session_id,
+            CommandId::new(2506).map_err(debug)?,
+        ),
+        npc: "oteryn:npc.tereban".into(),
+        trigger: NpcRuleTrigger::Say,
+        message: "yes".into(),
+        topic: 1,
+        transition_key: FATHER_ACCEPT.into(),
+    };
+    // Clear the session copy and reload it from persistence, then recover the
+    // historical command response without executing a second acceptance.
+    owner.admit_quest_session(admitted).await;
+    let replay = owner
+        .root
+        .reconcile_npc_program(authority, fence.character_id, request.clone())
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?
+        .ok_or("father receipt")?;
+    assert_eq!(reply, replay.reply);
+    let QuestTransitionOutcome::AlreadyCommitted(receipt) = replay.transition else {
+        return Err("father replay".into());
+    };
+    assert_eq!(receipt.changes.len(), 11);
+    assert_eq!(
+        receipt.committed_character_revision.get(),
+        receipt.original_character_revision.get() + 1
+    );
+    for change in &receipt.changes {
+        assert_eq!(
+            change.before,
+            if legacy {
+                if change.track.ends_with("/quest_log") {
+                    1
+                } else {
+                    0
+                }
+            } else {
+                -1
+            }
+        );
+        assert_eq!(
+            change.after,
+            if change.track.ends_with("/progress") {
+                0
+            } else {
+                1
+            }
+        );
+    }
+    if legacy {
+        assert_eq!(
+            Some(receipt.definition_hash),
+            old.catalogue().definition_hash(FATHER)
+        );
+    } else {
+        assert_eq!(
+            Some(receipt.definition_hash),
+            catalogue.definition_hash(FATHER)
+        );
+    }
+    let after = owner
+        .root
+        .read_character_quest_state(authority, fence.character_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    assert_eq!(
+        after
+            .states()
+            .get(FATHER)
+            .ok_or("father state")?
+            .definition_hash,
+        receipt.definition_hash
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        after
+            .tracks()
+            .iter()
+            .filter(|(key, _)| key.starts_with("oteryn:quest-progress/quest/u8_6/afathers_burden/"))
+            .count(),
+        11
+    );
+    let mut changed = request;
+    changed.message = "not yes".into();
+    assert!(matches!(owner.root.reconcile_npc_program(authority, fence.character_id, changed).await,
+        Err(crate::durability::character_progression::CharacterProgressionError::ConflictingOccurrence)));
+    let count: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
+        "SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_quest_receipts WHERE transition_key=$1",
+    )
+    .bind(FATHER_ACCEPT)
+    .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+    .await?;
+    assert_eq!(count, 1);
+    fence = owner
+        .current_quest_fence(admitted.game_session_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(|()| "fence")?
+        .ok_or("fence")?;
+    let mut slot = owner.revision_sequencer.acquire(fence.character_id).await;
+    let native = slot
+        .commit_quest_transition(
+            owner.root,
+            authority,
+            owner.holder,
+            fence,
+            QuestTransitionRequest {
+                transition_key: "oteryn:quest-transition/quest/u8_6/afathers_burden/status/npc_1"
+                    .into(),
+                cause: QuestCause::Command(CommandRef::new(
+                    admitted.game_session_id,
+                    CommandId::new(2507).map_err(debug)?,
+                )),
+            },
+            if legacy {
+                catalogue
+            } else {
+                std::sync::Arc::new(old.catalogue().clone())
+            },
+        )
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    if legacy {
+        // An existing native transition also keeps the exact historical pin.
+        let QuestTransitionOutcome::Committed(native) = native else {
+            return Err("legacy native writer compatibility".into());
+        };
+        assert_eq!(
+            Some(native.definition_hash),
+            old.catalogue().definition_hash(FATHER)
+        );
+        assert_eq!(native.changes[0].before, 1);
+        assert_eq!(native.changes[0].after, 2);
+        let values = owner
+            .root
+            .read_character_quest_state(authority, fence.character_id)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?
+            .tracks()
+            .clone();
+        fence = owner
+            .current_quest_fence(admitted.game_session_id)
+            .await
+            .map_err(|()| "fence")?
+            .ok_or("fence")?;
+        // Reverse compatibility for the historical pin: the original artifact
+        // can still execute an original native transition after the new owner.
+        let reverse = slot
+            .commit_quest_transition(
+                owner.root,
+                authority,
+                owner.holder,
+                fence,
+                QuestTransitionRequest {
+                    transition_key:
+                        "oteryn:quest-transition/quest/u8_6/afathers_burden/quest_log/npc_1".into(),
+                    cause: QuestCause::Command(CommandRef::new(
+                        admitted.game_session_id,
+                        CommandId::new(2508).map_err(debug)?,
+                    )),
+                },
+                std::sync::Arc::new(old.catalogue().clone()),
+            )
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let QuestTransitionOutcome::Committed(reverse) = reverse else {
+            return Err("legacy original artifact compatibility".into());
+        };
+        assert_eq!(
+            Some(reverse.definition_hash),
+            old.catalogue().definition_hash(FATHER)
+        );
+        assert_eq!(
+            owner
+                .root
+                .read_character_quest_state(authority, fence.character_id)
+                .await
+                .map_err(debug)?
+                .tracks(),
+            &values
+        );
+    } else {
+        // The old artifact cannot reinterpret a new pin: incompatible rollback
+        // refuses before a write rather than silently replacing the definition.
+        assert!(matches!(
+            native,
+            QuestTransitionOutcome::Refused(
+                oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::QuestRefusal::RevisionMismatch
+            )
+        ));
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn catalogue_npc_scoped_a_m_starts_are_source_ordered_and_atomic() -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() {
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+    super::kill_reward_e2e::scenario_thread::run("catalogue-npc-scoped", || {
+        super::quest_world_callbacks::scenario(
+            "catalogue-npc-scoped",
+            Position {
+                x: 33294,
+                y: 31677,
+                floor: -15,
+            },
+            false,
+        )
+    })?;
+    eprintln!(
+        "POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED catalogue_npc_scoped_a_m_starts_are_source_ordered_and_atomic {:?}",
+        started.elapsed()
+    );
+    Ok(())
+}

+#[test]
+fn hero_of_rathleton_pipe_greeting_commits_access_once_and_replays() -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() {
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+    super::kill_reward_e2e::scenario_thread::run("catalogue-npc-hero", || {
+        super::quest_world_callbacks::scenario(
+            "catalogue-npc-hero",
+            Position {
+                x: 33294,
+                y: 31677,
+                floor: -15,
+            },
+            false,
+        )
+    })?;
+    eprintln!(
+        "POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED hero_of_rathleton_pipe_greeting_commits_access_once_and_replays {:?}",
+        started.elapsed()
+    );
+    Ok(())
+}

+pub(super) async fn exercise_scoped(
+    owner: &mut ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    admitted: &mut AdmittedSession,
+    h: &Harness,
+    authority: &ReconciledCharacterAuthority<'_, '_>,
+    hero_only: bool,
+) -> TestResult {
+    use crate::gameplay_transport::connection::{FreshAdmissionAuthority, UseCommand};
+    use oteryn_protocol_oteryn::npc_service::{NpcIntentDisposition, NpcTalkIntent};
+    // Static NPC placement is a once-per-Channel operation. Keep every actor
+    // alive while the witness visits the existing source conversations.
+    let [pipe, palimuth, jean, eustacio, xelvar] = super::classic_quests::install(
+        owner,
+        admitted,
+        [
+            "oteryn:npc.strange_pipe",
+            "oteryn:npc.palimuth",
+            "oteryn:npc.jean_pierre",
+            "oteryn:npc.eustacio",
+            "oteryn:npc.xelvar",
+        ],
+    )
+    .await?;
+    let greeting = super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, pipe, 2600, "hi").await?;
+    let source_catalogue = crate::content::NpcDataCatalogue::load(
+        std::path::Path::new(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/../../../content")),
+        std::ffi::OsStr::new("world"),
+        crate::content::npc_catalogue_preproduction_limits(),
+    )
+    .map_err(debug)?;
+    let model = source_catalogue
+        .service_model_for_project()
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    let source_greeting = model
+        .npcs()
+        .iter()
+        .find(|(reference, _)| reference.key == "oteryn:npc.strange_pipe")
+        .and_then(|(_, npc)| npc.bank_dialogue.as_ref())
+        .and_then(|dialogue| dialogue.greet.first());
+    assert_eq!(source_greeting, None, "pipe inherits NpcHandler greeting");
+    assert_eq!(
+        greeting,
+        vec![oteryn_gameplay_npc::quest_greetings::render_player_name(
+            "Greetings, |PLAYERNAME|.",
+            "Quest witness"
+        )]
+    );
+    // Reopen the ordinary conversation, then replay the original source callback
+    // occurrence: its empty reply must not suppress the original handler greeting.
+    super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, pipe, 2613, "bye").await?;
+    assert_eq!(
+        super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, pipe, 2600, "hi").await?,
+        greeting
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        super::classic_quests::progress(
+            owner,
+            admitted,
+            authority,
+            "oteryn:quest-progress/hero_rathleton/access_door"
+        )
+        .await?,
+        1
+    );
+    if hero_only {
+        let receipts: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
+            "SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_quest_receipts WHERE transition_key=$1",
+        )
+        .bind("oteryn:quest-npc/hero_of_rathleton_quest/strange_pipe/greet-access")
+        .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+        .await?;
+        assert_eq!(receipts, 1, "repeated greeting occurrence writes once");
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, palimuth, 2601, "hi").await?;
+    super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, palimuth, 2602, "mission").await?;
+    assert_eq!(
+        super::classic_quests::progress(
+            owner,
+            admitted,
+            authority,
+            "oteryn:quest-progress/quest/u8_4/in_service_of_yalahar/questline"
+        )
+        .await?,
+        3
+    );
+    for (id, word) in [
+        (2603, "hi"),
+        (2604, "cook"),
+        (2605, "recipe"),
+        (2606, "apprentice"),
+        (2607, "yes"),
+    ] {
+        super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, jean, id, word).await?;
+    }
+    for suffix in ["quest_start", "current_dish", "quest_log"] {
+        assert_eq!(
+            super::classic_quests::progress(
+                owner,
+                admitted,
+                authority,
+                &format!("oteryn:quest-progress/quest/u8_5/hot_cuisine_quest/{suffix}")
+            )
+            .await?,
+            1
+        );
+    }
+    let greeting = super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, eustacio, 2608, "hi").await?;
+    assert!(greeting.iter().any(|line| line.contains("ongoing raids")));
+    let name = super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, eustacio, 2609, "name").await?;
+    assert_eq!(name, vec!["I am Eustacio. At your service."]);
+    super::classic_quests::talk(owner, admitted, xelvar, 2610, "hi").await?;
+    let fence = owner
+        .current_quest_fence(admitted.game_session_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(|()| "fence")?
+        .ok_or("fence")?;
+    let before = owner
+        .root
+        .read_character_quest_state(authority, fence.character_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    // Xelvar's source sets a track and grants four crystal items. The storage
+    // owner must refuse the complete branch until its item owner is composed.
+    let refused = owner
+        .npc_talk(
+            admitted.runtime_actor.ok_or("actor")?,
+            UseCommand {
+                game_session_id: admitted.game_session_id,
+                command_id: 2611,
+                item_fence: Some(fence.item_fence()),
+            },
+            NpcTalkIntent {
+                npc_actor: xelvar,
+                text: "adventures".into(),
+            },
+        )
+        .await;
+    assert_eq!(refused.disposition, NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected);
+    assert!(!refused.unknown);
+    assert_eq!(
+        owner
+            .root
+            .read_character_quest_state(authority, fence.character_id)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?,
+        before
+    );
+    let receipts: Vec<(String, i32)> = sqlx::query_as("SELECT quest_key,cardinality(track_keys) FROM game_character_quest_receipts WHERE transition_key LIKE 'oteryn:quest-npc/%' ORDER BY quest_key LIMIT 4")
+        .fetch_all(&h.pool).await?;
+    assert_eq!(
+        receipts,
+        vec![
+            ("oteryn:quest.hero_of_rathleton_quest".into(), 1),
+            ("oteryn:quest.hot_cuisine_quest".into(), 3),
+            ("oteryn:quest.in_service_of_yalahar_quest".into(), 1),
+        ]
+    );
+    Ok(())
+}

+//! Catalogue-wide reward qualification through the production USE and durable owners.
+//! This proves the reward at its source selector, not the quest's access path.
+use super::TestResult;
+use serde_json::Value;
+use std::collections::BTreeSet;
+
+pub(super) type CatalogueActivation = (
+    crate::content::ContentActivationController,
+    crate::content::NativeEntryContentPin,
+);
+
+pub(super) struct CatalogueChest {
+    pub claim: String,
+    pub position: (u16, u16, i8),
+    pub unique: u16,
+    pub servable: bool,
+    pub appearance: u16,
+    pub root: String,
+    pub quantity: u32,
+    pub contents: Vec<(String, i64, i32)>,
+    pub texts: Vec<(String, String)>,
+    pub key_number: Option<i32>,
+    pub achievement: bool,
+    pub charges: Vec<(String, i32)>,
+    pub fluids: Vec<(String, i32)>,
+}
+
+fn string(value: &Value) -> TestResult<String> {
+    Ok(value.as_str().ok_or("catalogue string")?.into())
+}
+
+fn cases() -> TestResult<Vec<CatalogueChest>> {
+    cases_including(|_| false)
+}
+
+fn cases_including(include: impl Fn(&str) -> bool) -> TestResult<Vec<CatalogueChest>> {
+    let coverage: Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
+        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
+        "/../../../docs/repository/QUEST_CALLBACK_COVERAGE.json"
+    )))?;
+    let mut supported: BTreeSet<_> = coverage["quests"]
+        .as_array()
+        .ok_or("coverage quests")?
+        .iter()
+        .flat_map(|quest| quest["chests"].as_array().into_iter().flatten())
+        .filter(|chest| chest["status"] != "not_playable")
+        .map(|chest| string(&chest["claim"]))
+        .collect::<TestResult<_>>()?;
+    // Random repeat sources have their own complete production USE scenarios;
+    // the generic once-only recipe harness cannot represent their cycle owner.
+    for chest in oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::falcon::CHESTS {
+        assert!(
+            supported.remove(&chest.key()),
+            "Falcon source needs cycle coverage"
+        );
+    }
+    let mut found = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut served = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut cases = Vec::new();
+    for shard in crate::content::world_reward_claims::REWARD_CLAIM_SHARDS {
+        let shard: Value = serde_json::from_str(shard)?;
+        for row in shard["records"].as_array().ok_or("claim records")? {
+            let definition = &row["definition"];
+            let claim = string(&definition["identity"]["key"])?;
+            // A new source slice must reach the production binder before its
+            // coverage report can claim qualification. The broad sweep still
+            // requires every previously supported claim exactly as before.
+            if include(&claim) {
+                supported.insert(claim.clone());
+            }
+            if !supported.contains(&claim) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            assert!(found.insert(claim.clone()), "duplicate claim {claim}");
+            for (index, placement) in definition["placements"]
+                .as_array()
+                .ok_or("claim placements")?
+                .iter()
+                .enumerate()
+            {
+                let binding = &placement["source_binding"];
+                let position = &binding["project_position"];
+                let ids = binding["legacy_unique_ids"]
+                    .as_array()
+                    .ok_or("source unique ids")?;
+                let crystal = ids
+                    .iter()
+                    .find(|source| source["server"] == "crystalserver");
+                let unique = crystal.or_else(|| ids.first()).ok_or("source selector")?["unique_id"]
+                    .as_u64()
+                    .ok_or("source unique id")?;
+                if crystal.is_some() {
+                    served.insert(claim.clone());
+                }
+                let reward = &placement["reward"];
+                let native = definition["native_contents"]["placements"].get(index);
+                let (root, quantity, contents) = if let Some(native) = native {
+                    let children = native["contents"]
+                        .as_array()
+                        .ok_or("native contents")?
+                        .iter()
+                        .map(|child| {
+                            Ok((
+                                string(&child["item"]["key"])?,
+                                child["count"].as_i64().ok_or("child count")?,
+                                i32::try_from(child["key_number"].as_i64().unwrap_or(0))?,
+                            ))
+                        })
+                        .collect::<TestResult<Vec<_>>>()?;
+                    (string(&native["root"]["key"])?, 1, children)
+                } else {
+                    let items = reward["items"].as_array().ok_or("leaf items")?;
+                    assert_eq!(items.len(), 1, "unsupported leaf shape {claim}");
+                    (
+                        string(&items[0]["item"]["key"])?,
+                        u32::try_from(items[0]["count"].as_u64().ok_or("leaf count")?)?,
+                        Vec::new(),
+                    )
+                };
+                let mut texts = Vec::new();
+                if let Some(native) = native {
+                    if let Some(text) = native["root_text"].as_str() {
+                        texts.push((root.clone(), text.into()));
+                    }
+                    for child in native["contents"].as_array().ok_or("native contents")? {
+                        if let Some(text) = child["text"].as_str() {
+                            texts.push((string(&child["item"]["key"])?, text.into()));
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                let key_number = reward["key_binding"]
+                    .as_str()
+                    .filter(|_| reward.get("container").is_none())
+                    .map(|binding| -> TestResult<i32> {
+                        Ok(binding
+                            .rsplit('/')
+                            .next()
+                            .ok_or("key binding")?
+                            .parse::<i32>()?)
+                    })
+                    .transpose()?;
+                cases.push(CatalogueChest {
+                    claim: claim.clone(),
+                    position: (
+                        u16::try_from(position["x"].as_u64().ok_or("source x")?)?,
+                        u16::try_from(position["y"].as_u64().ok_or("source y")?)?,
+                        -i8::try_from(position["z"].as_u64().ok_or("source z")?)?,
+                    ),
+                    unique: u16::try_from(unique)?,
+                    servable: crystal.is_some(),
+                    appearance: u16::try_from(
+                        placement["appearance_tibia_id"]
+                            .as_u64()
+                            .ok_or("source appearance")?,
+                    )?,
+                    root,
+                    quantity,
+                    contents,
+                    texts,
+                    key_number,
+                    achievement: native.is_some_and(|native| !native["achievement"].is_null()),
+                    charges: expected_charges(&claim),
+                    fluids: expected_fluids(&claim),
+                });
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    assert_eq!(found, supported, "every supported claim needs a scenario");
+    assert_eq!(
+        served, supported,
+        "every supported claim needs a native-source placement"
+    );
+    Ok(cases)
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn quest_catalogue_chests_every_supported_source_placement_use_replay_and_refusal() -> TestResult {
+    execute(cases()?)?;
+    super::falcon_chests::falcon_chests_postgres_real_use_frozen_draw_refusal_restart_and_fences()
+}
+
