+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_0.shardsof_ancient_winters.formorgar_mines",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_0.shardsof_ancient_winters.helheim",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_0.shardsof_ancient_winters.inukaya",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_0.shardsof_ancient_winters.okolnir",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_1.koshei_the_deathless.koshei_amulet_part1",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_1.koshei_the_deathless.koshei_amulet_part2",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_1.koshei_the_deathless.koshei_amulet_part3",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_1.koshei_the_deathless.koshei_amulet_part4",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_1.koshei_the_deathless.koshei_the_deathless_gold",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_1.koshei_the_deathless.koshei_the_deathless_legs",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_1.koshei_the_deathless.koshei_the_deathless_platinum",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_5.darashia_dragon",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_5.lone_medusa",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_54.children_of_the_revolution.chest_tome_of_knowledge1",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_54.children_of_the_revolution.chest_tome_of_knowledge2",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_54.children_of_the_revolution.flask_of_extra_greasy_oil",
+        "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u8_6.an_interest_in_botany.botany_almanach",
+    ]
+    .into_iter()
+    .collect();
+    let coverage: Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
+        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
+        "/../../../docs/repository/QUEST_CALLBACK_COVERAGE.json"
+    )))?;
+    // Lane ownership comes from the coverage quest title, including its leading
+    // article. Claim slugs can describe an unrelated item or donor storage key.
+    let owned_claims: BTreeSet<_> = coverage["quests"]
+        .as_array()
+        .ok_or("coverage quests")?
+        .iter()
+        .filter(|quest| {
+            quest["quest"]
+                .as_str()
+                .and_then(|title| title.rsplit('/').next())
+                .and_then(|title| title.bytes().next())
+                .is_some_and(|initial| (b'a'..=b'm').contains(&initial))
+        })
+        .flat_map(|quest| quest["chests"].as_array().into_iter().flatten())
+        .filter(|chest| chest["status"] != "not_playable")
+        .map(|chest| string(&chest["claim"]))
+        .collect::<TestResult<_>>()?;
+    assert!(
+        claims.iter().all(|claim| owned_claims.contains(*claim)),
+        "every frozen A..M claim must retain supported title ownership"
+    );
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases()?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| claims.contains(case.claim.as_str()))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected
+            .iter()
+            .map(|case| case.claim.as_str())
+            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>(),
+        claims,
+        "every A..M run-8 claim needs every source placement exercised"
+    );
+    execute(selected)
+}
+
+// Small cohorts use the same production dispatch scenario as the catalogue
+// sweep. Keep every placement and every already-qualified sibling so an early
+// checkpoint describes a whole reward set, rather than a partial failed sweep.
+// Access puzzles, NPC exchanges and outfits remain separate qualification work.
+fn phase_a_cohort(quests: &[&str]) -> TestResult {
+    let coverage: Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
+        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
+        "/../../../docs/repository/QUEST_CALLBACK_COVERAGE.json"
+    )))?;
+    let mut found = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut claims = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut placements = 0;
+    for quest in coverage["quests"].as_array().ok_or("coverage quests")? {
+        let title = quest["quest"]
+            .as_str()
+            .ok_or("quest title")?
+            .rsplit('/')
+            .next()
+            .ok_or("quest slug")?;
+        if !quests.contains(&title) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        assert!(found.insert(title), "duplicate quest {title}");
+        for chest in quest["chests"].as_array().ok_or("quest chests")? {
+            assert_ne!(chest["status"], "not_playable", "held reward in {title}");
+            assert!(
+                claims.insert(string(&chest["claim"])?),
+                "duplicate cohort claim"
+            );
+            placements += chest["placements"]
+                .as_array()
+                .ok_or("coverage placements")?
+                .len();
+        }
+    }
+    assert_eq!(
+        found,
+        quests.iter().copied().collect(),
+        "every cohort quest must be authored"
+    );
+    assert!(!claims.is_empty(), "a cohort must exercise actual rewards");
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases()?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| claims.contains(&case.claim))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected.len(),
+        placements,
+        "every source placement must execute"
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected
+            .iter()
+            .map(|case| &case.claim)
+            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>(),
+        claims.iter().collect(),
+        "every reward must execute"
+    );
+    execute(selected)
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn quest_catalogue_chests_phase_a_01_use_replay_and_refusal() -> TestResult {
+    phase_a_cohort(&[
+        "adorned_uh_rune_quest",

+fn quest_catalogue_chests_mintwallin_prison_key3620_use_replay_and_refusal() -> TestResult {
+    // TibiaWiki Key 3620 identifies the Mintwallin barracks drawer; the donor's
+    // inherited Black Knight section label is not this source's quest identity.
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases()?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| case.claim == "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.key.id3620")
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(selected.len(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(selected[0].position, (32411, 32155, -15));
+    assert_eq!(selected[0].unique, 5009);
+    assert_eq!(selected[0].appearance, 2434);
+    assert_eq!(selected[0].root, "oteryn:item.tibia.i2969");
+    assert_eq!(selected[0].key_number, Some(3620));
+    execute(selected)
+}

+fn expected_charges(claim: &str) -> Vec<(String, i32)> {
+    let ids: &[u16] = match claim.strip_prefix("oteryn:reward-claim.") {
+        Some("kv.deeperfibula4") => &[3082],
+        Some("quest.u7_2.the_queen_of_the_banshees.reward.stone_skin_amulet") => &[3081],
+        Some("quest.u15_24.targuna.silver_amulet_chest") => &[3054],
+        Some("quest.pre_u6_0.ornamented_shield.rewards.ornamented_shield") => &[3048, 3085],
+        Some("kv.ornamentedshield") => &[3083],
+        _ => &[],
+    };
+    ids.iter()
+        .map(|id| (format!("oteryn:item.tibia.i{id}"), 1))
+        .collect()
+}
+
+fn expected_fluids(claim: &str) -> Vec<(String, i32)> {
+    match claim.strip_prefix("oteryn:reward-claim.") {
+        Some("kv.goblintemple2") => vec![("oteryn:item.tibia.i2874".into(), 9)],
+        Some("quest.u7_9.dreamers_challenge.chests_wine") => {
+            vec![("oteryn:item.tibia.i2874".into(), 2)]
+        }
+        Some("kv.present_box") => vec![
+            ("oteryn:item.tibia.i2881".into(), 1),
+            ("oteryn:item.tibia.i2882".into(), 1),
+        ],
+        _ => Vec::new(),
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn quest_catalogue_chests_exact_partial_charges_use_replay_and_refusal() -> TestResult {
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases_including(|claim| !expected_charges(claim).is_empty())?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| !case.charges.is_empty())
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected.len(),
+        5,
+        "all five exact partial-charge source claims need USE proof"
+    );
+    execute(selected)
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn quest_catalogue_chests_exact_fluid_subtypes_use_replay_and_refusal() -> TestResult {
+    let selected: Vec<_> = cases_including(|claim| !expected_fluids(claim).is_empty())?
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter(|case| !case.fluids.is_empty())
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        selected.len(),
+        6,
+        "milk, four wine bags and water present need USE proof"
+    );
+    execute(selected)
+}

+pub(super) async fn assert_charges(
+    pool: &sqlx::PgPool,
+    root: [u8; 16],
+    chest: &CatalogueChest,
+) -> TestResult {
+    let actual: Vec<(String, i32, i64, bool, bool, bool)> = sqlx::query_as(
+        "SELECT i.definition_production_key,t.charges,t.state_revision::bigint, \
+            t.remaining_ms IS NULL,t.deadline_at IS NULL,t.xmin=r.xmin \
+         FROM game_item_timed_states t JOIN game_item_instances i USING(item_instance_id) \
+         JOIN game_reward_claim_mint_receipts r ON r.item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid \
+         WHERE i.item_instance_id=r.item_instance_id OR i.item_instance_id IN \
+            (SELECT item_instance_id FROM game_item_nested_entries WHERE parent_item_instance_id=r.item_instance_id) \
+         ORDER BY i.definition_production_key",
+    )
+    .bind(root.as_slice())
+    .fetch_all(pool)
+    .await?;
+    let expected: Vec<_> = chest
+        .charges
+        .iter()
+        .map(|(key, count)| (key.clone(), *count, 1, true, true, true))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        actual, expected,
+        "exact source charge state, minted atomically and stable on replay"
+    );
+    let (reserved, committed): (Value, Value) = sqlx::query_as(
+        "SELECT v.charges_plan,r.charges_plan FROM game_reward_claim_mint_receipts r \
+         JOIN game_reward_claim_mint_reservations v USING(game_session_id,command_id) \
+         WHERE r.item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+    )
+    .bind(root.as_slice())
+    .fetch_one(pool)
+    .await?;
+    let mut expected_plan = Vec::new();
+    for (key, charges) in &chest.charges {
+        let ordinal = if *key == chest.root {
+            0
+        } else {
+            chest
+                .contents
+                .iter()
+                .position(|(child, _, _)| child == key)
+                .ok_or("charged child absent")?
+                + 1
+        };
+        expected_plan.push(serde_json::json!({"ordinal": ordinal, "charges_remaining": charges}));
+    }
+    expected_plan.sort_by_key(|value| value["ordinal"].as_u64());
+    assert_eq!(committed, Value::Array(expected_plan));
+    assert_eq!(
+        reserved, committed,
+        "reservation binds the exact complete charge plan"
+    );
+    if !chest.charges.is_empty() {
+        let shape: (bool, bool, bool, bool, bool) = sqlx::query_as(
+            "SELECT game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(r.charges_plan,r.contents_plan), \
+                game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(r.charges_plan#-'{0,charges_remaining}',r.contents_plan), \
+                game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(jsonb_set(r.charges_plan,'{0,charges_remaining}','2'),r.contents_plan), \
+                game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(jsonb_set(r.charges_plan,'{0,ordinal}','-1'),r.contents_plan), \
+                game_reward_claim_charges_plan_valid(r.charges_plan||r.charges_plan,r.contents_plan) \
+             FROM game_reward_claim_mint_receipts r WHERE r.item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+        )
+        .bind(root.as_slice())
+        .fetch_one(pool)
+        .await?;
+        assert_eq!(shape, (true, false, false, false, false));
+        for mutation in [
+            "UPDATE game_reward_claim_mint_reservations SET charges_plan='[]'::jsonb WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+            "UPDATE game_item_timed_states SET charges=2,state_revision=state_revision+1 WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid OR item_instance_id IN (SELECT item_instance_id FROM game_item_nested_entries WHERE parent_item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid)",
+        ] {
+            let changed = sqlx::query(mutation)
+                .bind(root.as_slice())
+                .execute(pool)
+                .await;
+            assert!(
+                matches!(changed, Err(sqlx::Error::Database(ref error)) if error.code().as_deref() == Some("23514")),
+                "charge initialization never permits later plan substitution or unfenced inflation"
+            );
+        }
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+/// The binder admits a claim only when every source placement is present.
+/// Preserve all sibling selectors even while exercising one clicked chest.
+pub(super) fn catalogue_source_world(
+    chest: &CatalogueChest,
+) -> TestResult<crate::map::boot::BundleWorld> {
+    use oteryn_world_bundle::{bundle, sector};
+    use std::collections::BTreeMap;
+    let mut placements = Vec::new();
+    for shard in crate::content::world_reward_claims::REWARD_CLAIM_SHARDS {
+        let shard: Value = serde_json::from_str(shard)?;
+        for row in shard["records"].as_array().ok_or("claim records")? {
+            let definition = &row["definition"];
+            if definition["identity"]["key"] != chest.claim {
+                continue;
+            }
+            for source in definition["placements"]
+                .as_array()
+                .ok_or("claim placements")?
+            {
+                let binding = &source["source_binding"];
+                let unique = binding["legacy_unique_ids"]
+                    .as_array()
+                    .ok_or("source ids")?
+                    .iter()
+                    .find(|id| id["server"] == "crystalserver")
+                    .ok_or("native source")?;
+                let position = &binding["project_position"];
+                placements.push((
+                    u16::try_from(position["x"].as_u64().ok_or("source x")?)?,
+                    u16::try_from(position["y"].as_u64().ok_or("source y")?)?,
+                    -i8::try_from(position["z"].as_u64().ok_or("source z")?)?,
+                    u16::try_from(unique["unique_id"].as_u64().ok_or("source uid")?)?,
+                    u16::try_from(
+                        source["appearance_tibia_id"]
+                            .as_u64()
+                            .ok_or("source appearance")?,
+                    )?,
+                ));
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    assert!(
+        !placements.is_empty(),
+        "every catalogue claim has placed sources"
+    );
+    let (bytes, mut pins) = crate::map::boot::tests::bundle();
+    let mut data = bundle::read(&bytes)?;
+    data.manifest.palette.truncate(1);
+    let mut appearances = BTreeMap::new();
+    let mut tiles = BTreeMap::<(i8, u16, u16), sector::Tile>::new();
+    for (x, y, floor, unique, appearance) in placements {
+        let palette = if let Some(index) = appearances.get(&appearance) {
+            *index
+        } else {
+            let index = u32::try_from(data.manifest.palette.len())?;
+            data.manifest.palette.push(bundle::PaletteEntry {
+                key: format!("oteryn:terrain.tibia.i{appearance}"),
+                family: bundle::Family::Terrain,
+                id: index,
+                terrain: Some(bundle::Terrain {
+                    kind: bundle::TerrainKind::Common,
+                    walkable: None,
+                    ground_speed: None,
+                }),
+            });
+            appearances.insert(appearance, index);
+            index
+        };
+        for tile_x in x.saturating_sub(2)..=x {
+            tiles
+                .entry((floor, y, tile_x))
+                .or_insert_with(|| sector::Tile {
+                    x: tile_x,
+                    y,
+                    flags: 0,
+                    house: 0,
+                    zones: Vec::new(),
+                    items: vec![sector::Item {
+                        palette: 0,
+                        depth: 0,
+                        attrs: sector::Attrs::default(),
+                    }],
+                });
+        }
+        tiles
+            .get_mut(&(floor, y, x))
+            .ok_or("source tile")?
+            .items
+            .push(sector::Item {
+                palette,
+                depth: 0,
+                attrs: sector::Attrs {
+                    unique: Some(unique),
+                    ..Default::default()
+                },
+            });
+    }
+    let coordinates: Vec<_> = tiles.keys().copied().collect();
+    data.manifest.world = bundle::Extent {
+        min_x: coordinates
+            .iter()
+            .map(|(_, _, x)| *x)
+            .min()
+            .ok_or("source minimum x")?,
+        min_y: coordinates
+            .iter()
+            .map(|(_, y, _)| *y)
+            .min()
+            .ok_or("source minimum y")?,
+        max_x: coordinates
+            .iter()
+            .map(|(_, _, x)| *x)
+            .max()
+            .ok_or("source maximum x")?
+            + 1,
+        max_y: coordinates
+            .iter()
+            .map(|(_, y, _)| *y)
+            .max()
+            .ok_or("source maximum y")?
+            + 1,
+        floors: coordinates
+            .iter()
+            .map(|(floor, _, _)| *floor)
+            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()
+            .into_iter()
+            .collect(),
+    };
+    let mut grouped = BTreeMap::<(i8, u16, u16), Vec<sector::Tile>>::new();
+    for ((floor, y, x), tile) in tiles {
+        grouped
+            .entry((floor, x / 32, y / 32))
+            .or_default()
+            .push(tile);
+    }
+    let sectors: Vec<_> = grouped
+        .into_iter()
+        .map(|((floor, sx, sy), tiles)| bundle::Sector {
+            floor,
+            sx,
+            sy,
+            tiles,
+        })
+        .collect();
+    let bytes = oteryn_world_bundle::writer::write(&data.manifest, &sectors, &Default::default())?;
+    pins.digest = bundle::read(&bytes)?.digest;
+    let base = crate::map::load(&bytes, &pins).map_err(super::fixture::debug)?;
+    crate::map::boot::boot(
+        &bytes,
+        &crate::map::boot::BootPins {
+            map_revision: crate::map::overlay::map_revision(&base),
+            bundle: pins,
+            start: crate::map::overlay::TilePos {
+                x: chest.position.0 - 1,
+                y: chest.position.1,
+                floor: chest.position.2,
+            },
+        },
+        crate::foundation::WorldId::decode(&super::fixture::id(42))
+            .map_err(super::fixture::debug)?,
+        crate::foundation::ChannelId::decode(&super::fixture::id(43))
+            .map_err(super::fixture::debug)?,
+        |_| None,
+    )
+    .map_err(super::fixture::debug)
+}
+
+fn execute(cases: Vec<CatalogueChest>) -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() {
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    std::thread::Builder::new()
+        .stack_size(256 << 20)
+        .spawn(move || -> Result<(), String> {
+            // Every case has an isolated durable world/session, using the same
+            // immutable production content generation. Qualify that content once;
+            // no mutable character, command or receipt state is shared.
+            let world = crate::foundation::WorldId::decode(&super::fixture::id(42))
+                .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
+            let activation =
+                super::death_ml_loss_composed::production_generation_with_pin(world, "content-1")
+                    .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
+            let generation = activation.0.active().ok_or("active generation")?;
+            let native = generation.native_gameplay().ok_or("native generation")?;
+            for case in cases {
+                for key in std::iter::once(case.root.as_str())
+                    .chain(case.contents.iter().map(|(key, _, _)| key.as_str()))
+                {
+                    let definition = crate::durability::item_mint::TypedDefinitionRef {
+                        family: "Item".into(), production_key: key.into(), revision_ref: "definition-r1".into(),
+                    };
+                    let facts = crate::gameplay_transport::item_ref_admission::generation_item_facts(native, &definition)
+                        .map_err(|error| format!("{} {key}: {error:?}", case.claim))?;
+                    if matches!(facts.stack, crate::durability::item_transfer::ItemStackClass::Unknown) {
+                        return Err(format!("{} {key}: native Item admission missing", case.claim));
+                    }
+                }
+                let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+                super::quest_container_chests::catalogue_scenario(&case, &activation)
+                    .map_err(|error| format!("{} {:?}: {error}", case.claim, case.position))?;
+                println!(
+                    "QUEST_CATALOGUE_CHEST POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED {} {:?} native_source={} duration={:?}",
+                    case.claim,
+                    case.position,
+                    case.servable,
+                    started.elapsed()
+                );
+            }
+            Ok(())
+        })?
+        .join()
+        .map_err(|_| "catalogue chest scenario panicked")??;
+    Ok(())
+}

+//! Literal source USE callbacks must select the clicked object above ground.
+use super::*;
+
+#[test]
+fn quest_world_callbacks_literal_use_accepts_clicked_nonzero_ordinal() -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() { return Ok(()); }
+    super::super::kill_reward_e2e::scenario_thread::run("literal-callback-use", || {
+        for (name, appearance, ordinal, source, destination) in [
+            ("blue-valley-2", 49948, 3, (33605,31468,-7), (32456,32291,-7)),
+            ("blue-valley-3", 49948, 3, (33601,31468,-7), (32456,32291,-7)),
+            ("blue-valley-4", 49948, 1, (32456,32290,-7), (33614,31494,-7)),
+            ("blue-valley-5", 50310, 2, (32203,32303,-6), (33614,31494,-7)),
+            ("pirates-tail-cheesy-exit", 1760, 1, (33925,31355,-7), (33799,31321,-7)),
+            ("order-of-the-stag-boat-return", 6391, 2, (33874,32250,-7), (32395,32511,-7)),
+            ("order-of-the-stag-crypt-exit", 52656, 1, (32402,32472,-5), (32402,32473,-4)),
+        ] {
+            let callback = callback(name)?;
+            assert_eq!(callback.selector.position, Some(position(source)));
+            assert_eq!(callback.teleport, Some(position(destination)));
+            exercise(callback, appearance, ordinal, None)?;
+        }
+        Ok(())
+    })
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn quest_world_callbacks_read_only_stag_uses_gate_without_quest_writes() -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() { return Ok(()); }
+    super::super::kill_reward_e2e::scenario_thread::run("read-only-stag-use", || {
+        for (name, appearance, ordinal, minimum, source, destination) in [
+            ("order-of-the-stag-boat-to-ada", 6391, 4, 3, (32393,32513,-7), (33875,32249,-7)),
+            ("order-of-the-stag-mark-wall", 52735, 1, 11, (33834,32139,-4), (33833,32138,-4)),
+        ] {
+            let callback = callback(name)?;
+            assert_eq!(callback.selector.position, Some(position(source)));
+            assert_eq!(callback.teleport, Some(position(destination)));
+            assert!(callback.is_read_only());
+            assert_eq!(callback.storage[0].minimum, minimum);
+            for stage in [None, Some(minimum - 1), Some(minimum), Some(13)] {
+                exercise(callback.clone(), appearance, ordinal, stage)?;
+            }
+        }
+        // The production map contains the pre-startup wall too; position-based
+        // server dispatch must not depend on the donor's visual transform.
+        exercise(callback("order-of-the-stag-crypt-exit")?, 1507, 1, None)?;
+        Ok(())
+    })
+}
+
+fn position((x,y,floor): (u16,u16,i8)) -> Position { Position { x,y,floor } }
+
+fn exercise(callback: Callback, appearance: u16, ordinal: u8, stage: Option<i64>) -> TestResult {
+    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+    let trigger = callback.selector.position.ok_or("position")?;
+    let world = world_ordinal(&callback, trigger, appearance, ordinal)?;
+    run(async |admin| {
+        let h = Harness::create_with_admission_seed(admin, "quest_world_callbacks", false, false)
+            .await?;
+        prepare_current_session(&h).await?;
+        let seal = h.recovery.seal_current().map_err(debug)?;
+        let authority = h
+            .root
+            .open_character_authority(&seal)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let mut deps = Dependencies::new()?;
+        let (controller, activated) =
+            super::super::death_ml_loss_composed::production_generation_with_pin(
+                deps.admitted.world_id,
+                "content-1",
+            )?;
+        let generation = controller.active().ok_or("generation")?;
+        let cells = world
+            .movement_cells(activated.qualified_room().movement_cells())
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let pin = ChannelContentPin::from_activation(
+            deps.admitted.world_id,
+            1,
+            generation.identity().server_artifact_digest(),
+            generation.identity().client_artifact_digest(),
+            activated.frame_binding().digest(),
+            world.map_revision_digest(),
+            (
+                i32::from(trigger.x) - 1,
+                i32::from(trigger.y),
+                -i16::from(trigger.floor),
+            ),
+        );
+        let mut runtime = ChannelRuntimeV1::from_committed_assignment(
+            deps.admitted.world_id,
+            deps.admitted.channel_id,
+            crate::foundation::NodeId::decode(&id(1)).map_err(debug)?,
+            1,
+            1,
+            1,
+            "runtime-scope-assignment:1",
+            2,
+            pin,
+        )
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+        let reservation = runtime
+            .reserve_fresh_session(deps.admitted.game_session_id)
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        deps.actor = runtime.commit_fresh_session(reservation).map_err(debug)?;
+        let origin = local(Position {
+            x: trigger.x - 1,
+            ..trigger
+        });
+        runtime
+            .initialize_pinned_test_position(deps.actor, origin)
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        deps.runtime = tokio::sync::Mutex::new(runtime);
+        let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+        owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+        owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+        owner.quest_catalogue = Some(std::sync::Arc::new(
+            oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::loader::load_embedded_quest_state("content-1")
+                .map_err(debug)?
+                .catalogue()
+                .clone(),
+        ));
+        {
+            assert!(
+                owner
+                    .states
+                    .quest_callbacks
+                    .fixture
+                    .set(vec![callback.clone()])
+                    .is_ok()
+            );
+        }
+        let mut admitted = deps.admitted;
+        admitted.first_entry = FirstEntryOutcome::Positioned;
+        let fence = owner
+            .current_quest_fence(admitted.game_session_id)
+            .await
+            .map_err(|()| "fence")?
+            .ok_or("fence")?;
+        admitted.item_fence = Some(fence.item_fence());
+        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_control_scope_grants VALUES (session_user, encode($1,'hex')::uuid, encode($2,'hex')::uuid, 4)")
+            .bind(id(42).as_slice()).bind(id(43).as_slice()).execute(&h.pool).await?;
+        sqlx::query("SELECT game_content_record_activation(encode($1,'hex')::uuid, encode($2,'hex')::uuid, 1, NULL, $3, $4, $5)")
+            .bind(id(42).as_slice()).bind(id(43).as_slice()).bind(generation.identity().server_artifact_digest().as_slice())
+            .bind(generation.identity().client_artifact_digest().as_slice()).bind(activated.frame_binding().digest().as_slice()).execute(&h.pool).await?;
+        let fresh = super::super::fresh_fixture::fresh::Source::new(0)?.request()?;
+        h.root
+            .initialize_admission_equipment(
+                &authority,
+                &h.node,
+                &fence.item_fence(),
+                generation.identity().server_artifact_digest(),
+                fresh.operation(),
+            )
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let native = generation.native_gameplay().ok_or("native")?;
+        let template = crate::durability::starter_grant::StarterTemplate {
+            definition: crate::durability::item_mint::TypedDefinitionRef {
+                family: "Item".into(),
+                production_key: "oteryn:item.tibia.i2854".into(),
+                revision_ref: "definition-r1".into(),
+            },
+            ..crate::durability::starter_grant::StarterTemplate::main()
+        };
+        h.root
+            .grant_starter_items(
+                &authority,
+                &h.node,
+                &fence.item_fence(),
+                &crate::gameplay_transport::item_ref_admission::NativeItemWeights(native),
+                &template,
+            )
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+        deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+        owner.admit_quest_session(&admitted).await;
+
+        owner.states.chat.admit(admitted.game_session_id, "Traveller", owner.owner_now());
+        if let Some(stage) = stage {
+            use oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::{QuestComparison, QuestEffect, QuestEffectKind, QuestStateCatalogue, QuestTrack, QuestTransition};
+            let storage = callback.storage.first().ok_or("guard storage")?;
+            let seed = "oteryn:quest-transition/fixture/stag-stage";
+            let catalogue = std::sync::Arc::new(QuestStateCatalogue::new("content-1", vec![QuestTrack {
+                key: storage.track.clone(), quest: callback.quest.clone(), initial: -1, min: -1, max: 13,
+            }], vec![QuestTransition {
+                key: seed.into(), quest: callback.quest.clone(), effects: vec![QuestEffect {
+                    track: storage.track.clone(), from: QuestComparison::Between(-1, 13), effect: QuestEffectKind::Set(stage),
+                }], completes: false, experience: None,
+            }]).map_err(debug)?);
+            let mut slot = owner.revision_sequencer.acquire(fence.character_id).await;
+            let seeded = slot.commit_quest_transition(owner.root, owner.character, owner.holder, fence,
+                crate::durability::quest_state::QuestTransitionRequest {
+                    transition_key: seed.into(), cause: crate::durability::quest_state::QuestCause::Command(
+                        crate::foundation::CommandRef::new(admitted.game_session_id, crate::foundation::CommandId::new(900).map_err(debug)?)),
+                }, catalogue.clone()).await.map_err(debug)?;
+            assert!(matches!(seeded, crate::durability::quest_state::QuestTransitionOutcome::Committed(_)));
+            drop(slot);
+            owner.quest_catalogue = Some(catalogue);
+        }
+        let key = oteryn_world_bundle::bundle::placement_key(trigger.floor, trigger.x, trigger.y, ordinal).ok_or("key")?;
+        let mut placement = owner.bundle_map().ok_or("map")?.overlay.base().digest().to_vec();
+        placement.extend_from_slice(&key.to_be_bytes());
+        let target = WorldObjectTarget { placement, expected_revision: 0 };
+        let before = owner.root.read_character_quest_state(&authority, fence.character_id).await.map_err(debug)?;
+        let receipts_before: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_quest_receipts").fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+        let allowed = callback.storage.first().is_none_or(|storage| stage.is_some_and(|stage| stage >= storage.minimum));
+        let reply = wire(&owner, &mut admitted, 1, 2,
+            oteryn_protocol_oteryn::world_object::encode_use_intent(&target).map_err(debug)?).await?;
+        assert_eq!(oteryn_protocol_oteryn::world_object::decode_use_result(&reply).map_err(debug)?, if allowed { UseDisposition::Committed } else { UseDisposition::Rejected });
+        assert_eq!(owner.runtime.lock().await.read_actor_position(deps.actor).map_err(debug)?.position(),
+            if allowed { local(callback.teleport.ok_or("destination")?) } else { origin });
+        let messages = owner.states.chat.drain(admitted.game_session_id);
+        let expected_message = if allowed { &callback.success_message } else { &callback.failure_message };
+        if let Some(expected_message) = expected_message {
+            assert_eq!(messages.iter().filter(|line| matches!(line,
+                oteryn_protocol_oteryn::chat::ChatLine::Private {text, ..} if text == expected_message)).count(), 1);
+        }
+        let after = owner.root.read_character_quest_state(&authority, fence.character_id).await.map_err(debug)?;
+        assert_eq!(before, after, "literal USE changed durable quest state");
+        let receipts: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_quest_receipts").fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+        assert_eq!(receipts, receipts_before);
+        eprintln!("QUEST_CALLBACK_LITERAL_USE_POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED {} stage={stage:?} allowed={allowed} tracks_unchanged receipts_unchanged revision_unchanged duration={:?}", callback.key, started.elapsed());
+        drop(owner);
+        drop(authority);
+        drop(seal);
+        h.cleanup().await
+    })
+}

+//! Lost Wisdom drives real command-7 dispatch and reloads its atomic item/XP receipt.
+use super::fixture::{Harness, debug, id, run};
+use super::{Dependencies, TestResult, prepare_current_session};
+use crate::content::channel_content_pin::ChannelContentPin;
+use crate::foundation::{ChannelRuntimeV1, MovementLocalPosition};
+use crate::gameplay_transport::connection::FreshAdmissionAuthority;
+
+#[test]
+fn viola_mission_yes_book_handin_is_atomic_and_survives_restart() -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() {
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+    super::kill_reward_e2e::scenario_thread::run("viola-handin", || scenario(false))?;
+    eprintln!(
+        "POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED viola_mission_yes_book_handin_is_atomic_and_survives_restart {:?}",
+        started.elapsed()
+    );
+    Ok(())
+}
+#[test]
+fn viola_mission_yes_nested_book_refuses_full_bag_without_consuming() -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() {
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+    super::kill_reward_e2e::scenario_thread::run("viola-nested", || scenario(true))?;
+    eprintln!(
+        "POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED viola_mission_yes_nested_book_refuses_full_bag_without_consuming duration={:?}",
+        started.elapsed()
+    );
+    Ok(())
+}
+fn scenario(nested: bool) -> TestResult {
+    run(async |admin| {
+        let h = Harness::create_with_admission_seed(admin, "viola_handin", false, false).await?;
+        prepare_current_session(&h).await?;
+        let seal = h.recovery.seal_current().map_err(debug)?;
+        let authority = h
+            .root
+            .open_character_authority(&seal)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let mut deps = Dependencies::new()?;
+        let (controller, activated) =
+            super::death_ml_loss_composed::production_generation_with_pin(
+                deps.admitted.world_id,
+                "content-1",
+            )?;
+        let generation = controller.active().ok_or("generation")?;
+        let native = generation.native_gameplay().ok_or("native")?;
+        let world = super::viola_book::source_world()?;
+        let cells = world
+            .movement_cells(activated.qualified_room().movement_cells())
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let pin = ChannelContentPin::from_activation(
+            deps.admitted.world_id,
+            1,
+            generation.identity().server_artifact_digest(),
+            generation.identity().client_artifact_digest(),
+            activated.frame_binding().digest(),
+            world.map_revision_digest(),
+            (32570, 32507, 7),
+        );
+        let mut runtime = ChannelRuntimeV1::from_committed_assignment(
+            deps.admitted.world_id,
+            deps.admitted.channel_id,
+            crate::foundation::NodeId::decode(&id(1)).map_err(debug)?,
+            1,
+            1,
+            1,
+            "runtime-scope-assignment:1",
+            2,
+            pin,
+        )
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+        let reservation = runtime
+            .reserve_fresh_session(deps.admitted.game_session_id)
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        deps.actor = runtime.commit_fresh_session(reservation).map_err(debug)?;
+        runtime
+            .initialize_pinned_test_position(
+                deps.actor,
+                MovementLocalPosition {
+                    x: 32570,
+                    y: 32507,
+                    floor: 7,
+                },
+            )
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        deps.runtime = tokio::sync::Mutex::new(runtime);
+        let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+        owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+        owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+        let mut admitted = deps.admitted;
+        admitted.first_entry = crate::gameplay_transport::connection::FirstEntryOutcome::Positioned;
+        let fence = owner
+            .current_quest_fence(admitted.game_session_id)
+            .await
+            .map_err(|()| "fence")?
+            .ok_or("fence")?;
+        admitted.item_fence = Some(fence.item_fence());
+        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_control_scope_grants VALUES(session_user,encode($1,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid,4)").bind(id(42).as_slice()).bind(id(43).as_slice()).execute(&h.pool).await?;
+        sqlx::query("SELECT game_content_record_activation(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid,1,NULL,$3,$4,$5)").bind(id(42).as_slice()).bind(id(43).as_slice()).bind(generation.identity().server_artifact_digest().as_slice()).bind(generation.identity().client_artifact_digest().as_slice()).bind(activated.frame_binding().digest().as_slice()).execute(&h.pool).await?;
+        let fresh = super::fresh_fixture::fresh::Source::new(0)?.request()?;
+        h.root
+            .initialize_admission_equipment(
+                &authority,
+                &h.node,
+                &fence.item_fence(),
+                generation.identity().server_artifact_digest(),
+                fresh.operation(),
+            )
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let template = crate::durability::starter_grant::StarterTemplate {
+            definition: crate::durability::item_mint::TypedDefinitionRef {
+                family: "Item".into(),
+                production_key: "oteryn:item.tibia.i2854".into(),
+                revision_ref: "definition-r1".into(),
+            },
+            ..crate::durability::starter_grant::StarterTemplate::main()
+        };
+        h.root
+            .grant_starter_items(
+                &authority,
+                &h.node,
+                &fence.item_fence(),
+                &crate::gameplay_transport::item_ref_admission::NativeItemWeights(native),
+                &template,
+            )
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+        deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+        let npc = install_source(&owner, &admitted).await?;
+        let say = super::classic_quests::talk;
+        say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 900, "hi").await?;
+        {
+            // Contended spell state must refuse without suspending with the
+            // runtime locked or reserving any part of a Viola transaction.
+            let states = owner.states.spell_states.lock().await;
+            let refused = tokio::time::timeout(
+                std::time::Duration::from_secs(1),
+                owner.npc_talk(
+                    deps.actor,
+                    crate::gameplay_transport::connection::UseCommand {
+                        game_session_id: admitted.game_session_id,
+                        command_id: 9,
+                        item_fence: Some(fence.item_fence()),
+                    },
+                    oteryn_protocol_oteryn::npc_service::NpcTalkIntent {
+                        npc_actor: npc,
+                        text: "mission".into(),
+                    },
+                ),
+            )
+            .await
+            .map_err(|_| "Viola suspended while holding the runtime lock")?;
+            assert_eq!(
+                refused.disposition,
+                oteryn_protocol_oteryn::npc_service::NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected
+            );
+            assert!(!refused.unknown);
+            drop(states);
+            let receipts: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_viola_receipts")
+                .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+                .await?;
+            assert_eq!(receipts, 0, "contended state creates no transaction");
+        }
+        let mut stale = fence.item_fence();
+        stale.character_lease_generation += 1;
+        let refused = owner
+            .npc_talk(
+                deps.actor,
+                crate::gameplay_transport::connection::UseCommand {
+                    game_session_id: admitted.game_session_id,
+                    command_id: 8,
+                    item_fence: Some(stale),
+                },
+                oteryn_protocol_oteryn::npc_service::NpcTalkIntent {
+                    npc_actor: npc,
+                    text: "mission".into(),
+                },
+            )
+            .await;
+        assert_eq!(
+            refused.disposition,
+            oteryn_protocol_oteryn::npc_service::NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected
+        );
+
+        assert!(
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 10, "yes")
+                .await?
+                .join(" ")
+                .contains("first")
+        );
+        assert!(
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 11, "mission")
+                .await?
+                .join(" ")
+                .contains("Say yes")
+        );
+        assert!(
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 12, "no")
+                .await?
+                .join(" ")
+                .contains("change")
+        );
+        assert!(
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 13, "yes")
+                .await?
+                .join(" ")
+                .contains("first")
+        );
+        say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 14, "mission").await?;
+        say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 15, "yes").await?;
+        assert!(
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 16, "mission")
+                .await?
+                .join(" ")
+                .contains("Did you find")
+        );
+        assert!(
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 17, "yes")
+                .await?
+                .join(" ")
+                .contains("Have you found")
+        );
+        let before:i64=sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT total_experience FROM game_character_progression_state WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid").bind(id(41).as_slice()).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+        relocate(&owner, deps.actor, 32613, 32419).await?;
+        let target = super::viola_book::target(&world)?;
+        assert_eq!(
+            super::quest_callbacks::wire_uses(&owner, admitted, &[1], &target).await?,
+            [oteryn_protocol_oteryn::world_object::UseDisposition::Committed]
+        );
+        if nested {
+            super::quest_world_callbacks::input_item(
+                &owner,
+                fence.item_fence(),
+                &oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::world_callbacks::Offering {
+                    item: "oteryn:item.tibia.i2854".into(),
+                    count: 1,
+                    position: oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::world_callbacks::Position {
+                        x: 32613,
+                        y: 32419,
+                        floor: -7,
+                    },
+                },
+                70,
+                true,
+            )
+            .await?;
+            let (book,bag):(Vec<u8>,Vec<u8>)=sqlx::query_as("SELECT uuid_send(i.item_instance_id),uuid_send(c.item_instance_id) FROM game_item_instances i CROSS JOIN game_item_instances c JOIN game_item_container_entries e ON e.item_instance_id=c.item_instance_id WHERE i.lifecycle=1 AND i.definition_production_key='oteryn:item.tibia.i51977' AND c.lifecycle=1 AND c.definition_production_key='oteryn:item.tibia.i2854'").fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+            let permit = owner.spell_lane_permit().await.ok_or("permit")?;
+            let moved = h
+                .root
+                .commit_item_loop_move(
+                    &permit,
+                    &authority,
+                    &h.node,
+                    &fence.item_fence(),
+                    reference(admitted.game_session_id, 1800)?,
+                    &crate::gameplay_transport::item_ref_admission::NativeItemWeights(native),
+                    crate::durability::item_loop_move::LoopMoveRequest {
+                        item: book.try_into().map_err(|_| "book id")?,
+                        count: None,
+                        pickup_cell: None,
+                        destination: crate::durability::item_loop_move::LoopDestination::Container(
+                            bag.try_into().map_err(|_| "bag id")?,
+                        ),
+                    },
+                )
+                .await
+                .map_err(debug)?;
+            assert!(moved.is_ok(), "nested book: {moved:?}");
+            drop(permit);
+            for index in 0..19 {
+                burden(&owner, fence.item_fence(), index).await?;
+            }
+        }
+        relocate(&owner, deps.actor, 32570, 32507).await?;
+        say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 101, "hi").await?;
+        say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 102, "mission").await?;
+        let completion_id = if nested {
+            assert!(
+                say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 103, "yes")
+                    .await?
+                    .join(" ")
+                    .contains("make room")
+            );
+            assert_unfinished(&h, before).await?;
+            relocate(&owner, deps.actor, 32613, 32419).await?;
+            super::viola_book::free_one(&owner, fence.item_fence(), 1900).await?;
+            relocate(&owner, deps.actor, 32570, 32507).await?;
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 104, "hi").await?;
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 105, "mission").await?;
+            106
+        } else {
+            // Sequence advancement survives the rollback and proves the deferred
+            // completion trigger actually ran, rather than an earlier unknown gate.
+            sqlx::query("CREATE SEQUENCE fixture_viola_failure_seen")
+                .execute(&h.pool)
+                .await?;
+            sqlx::query(
+                "GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON SEQUENCE fixture_viola_failure_seen TO oteryn_game_runtime",
+            )
+            .execute(&h.pool)
+            .await?;
+            sqlx::query("CREATE FUNCTION fixture_viola_failure() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$ BEGIN IF NEW.reply=3 THEN PERFORM nextval('fixture_viola_failure_seen'); RAISE EXCEPTION 'fixture atomic handoff rollback' USING ERRCODE='23514'; END IF; RETURN NULL; END $$").execute(&h.pool).await?;
+            sqlx::query("CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER fixture_viola_failure AFTER INSERT ON game_viola_receipts DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION fixture_viola_failure()").execute(&h.pool).await?;
+            owner.clock_origin = owner
+                .clock_origin
+                .checked_sub(std::time::Duration::from_secs(2))
+                .ok_or("clock")?;
+            let failed = owner
+                .npc_talk(
+                    deps.actor,
+                    crate::gameplay_transport::connection::UseCommand {
+                        game_session_id: admitted.game_session_id,
+                        command_id: 103,
+                        item_fence: Some(fence.item_fence()),
+                    },
+                    oteryn_protocol_oteryn::npc_service::NpcTalkIntent {
+                        npc_actor: npc,
+                        text: "yes".into(),
+                    },
+                )
+                .await;
+            assert!(failed.unknown, "deferred COMMIT failure is unknown");
+            assert!(
+                sqlx::query_scalar::<_, bool>("SELECT is_called FROM fixture_viola_failure_seen")
+                    .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+                    .await?,
+                "the rollback must reach the deferred completion trigger"
+            );
+            assert_unfinished(&h, before).await?;
+            sqlx::query("DROP TRIGGER fixture_viola_failure ON game_viola_receipts")
+                .execute(&h.pool)
+                .await?;
+            sqlx::query("DROP FUNCTION fixture_viola_failure()")
+                .execute(&h.pool)
+                .await?;
+            sqlx::query("DROP SEQUENCE fixture_viola_failure_seen")
+                .execute(&h.pool)
+                .await?;
+            owner.clock_origin = owner
+                .clock_origin
+                .checked_sub(std::time::Duration::from_secs(2))
+                .ok_or("clock")?;
+            let reconciled = owner
+                .resume_npc_bank(
+                    deps.actor,
+                    crate::gameplay_transport::connection::UseCommand {
+                        game_session_id: admitted.game_session_id,
+                        command_id: 103,
+                        item_fence: Some(fence.item_fence()),
+                    },
+                )
+                .await
+                .ok_or("retained Viola handoff")?;
+            assert!(!reconciled.unknown);
+            assert_eq!(
+                reconciled.disposition,
+                oteryn_protocol_oteryn::npc_service::NpcIntentDisposition::Rejected
+            );
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 104, "mission").await?;
+            105
+        };
+        assert!(
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, completion_id, "yes")
+                .await?
+                .join(" ")
+                .contains("800 experience")
+        );
+        let after:i64=sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT total_experience FROM game_character_progression_state WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid").bind(id(41).as_slice()).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+        assert_eq!(after - before, 800);
+        let counts:(i64,i64)=sqlx::query_as("SELECT count(*) FILTER(WHERE definition_production_key='oteryn:item.tibia.i51977'),count(*) FILTER(WHERE definition_production_key='oteryn:item.tibia.i3572') FROM game_item_instances WHERE lifecycle=1").fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+        assert_eq!(counts, (0, 1));
+        drop(owner);
+        let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+        owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+        owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+        owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+        deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+        let npc = install_source(&owner, &admitted).await?;
+        say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 2001, "hi").await?;
+        assert!(
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 2002, "mission")
+                .await?
+                .join(" ")
+                .contains("already")
+        );
+        let reference = crate::foundation::CommandRef::new(
+            admitted.game_session_id,
+            crate::foundation::CommandId::new(completion_id).map_err(debug)?,
+        );
+        assert_eq!(
+            h.root
+                .reconcile_viola(&authority, fence.character_id, reference)
+                .await
+                .map_err(debug)?,
+            Some(crate::durability::tutorial_viola::ViolaReply::Completed)
+        );
+        assert_eq!(
+            sqlx::query_scalar::<_, i64>("SELECT count(*) FROM game_viola_receipts WHERE reply=3")
+                .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+                .await?,
+            1
+        );
+        assert!(
+            say(&mut owner, &admitted, npc, 2003, "spells")
+                .await?
+                .join(" ")
+                .contains("retired")
+        );
+        Ok(())
+    })
+}
+
+fn reference(
+    session: crate::foundation::GameSessionId,
+    id: u64,
+) -> TestResult<crate::foundation::CommandRef> {
+    Ok(crate::foundation::CommandRef::new(
+        session,
+        crate::foundation::CommandId::new(id).map_err(debug)?,
+    ))
+}
+async fn relocate(
+    owner: &crate::gameplay_transport::ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    actor: crate::foundation::ExactActorRef,
+    x: i32,
+    y: i32,
+) -> TestResult {
+    let mut runtime = owner.runtime.lock().await;
+    let mut current = runtime.read_actor_position(actor).map_err(debug)?;
+    assert_eq!(current.position().floor, 7);
+    // The fixture visits two source cells; initialization is admission-only.
+    // Keep the actor, active pin and exact position revision through each step.
+    while current.position().x != x || current.position().y != y {
+        let mut next = current.position();
+        if next.x != x {
+            next.x += (x - next.x).signum();
+        } else {
+            next.y += (y - next.y).signum();
+        }
+        current = runtime
+            .borrow_movement_position()
+            .commit_cardinal(current, next)
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+    }
+    assert_eq!(current.position(), MovementLocalPosition { x, y, floor: 7 });
+    Ok(())
+}
+async fn install_source(
+    owner: &crate::gameplay_transport::ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    admitted: &crate::gameplay_transport::connection::AdmittedSession,
+) -> TestResult<oteryn_protocol_oteryn::world_spatial_entities::EntityRef> {
+    use crate::foundation::{MovementFacing, NpcPlacement, NpcPlacementTable, NpcPlacements};
+    let catalogue = crate::content::NpcDataCatalogue::load(
+        std::path::Path::new(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/../../../content")),
+        std::ffi::OsStr::new("world"),
+        crate::content::npc_catalogue_preproduction_limits(),
+    )
+    .map_err(debug)?;
+    let model = catalogue.service_model_for_project().map_err(debug)?;
+    owner.states.chat.install_npc_talk(std::sync::Arc::new(
+        crate::gameplay_transport::npc_talk::NpcTalkBook::from_model_with_native_quests(
+            &model,
+            &["oteryn:npc.viola"],
+        ),
+    ));
+    owner.states.chat.admit(
+        admitted.game_session_id,
+        "Lost Wisdom witness",
+        owner.owner_now(),
+    );
+    owner.states.chat.set_npc_service(admitted.game_session_id);
+    let document: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(concat!(
+        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
+        "/../../../content/world/npc-placements/placements-00000-00033.json"
+    ))?)?;
+    let declaration = document["records"]
+        .as_array()
+        .ok_or("placements")?
+        .iter()
+        .find_map(|row| {
+            let d = &row["declaration"];
+            (d["npc"].as_str() == Some("oteryn:npc.viola")).then_some(d)
+        })
+        .ok_or("authored Viola placement")?;
+    assert_eq!(
+        declaration["cell"],
+        serde_json::json!({"x":32571,"y":32507,"floor":7})
+    );
+    assert_eq!(declaration["direction"], "south");
+    let fence = owner
+        .current_quest_fence(admitted.game_session_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(|()| "fence")?
+        .ok_or("fence")?;
+    let actor = admitted.runtime_actor.ok_or("actor")?;
+    let mut runtime = owner.runtime.lock().await;
+    runtime
+        .bind_attacker_lease(
+            actor,
+            admitted.game_session_id,
+            crate::foundation::CharacterLease::new(
+                crate::foundation::CharacterId::decode(fence.character_id.as_bytes())
+                    .map_err(debug)?,
+                fence.character_lease_generation,
+            )
+            .map_err(debug)?,
+        )
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    if !runtime
+        .visible_entities()
+        .npcs
+        .iter()
+        .any(|npc| runtime.npc_key(npc.actor) == Some("oteryn:npc.viola"))
+    {
+        runtime
+            .place_npcs(
+                &NpcPlacementTable {
+                    npcs: vec![NpcPlacements {
+                        key: "oteryn:npc.viola".into(),
+                        placements: vec![NpcPlacement {
+                            x: 32571,
+                            y: 32507,
+                            floor: -7,
+                            direction: MovementFacing::South,
+                        }],
+                    }],
+                },
+                |_| true,
+                true,
+            )
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+    }
+    let npc = runtime
+        .visible_entities()
+        .npcs
+        .into_iter()
+        .find(|npc| runtime.npc_key(npc.actor) == Some("oteryn:npc.viola"))
+        .ok_or("source NPC")?;
+    assert_eq!(
+        (npc.position.x, npc.position.y, npc.position.floor),
+        (32571, 32507, 7)
+    );
+    Ok(oteryn_protocol_oteryn::world_spatial_entities::EntityRef {
+        identity: npc.actor.placement_identity(),
+        generation: npc.generation,
+    })
+}
+async fn assert_unfinished(h: &Harness, before: i64) -> TestResult {
+    let (xp,stage,completed,crafts,books,scarves):(i64,i16,i64,i64,i64,i64)=sqlx::query_as("SELECT (SELECT total_experience FROM game_character_progression_state WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid),(SELECT stage FROM game_character_viola WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid),(SELECT count(*) FROM game_viola_receipts WHERE reply=3),(SELECT count(*) FROM game_item_craft_records WHERE recipe='oteryn:npc-handoff.viola'),(SELECT count(*) FROM game_item_instances WHERE lifecycle=1 AND definition_production_key='oteryn:item.tibia.i51977'),(SELECT count(*) FROM game_item_instances WHERE lifecycle=1 AND definition_production_key='oteryn:item.tibia.i3572')").bind(id(41).as_slice()).fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+    assert_eq!(
+        (xp, stage, completed, crafts, books, scarves),
+        (before, 1, 0, 0, 1, 0)
+    );
+    Ok(())
+}
+async fn burden(
+    owner: &crate::gameplay_transport::ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    fence: crate::durability::item_transfer::CurrentCharacterItemFence,
+    index: u64,
+) -> TestResult {
+    use crate::durability::{
+        item_mint::TypedDefinitionRef, reward_claim_mint::RewardClaimMintRequest,
+    };
+    use crate::gameplay_transport::item_ref_admission::generation_item_facts;
+    let native = owner
+        .active_generation
+        .and_then(crate::content::ActiveGeneration::native_gameplay)
+        .ok_or("native")?;
+    let definition = |id| TypedDefinitionRef {
+        family: "Item".into(),
+        production_key: format!("oteryn:item.tibia.i{id}"),
+        revision_ref: "definition-r1".into(),
+    };
+    let request = RewardClaimMintRequest {
+        command: reference(fence.game_session_id, 200 + index)?,
+        claim: TypedDefinitionRef {
+            family: "RewardClaim".into(),
+            production_key: format!("fixture:viola-handoff-burden.{index}"),
+            revision_ref: "definition-r1".into(),
+        },
+        source_placement: "fixture:viola-handoff-burden".into(),
+        item: generation_item_facts(native, &definition(3031)).map_err(debug)?,
+        quantity: 1,
+        contents: Vec::new(),
+        root_text: None,
+        root_fluid_subtype: None,
+        root_charges_remaining: None,
+        backpack: generation_item_facts(native, &definition(2854)).map_err(debug)?,
+        content_revision: "content-1".into(),
+        ruleset_revision: "ruleset-1".into(),
+        sim_revision: "sim-1".into(),
+        achievement: None,
+        quest_transition: None,
+        key_number: None,
+    };
+    let mut candidate = owner
+        .root
+        .freeze_reward_claim_mint(owner.character, owner.holder, fence, request)
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    owner
+        .root
+        .commit_reward_claim_mint(owner.character, owner.holder, fence, &mut candidate)
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    Ok(())
+}

+//! Real Book of Wisdom command-2 acquisition, durable repeat and replay.
+use super::fixture::{Harness, debug, id, run};
+use super::quest_callbacks::wire_uses;
+use super::{Dependencies, TestResult, prepare_current_session};
+use crate::content::channel_content_pin::ChannelContentPin;
+use crate::durability::item_mint::TypedDefinitionRef;

+use crate::gameplay_transport::connection::FirstEntryOutcome;

+use oteryn_protocol_oteryn::world_object::{UseDisposition, WorldObjectTarget};
