+#[test]
+fn viola_book_postgres_real_use_repeat_replay_refusal_and_fences() -> TestResult {
+    if std::env::var_os("OTERYN_TEST_POSTGRES_ADMIN_URL").is_none() {
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+    super::kill_reward_e2e::scenario_thread::run("viola-book", scenario)?;
+    eprintln!(
+        "POSTGRES_CASE_COMPLETED viola_book_postgres_real_use_repeat_replay_refusal_and_fences duration={:?}",
+        started.elapsed()
+    );
+    Ok(())
+}
+fn definition(id: u16) -> TypedDefinitionRef {
+    TypedDefinitionRef {
+        family: "Item".into(),
+        production_key: format!("oteryn:item.tibia.i{id}"),
+        revision_ref: "definition-r1".into(),
+    }
+}
+fn scenario() -> TestResult {
+    run(async |admin| {
+        let h = Harness::create_with_admission_seed(admin, "viola_book", false, false).await?;
+        prepare_current_session(&h).await?;
+        let seal = h.recovery.seal_current().map_err(debug)?;
+        let authority = h
+            .root
+            .open_character_authority(&seal)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let mut deps = Dependencies::new()?;
+        let (controller, activated_pin) =
+            super::death_ml_loss_composed::production_generation_with_pin(
+                deps.admitted.world_id,
+                "content-1",
+            )?;
+        let generation = controller.active().ok_or("active generation")?;
+        let native = generation.native_gameplay().ok_or("native generation")?;
+        generation_item_facts(native, &definition(51977)).map_err(debug)?;
+        let world = source_world()?;
+        let cells = world
+            .movement_cells(deps.room.movement_cells())
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let pin = ChannelContentPin::from_activation(
+            deps.admitted.world_id,
+            1,
+            generation.identity().server_artifact_digest(),
+            generation.identity().client_artifact_digest(),
+            activated_pin.frame_binding().digest(),
+            world.map_revision_digest(),
+            (32613, 32419, 7),
+        );
+        let mut runtime = ChannelRuntimeV1::from_committed_assignment(
+            deps.admitted.world_id,
+            deps.admitted.channel_id,
+            crate::foundation::NodeId::decode(&id(1)).map_err(debug)?,
+            1,
+            1,
+            1,
+            "runtime-scope-assignment:1",
+            2,
+            pin,
+        )
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+        let reservation = runtime
+            .reserve_fresh_session(deps.admitted.game_session_id)
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        deps.actor = runtime.commit_fresh_session(reservation).map_err(debug)?;
+        runtime
+            .initialize_pinned_test_position(
+                deps.actor,
+                MovementLocalPosition {
+                    x: 32613,
+                    y: 32419,
+                    floor: 7,
+                },
+            )
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        deps.runtime = tokio::sync::Mutex::new(runtime);
+        let current =
+            crate::durability::fresh_admission::FreshAdmissionStore::from_root(h.root.clone())
+                .current_session_at(deps.admitted.game_session_id)
+                .await?
+                .0;
+        let fence = crate::gameplay_transport::item_fence_of(current).ok_or("item fence")?;
+        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_control_scope_grants VALUES(session_user,encode($1,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid,4)").bind(id(42).as_slice()).bind(id(43).as_slice()).execute(&h.pool).await?;
+        sqlx::query("SELECT game_content_record_activation(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid,1,NULL,$3,$4,$5)")
+            .bind(id(42).as_slice()).bind(id(43).as_slice()).bind(generation.identity().server_artifact_digest().as_slice())
+            .bind(generation.identity().client_artifact_digest().as_slice()).bind(activated_pin.frame_binding().digest().as_slice()).execute(&h.pool).await?;
+        let weights = NativeItemWeights(native);
+        let fresh = super::fresh_fixture::fresh::Source::new(0)?.request()?;
+        h.root
+            .initialize_admission_equipment(
+                &authority,
+                &h.node,
+                &fence,
+                generation.identity().server_artifact_digest(),
+                fresh.operation(),
+            )
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let template = crate::durability::starter_grant::StarterTemplate {
+            definition: definition(2854),
+            ..crate::durability::starter_grant::StarterTemplate::main()
+        };
+        h.root
+            .grant_starter_items(&authority, &h.node, &fence, &weights, &template)
+            .await
+            .map_err(debug)?;
+        let mut admitted = deps.admitted;
+        admitted.first_entry = FirstEntryOutcome::Positioned;
+        admitted.item_fence = Some(fence);
+        let target = target(&world)?;
+        // Every negative case keeps unrelated identity, content and custody valid.
+        {
+            let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+            owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+            owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+            owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+            deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+            let mut stale = admitted;
+            let mut stale_fence = fence;
+            stale_fence.character_lease_generation += 1;
+            stale.item_fence = Some(stale_fence);
+            assert_eq!(
+                wire_uses(&owner, stale, &[1], &target).await?,
+                [UseDisposition::Rejected]
+            );
+        }
+        // A reconstructed owner has a fresh ingress beginning at command 1.
+        // Each cold stream sends contiguous IDs; durable refusals wrote no mint cause.
+        {
+            let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+            owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+            owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+            owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+            deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+            let mut wrong = target.clone();
+            let key =
+                oteryn_world_bundle::bundle::placement_key(-7, 32614, 32419, 1).ok_or("key")?;
+            wrong.placement[32..].copy_from_slice(&key.to_be_bytes());
+            assert_eq!(
+                wire_uses(&owner, admitted, &[1], &wrong).await?,
+                [UseDisposition::NothingToUse]
+            );
+            let count: i64 =
+                sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_viola_book_cycles")
+                    .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+                    .await?;
+            assert_eq!(count, 0);
+        }
+        // No backpack room: refusal must not reserve a cycle or begin the timer.
+        for index in 0..20 {
+            let request = RewardClaimMintRequest {
+                command: CommandRef::new(
+                    admitted.game_session_id,
+                    CommandId::new(100 + index).map_err(debug)?,
+                ),
+                claim: TypedDefinitionRef {
+                    family: "RewardClaim".into(),
+                    production_key: format!("fixture:viola-book-burden.{index}"),
+                    revision_ref: "definition-r1".into(),
+                },
+                source_placement: "fixture:viola-book-burden".into(),
+                item: generation_item_facts(native, &definition(3031)).map_err(debug)?,
+                quantity: 1,
+                contents: Vec::new(),
+                root_text: None,
+                root_fluid_subtype: None,
+                root_charges_remaining: None,
+                backpack: generation_item_facts(native, &template.definition).map_err(debug)?,
+                content_revision: "content-1".into(),
+                ruleset_revision: "ruleset-1".into(),
+                sim_revision: "sim-1".into(),
+                achievement: None,
+                quest_transition: None,
+                key_number: None,
+            };
+            let mut candidate = h
+                .root
+                .freeze_reward_claim_mint(&authority, &h.node, fence, request)
+                .await
+                .map_err(debug)?;
+            h.root
+                .commit_reward_claim_mint(&authority, &h.node, fence, &mut candidate)
+                .await
+                .map_err(debug)?;
+        }
+        {
+            let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+            owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+            owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+            owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+            deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+            assert_eq!(
+                wire_uses(&owner, admitted, &[1], &target).await?,
+                [UseDisposition::Rejected]
+            );
+            let count: i64 =
+                sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_viola_book_cycles")
+                    .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+                    .await?;
+            assert_eq!(count, 0);
+            let reservations:i64=sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM game_reward_claim_mint_reservations WHERE claim_production_key LIKE 'oteryn:reward-claim.tutorial.viola-book:%'").fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+            assert_eq!(reservations, 0, "refusal cannot reserve an internal cycle");
+            free_one(&owner, fence, 500).await?;
+        }
+        for _ in 0..2 {
+            let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+            owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+            owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+            owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+            deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+            assert_eq!(
+                wire_uses(&owner, admitted, &[1, 1, 2], &target).await?,
+                [
+                    UseDisposition::Committed,
+                    UseDisposition::Committed,
+                    UseDisposition::NothingToUse
+                ]
+            );
+        }
+        let claimed: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
+            "SELECT claimed_at FROM game_character_viola_book_cycles WHERE cycle=1",
+        )
+        .fetch_one(&h.pool)
+        .await?;
+        for (now, expected) in [
+            (claimed + 299_999, UseDisposition::NothingToUse),
+            (claimed + 300_000, UseDisposition::Committed),
+        ] {
+            sqlx::query(sqlx::AssertSqlSafe(format!("CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION game_viola_book_now_ms() RETURNS BIGINT LANGUAGE SQL VOLATILE AS 'SELECT {now}::bigint'"))).execute(&h.pool).await?;
+            let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+            owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+            owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+            owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+            deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+            if expected == UseDisposition::Committed {
+                free_one(&owner, fence, 501).await?;
+            }
+            assert_eq!(
+                wire_uses(&owner, admitted, &[1, 2], &target).await?,
+                [UseDisposition::Committed, expected]
+            );
+        }
+        {
+            let mut owner = deps.owner(&h, &authority);
+            owner.active_generation = Some(generation);
+            owner.movement_cells = &cells;
+            owner.admit_progression_session(&mut admitted).await;
+            deps.initialize(&owner).await;
+            assert_eq!(
+                wire_uses(&owner, admitted, &[1, 2], &target).await?,
+                [UseDisposition::Committed, UseDisposition::Committed]
+            );
+        }
+        let counts:(i64,i64,i64)=sqlx::query_as("SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM game_character_viola_book_cycles),(SELECT count(*) FROM game_reward_claim_mint_receipts WHERE claim_production_key LIKE 'oteryn:reward-claim.tutorial.viola-book:%'),(SELECT count(*) FROM game_item_instances WHERE definition_production_key='oteryn:item.tibia.i51977')").fetch_one(&h.pool).await?;
+        assert_eq!(counts, (2, 2, 2));
+        drop(authority);
+        drop(seal);
+        h.cleanup().await
+    })
+}
+pub(super) async fn free_one(
+    owner: &crate::gameplay_transport::ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_>,
+    fence: crate::durability::item_transfer::CurrentCharacterItemFence,
+    command_id: u64,
+) -> TestResult {
+    let native = owner
+        .active_generation
+        .and_then(|generation| generation.native_gameplay())
+        .ok_or("native")?;
+    let bag = owner
+        .root
+        .read_character_backpack(owner.character, fence.character_id)
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?
+        .ok_or("bag")?;
+    let filler = bag
+        .entries
+        .iter()
+        .find(|entry| entry.item.definition.production_key == "oteryn:item.tibia.i3031")
+        .ok_or("filler")?;
+    let (digest, frame) = {
+        let runtime = owner.runtime.lock().await;
+        (
+            runtime.content_pin().server_artifact_digest(),
+            runtime.content_pin().frame_binding_digest(),
+        )
+    };
+    let revision = format!(
+        "sha256:{}",
+        digest
+            .iter()
+            .map(|b| format!("{b:02x}"))
+            .collect::<String>()
+    );
+    let permit = owner.spell_lane_permit().await.ok_or("lane permit")?;
+    let moved = owner
+        .root
+        .commit_item_loop_move(
+            &permit,
+            owner.character,
+            owner.holder,
+            &fence,
+            CommandRef::new(
+                fence.game_session_id,
+                CommandId::new(command_id).map_err(debug)?,
+            ),
+            &NativeItemWeights(native),
+            crate::durability::item_loop_move::LoopMoveRequest {
+                item: filler.item.item_instance_id,
+                count: None,
+                pickup_cell: None,
+                destination: crate::durability::item_loop_move::LoopDestination::Ground(
+                    crate::durability::item_loop_move::LoopGround {
+                        spatial_position:
+                            crate::durability::map_item_mint_audit::map_item_spatial_position(
+                                32613, 32419, -7,
+                            ),
+                        map_revision: crate::map::overlay::map_revision(
+                            owner.bundle_map().ok_or("bundle map")?.overlay.base(),
+                        ),
+                        content_revision: revision,
+                        placement_context: frame.to_vec(),
+                    },
+                ),
+            },
+        )
+        .await
+        .map_err(debug)?;
+    assert!(moved.is_ok(), "free capacity: {moved:?}");
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+/// Exact committed source stack; the marker points one tile south of the chest.
+pub(super) fn source_world() -> TestResult<crate::map::boot::BundleWorld> {
+    use oteryn_world_bundle::{bundle, sector};
+    let (bytes, mut pins) = crate::map::boot::tests::bundle();
+    let mut data = bundle::read(&bytes)?;
+    data.manifest.palette.clear();
+    for (id, appearance) in [16485, 26996, 2472].into_iter().enumerate() {
+        data.manifest.palette.push(bundle::PaletteEntry {
+            key: format!("oteryn:terrain.tibia.i{appearance}"),
+            family: bundle::Family::Terrain,
+            id: u32::try_from(id)?,
+            terrain: Some(bundle::Terrain {
+                kind: if id == 0 {
+                    bundle::TerrainKind::Ground
+                } else {
+                    bundle::TerrainKind::Common
+                },
+                walkable: (id == 0).then_some(true),
+                ground_speed: (id == 0).then_some(150),
+            }),
+        });
+    }
+    data.manifest.world = bundle::Extent {
+        min_x: 32570,
+        min_y: 32419,
+        max_x: 32615,
+        max_y: 32509,
+        floors: vec![-7],
+    };
+    let mut tiles = Vec::new();
+    for y in 32419..=32420 {
+        for x in 32612..=32614 {
+            let palettes = if (x, y) == (32614, 32419) {
+                vec![0, 1, 2]
+            } else {
+                vec![0]
+            };
+            tiles.push(sector::Tile {
+                x,
+                y,
+                flags: 0,
+                house: 0,
+                zones: Vec::new(),
+                items: palettes
+                    .into_iter()
+                    .map(|palette| sector::Item {
+                        palette,
+                        depth: 0,
+                        attrs: sector::Attrs::default(),
+                    })
+                    .collect(),
+            });
+        }
+    }
+    // Retain Viola's real source placement, with a walkable approach in her sector.
+    for y in 32506..=32508 {
+        for x in 32570..=32572 {
+            tiles.push(sector::Tile {
+                x,
+                y,
+                flags: 0,
+                house: 0,
+                zones: Vec::new(),
+                items: vec![sector::Item {
+                    palette: 0,
+                    depth: 0,
+                    attrs: sector::Attrs::default(),
+                }],
+            });
+        }
+    }
+    let mut grouped = std::collections::BTreeMap::<(u16, u16), Vec<sector::Tile>>::new();
+    for tile in tiles {
+        grouped
+            .entry((tile.y / 32, tile.x / 32))
+            .or_default()
+            .push(tile);
+    }
+    let sectors = grouped
+        .into_iter()
+        .map(|((sy, sx), mut tiles)| {
+            tiles.sort_by_key(|tile| (tile.y, tile.x));
+            bundle::Sector {
+                floor: -7,
+                sx,
+                sy,
+                tiles,
+            }
+        })
+        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
+    let bytes = oteryn_world_bundle::writer::write(&data.manifest, &sectors, &Default::default())?;
+    pins.digest = bundle::read(&bytes)?.digest;
+    let base = crate::map::load(&bytes, &pins).map_err(debug)?;
+    crate::map::boot::boot(
+        &bytes,
+        &crate::map::boot::BootPins {
+            map_revision: crate::map::overlay::map_revision(&base),
+            bundle: pins,
+            start: crate::map::overlay::TilePos {
+                x: 32613,
+                y: 32419,
+                floor: -7,
+            },
+        },
+        crate::foundation::WorldId::decode(&id(42)).map_err(debug)?,
+        crate::foundation::ChannelId::decode(&id(43)).map_err(debug)?,
+        |_| None,
+    )
+    .map_err(debug)
+}
+
+pub(super) fn target(world: &crate::map::boot::BundleWorld) -> TestResult<WorldObjectTarget> {
+    let key =
+        oteryn_world_bundle::bundle::placement_key(-7, 32614, 32419, 2).ok_or("book placement")?;
+    let mut placement = world.base().digest().to_vec();
+    placement.extend_from_slice(&key.to_be_bytes());
+    Ok(WorldObjectTarget {
+        placement,
+        expected_revision: 0,
+    })
+}

+                .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, ()>>()?
         };
-        let desired = corruptor_items(build.vocation());
-        let defined = if desired.iter().all(|id| definitions.contains_key(id)) {
-            desired.to_vec()
-        } else {
-            Vec::new()
+        // Requeues and historical recapture share this cell for the exact retained
+        // corpse. Freeze ordinary stamina/event/duplex results before any MINT.
+        table = entry.tutorial_loot.get_or_init(|| table).clone();
+        let first = source_draw_count(&table.entries).ok_or(())?;
+        let claim = claim.bind_draw_ordinals(first).ok_or(())?;
+        // Append after all ordinary transforms. The DailyBoosted prefix remains
+        // ordinary loot, and each guarantee has its own source ordinal.
+        table
+            .entries
+            .extend(definitions.into_iter().map(guaranteed_entry));
+        Ok((table, Some(claim)))
+    }
+}
+
+fn source_draw_count(entries: &[LootTableEntry]) -> Option<u32> {
+    entries.iter().try_fold(0_u32, |count, entry| {
+        count
+            .checked_add(1)?
+            .checked_add(source_draw_count(&entry.details.children)?)
+    })
+}
+
+fn guaranteed_entry(item: LootDefinitionRef) -> LootTableEntry {
+    LootTableEntry {
+        item,
+        min_count: 1,
+        max_count: 1,
+        probability_ppm: Some(1_000_000),
+        // Ordinary rolls apply a 95..=105% dynamic factor even at 100% chance.
+        // Existing rated chances allow 2M: 2M * 95% exceeds every 0..1M draw.
+        details: LootEntryDetails {
+            rated_probability_ppm: Some(2_000_000),
+            ..Default::default()
+        },
+    }
+}
+
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod tests {
+    use super::*;
+    use oteryn_gameplay_items::loot_plan::{LootPlanDeathKey, plan_creature_loot};
+
+    #[test]
+    fn newhaven_corruptor_guarantee_survives_low_factor_and_keeps_independent_ordinary_drop()
+    -> Result<(), String> {
+        let item = LootDefinitionRef::new("Item", "oteryn:item.tibia.i3359", "definition-r1");
+        let reference = LootDefinitionRef::new(
+            "Loot",
+            "oteryn:loot.creature.the_corruptor",
+            "definition-r1",
+        );
+        let mut ordinary = guaranteed_entry(item.clone());
+        ordinary.details.rated_probability_ppm = None;
+        let fixed = LootTableDefinition {
+            algorithm: crate::combat::LootSelectionAlgorithm::IndependentBernoulliPpm,
+            entries: vec![ordinary.clone(), guaranteed_entry(item)],
         };
-        let claimed = self
-            .root
-            .claim_tutorial_corruptor(
-                self.character,
-                self.holder,
-                fence,
-                entry.facts.death,
-                defined,
-            )
-            .await
-            .map_err(|_| ())?;
-        for id in claimed {
-            table.entries.push(LootTableEntry {
-                item: definitions.get(&id).ok_or(())?.clone(),
-                min_count: 1,
-                max_count: 1,
-                probability_ppm: Some(1_000_000),
-                details: Default::default(),
-            });
+        let legacy = LootTableDefinition {
+            algorithm: fixed.algorithm,
+            entries: vec![ordinary.clone(), ordinary],
+        };
+        let mut low_factor_witness = None;
+        let mut ordinary_witness = false;
+        for generation in 1..=4096 {
+            let death = LootPlanDeathKey::new([1; 16], [2; 16], 1, 2, generation);
+            let old =
+                plan_creature_loot(death, &reference, &legacy).map_err(|e| format!("{e:?}"))?;
+            let new =
+                plan_creature_loot(death, &reference, &fixed).map_err(|e| format!("{e:?}"))?;
+            assert_eq!(
+                new.entries.iter().filter(|e| e.draw_ordinal == 1).count(),
+                1
+            );
+            if !old.entries.iter().any(|e| e.draw_ordinal == 1) {
+                low_factor_witness = Some(generation);
+            }
+            if old.entries.iter().any(|e| e.draw_ordinal == 0) {
+                ordinary_witness = true;
+                assert_eq!(
+                    new.entries.len(),
+                    2,
+                    "ordinary and special drops have distinct causes"
+                );
+            }
         }
-        Ok(table)
+        assert!(
+            low_factor_witness.is_some(),
+            "exercise a nominal100% entry rejected by low factor/high draw"
+        );
+        assert!(ordinary_witness);
+        Ok(())
     }
 }

+//! Current Book of Wisdom chest: exact committed selector and five-minute personal repeat.
+use super::ComposedFreshAdmission;
+use super::connection::{EarnedNotice, UseCommand, UseOutcome};
+use super::item_ref_admission::{NativeItemWeights, generation_item_facts, pinned_index};

+use crate::map::view::MapFacts;
+use oteryn_protocol_oteryn::world_object::{UseDisposition, WorldObjectTarget};

+const POSITION: (u16, u16, i8) = (32614, 32419, -7);
+const CLAIM: &str = "oteryn:reward-claim.tutorial.viola-book";
+const SOURCE: &str = "oteryn:placement.tutorial.viola-book.32614-32419-7";
+
+fn outcome(disposition: UseDisposition) -> UseOutcome {
+    UseOutcome {
+        disposition,
+        committed: None,
+        earned: EarnedNotice::NoneEarned,
+        gate_message_id: None,
+        refusal_detail: None,
+    }
+}
+
+impl ComposedFreshAdmission<'_, '_, '_> {
+    pub(super) async fn use_viola_book(
+        &self,
+        actor: ExactActorRef,
+        command: UseCommand,
+        map: &crate::map::boot::BundleMap,
+        target: &WorldObjectTarget,
+    ) -> Option<UseOutcome> {
+        let generation = self.active_generation?;
+        let native = generation.native_gameplay()?;
+        let source = MapViewSource {
+            overlay: &map.overlay,
+            facts: &map.facts,
+            content_generation: generation.identity().client_artifact_digest(),
+            reset_epoch: self.reset_epoch,
+        };
+        let resolved = resolve_map_target(&source, target, |_| Some(())).ok()?;
+        if (resolved.pos.x, resolved.pos.y, resolved.pos.floor) != POSITION {
+            return None;
+        }
+        let base = map.overlay.base();
+        let tile = RewardClaimBase::tile(base.as_ref(), POSITION.0, POSITION.1, POSITION.2)?;
+        let id = tile
+            .depths()
+            .iter()
+            .zip(tile.ids())
+            .filter(|(depth, _)| **depth == 0)
+            .nth(usize::from(resolved.ordinal))
+            .map(|(_, id)| *id)?;
+        let entry = map.facts.base_entry(resolved.pos, resolved.ordinal, id)?;
+        if resolved.ordinal != 2
+            || tile
+                .unique(resolved.ordinal)
+                .is_some_and(|entry| entry.unique != 0)
+            || entry.appearance_id != 2472
+        {
+            return Some(outcome(UseDisposition::NothingToUse));
+        }
+        Some(
+            tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10), async {
+                let Some(_permit) = self.spell_lane_permit().await else {
+                    return outcome(UseDisposition::Rejected);
+                };
+                let requests = {
+                    let mut runtime = self.runtime.lock().await;
+                    // Do not suspend while holding the runtime owner lock.
+                    let Ok(states) = self.states.spell_states.try_lock() else {
+                        return outcome(UseDisposition::Rejected);
+                    };
+                    if !runtime
+                        .player_control_facts(actor, command.game_session_id)
+                        .is_ok_and(|facts| facts.control_loss.is_none())
+                        || states.is_dead(actor)
+                        || !states.spell_idle(&runtime, actor, command.game_session_id)
+                        || !self
+                            .states
+                            .player_trades
+                            .physical(command.game_session_id, None)
+                    {
+                        return outcome(UseDisposition::Rejected);
+                    }
+                    let Some(index) = pinned_index(&runtime, self.active_generation) else {
+                        return outcome(UseDisposition::Rejected);
+                    };
+                    let Ok(position) = runtime.borrow_movement_position().read(actor) else {
+                        return outcome(UseDisposition::Rejected);
+                    };
+                    if position.context() != runtime.pinned_movement_context() {
+                        return outcome(UseDisposition::Rejected);
+                    }
+                    let position = position.position();
+                    if position.x.abs_diff(i32::from(POSITION.0)) > 1
+                        || position.y.abs_diff(i32::from(POSITION.1)) > 1
+                        || i32::from(position.floor) != -i32::from(POSITION.2)
+                    {
+                        return outcome(UseDisposition::TooFar);
+                    }
+                    if !self
+                        .states
+                        .world_gate
+                        .lock()
+                        .is_ok_and(|mut gate| gate.admit(actor, self.clock_origin.elapsed()))
+                    {
+                        return outcome(UseDisposition::Exhausted);
+                    }
+                    let key = "oteryn:item.tibia.i51977";
+                    index.revision(key).and_then(|revision| {
+                        generation_item_facts(
+                            native,
+                            &TypedDefinitionRef {
+                                family: "Item".into(),
+                                production_key: key.into(),
+                                revision_ref: revision.into(),
+                            },
+                        )
+                        .ok()
+                    })
+                };
+                let Some(item) = requests else {
+                    return outcome(UseDisposition::Rejected);
+                };
+                let (Some(fence), Ok(id)) =
+                    (command.item_fence, CommandId::new(command.command_id))
+                else {
+                    return outcome(UseDisposition::Rejected);
+                };
+                let Ok(Some(bag)) = self
+                    .root
+                    .read_character_backpack(self.character, fence.character_id)
+                    .await
+                else {
+                    return outcome(UseDisposition::Rejected);
+                };
+                let Ok(backpack) = generation_item_facts(native, &bag.backpack.definition) else {
+                    return outcome(UseDisposition::Rejected);
+                };
+                let content_revision = format!(
+                    "sha256:{}",
+                    native
+                        .source_digest()
+                        .iter()
+                        .map(|b| format!("{b:02x}"))
+                        .collect::<String>()
+                );
+                let request = RewardClaimMintRequest {
+                    command: CommandRef::new(command.game_session_id, id),
+                    claim: TypedDefinitionRef {
+                        family: "RewardClaim".into(),
+                        production_key: CLAIM.into(),
+                        revision_ref: "reward-claim-r1".into(),
+                    },
+                    source_placement: SOURCE.into(),
+                    item,
+                    quantity: 1,
+                    contents: Vec::new(),
+                    root_text: None,
+                    root_fluid_subtype: None,
+                    root_charges_remaining: None,
+                    backpack,
+                    content_revision,
+                    ruleset_revision: crate::interaction_chest_use::entry_chest::RULESET_REVISION
+                        .into(),
+                    sim_revision: crate::interaction_chest_use::entry_chest::SIM_REVISION.into(),
+                    achievement: None,
+                    quest_transition: None,
+                    key_number: None,
+                };
+                match self
+                    .root
+                    .claim_viola_book(
+                        self.character,
+                        self.holder,
+                        fence,
+                        request,
+                        &NativeItemWeights(native).with_wheel(self.premium_coordinator),
+                    )
+                    .await
+                {
+                    Ok(_) => outcome(UseDisposition::Committed),
+                    Err(error) => viola_book_refusal(error),
+                }
+            })
+            .await
+            .unwrap_or_else(|_| outcome(UseDisposition::Rejected)),
+        )
+    }
+}
+fn viola_book_refusal(error: RewardClaimMintError) -> UseOutcome {
+    outcome(crate::interaction_chest_use::chest_use_disposition(
+        &crate::interaction_chest_use::ChestUseError::Mint(error),
+    ))
+}

+//! Source NPC storage writes use the existing Character cursor and retry contract.
+use super::super::quest_state::npc_program::{NpcProgramCommit, NpcProgramRequest};
+use super::*;
+impl RevisionSlot {
+    pub async fn commit_npc_program(
+        &mut self,
+        root: &DurabilityRoot,
+        authority: &ReconciledCharacterAuthority<'_, '_>,
+        node: &NodeIncarnationProof,
+        fence: CurrentCharacterGameplayFence,
+        request: NpcProgramRequest,
+        catalogue: Arc<QuestStateCatalogue>,
+    ) -> Result<NpcProgramCommit, CharacterProgressionError> {
+        self.sequenced(
+            root_revision(root, authority),
+            fence,
+            Expect::Cursor(OnMismatch::RetryOnce),
+            |fence| {
+                root.commit_character_npc_program(
+                    authority,
+                    node,
+                    fence,
+                    request.clone(),
+                    Arc::clone(&catalogue),
+                )
+            },
+        )
+        .await
+    }
+}
+
+impl SequencedOutcome for NpcProgramCommit {
+    fn committed_revision(&self) -> Option<CharacterRevision> {
+        self.transition.committed_revision()
+    }
+}

+//! Ordered source NPC storage operations on the existing Character revision and quest receipt owners.
+use super::*;
+use oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::npc_rules::{
+    NpcInventorySnapshot, NpcRuleCatalog, NpcRuleInput, NpcRulePlan, NpcRuleTrigger,
+};
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub struct NpcProgramRequest {
+    pub command: CommandRef,
+    pub npc: String,
+    pub trigger: NpcRuleTrigger,
+    pub message: String,
+    pub topic: i32,
+    pub transition_key: String,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct NpcProgramCommit {
+    pub transition: QuestTransitionOutcome,
+    pub reply: Vec<String>,
+    pub next_topic: i32,
+}
+
+impl NpcProgramRequest {
+    fn quest_request(&self) -> QuestTransitionRequest {
+        QuestTransitionRequest {
+            transition_key: self.transition_key.clone(),
+            cause: QuestCause::Command(self.command),
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn binding(&self, character: CharacterId) -> [u8; 33] {
+        let mut digest =
+            Sha256::new().chain_update(request_binding(character, &self.quest_request()));
+        for value in [&self.npc, &self.message] {
+            digest = digest
+                .chain_update((value.len() as u64).to_be_bytes())
+                .chain_update(value.as_bytes());
+        }
+        let trigger = match self.trigger {
+            NpcRuleTrigger::Say => 0,
+            NpcRuleTrigger::Greet => 1,
+            NpcRuleTrigger::Farewell => 2,
+            NpcRuleTrigger::Walkaway => 3,
+        };
+        let digest = digest
+            .chain_update([trigger])
+            .chain_update(self.topic.to_be_bytes())
+            .finalize();
+        let mut result = [1; 33];
+        result[1..].copy_from_slice(&digest);
+        result
+    }
+}
+
+/// Pure preview and locked reevaluation use exactly the same source branch order.
+pub fn preview(
+    request: &NpcProgramRequest,
+    tracks: &BTreeMap<String, i64>,
+    catalogue: &QuestStateCatalogue,
+    now: i64,
+) -> std::result::Result<Option<NpcRulePlan>, oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::npc_rules::NpcRuleError>
+{
+    let source = NpcRuleCatalog::runtime()?
+        .clone()
+        .with_native_bounds(catalogue)?;
+    evaluate_source(&source, request, tracks, catalogue, now)
+}
+
+fn evaluate_source(
+    source: &NpcRuleCatalog,
+    request: &NpcProgramRequest,
+    tracks: &BTreeMap<String, i64>,
+    catalogue: &QuestStateCatalogue,
+    now: i64,
+) -> std::result::Result<Option<NpcRulePlan>, oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::npc_rules::NpcRuleError>
+{
+    let mut complete = tracks.clone();
+    for program in source.programs() {
+        for rule in &program.document.rules {
+            for guard in &rule.guards {
+                if let oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::npc_rules::NpcRuleGuard::Storage {
+                    track, ..
+                }
+                | oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::npc_rules::NpcRuleGuard::StorageTime {
+                    track,
+                    ..
+                } = guard
+                    && let Some(definition) = catalogue.track(track)
+                {
+                    let initial = source
+                        .programs()
+                        .iter()
+                        .flat_map(|program| &program.document.tracks)
+                        .find(|source_track| source_track.key == *track)
+                        .map_or(definition.initial, |source_track| source_track.initial);
+                    complete.entry(track.clone()).or_insert(initial);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    source.plan_storage(&NpcRuleInput {
+        npc: &request.npc,
+        trigger: request.trigger,
+        message: &request.message,
+        topic: request.topic,
+        tracks: &complete,
+        inventory: &NpcInventorySnapshot::default(),
+        now_epoch_seconds: now,
+        calendar_month: 1,
+        level: 0,
+        vocation: "",
+        sex: 0,
+        outfits: &BTreeMap::new(),
+    })
+}
+
+impl DurabilityRoot {
+    /// Replay a bound NPC response without granting current gameplay authority.
+    pub async fn reconcile_npc_program(
+        &self,
+        authority: &ReconciledCharacterAuthority<'_, '_>,
+        character: CharacterId,
+        request: NpcProgramRequest,
+    ) -> Result<Option<NpcProgramCommit>> {
+        let recovery = authority
+            .record_for(self)
+            .map_err(|_| CharacterProgressionError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+        self.try_issue_semantic_pass()?
+            .run(move |holder, deadline| {
+                Box::pin(async move {
+                    let mut tx = begin_semantic_transaction(holder, deadline).await?;
+                    assert_recovery_fence(&mut tx, &recovery).await?;
+                    lock_receipt_key(&mut tx, character, &request.quest_request()).await?;
+                    let result = load(&mut tx, character, &request).await?;
+                    commit_semantic_transaction(tx, deadline).await?;
+                    Ok(result)
+                })
+            })
+            .await?
+    }
+
+    /// One source storage operation, including Father's eleven-track acceptance, is one transaction.
+    pub async fn commit_character_npc_program(
+        &self,
+        authority: &ReconciledCharacterAuthority<'_, '_>,
+        node: &NodeIncarnationProof,
+        fence: CurrentCharacterGameplayFence,
+        request: NpcProgramRequest,
+        catalogue: Arc<QuestStateCatalogue>,
+    ) -> Result<NpcProgramCommit> {
+        if request.command.game_session_id() != fence.game_session_id
+            || request.message.len() > 255
+            || !request.transition_key.starts_with("oteryn:quest-npc/")
+            || !valid_quest_key(&request.transition_key)
+            || request.topic < 0
+            || fence.character_lease_generation == 0
+            || !matches!(fence.runtime_scope, RuntimeScopeRefV1::Channel { .. })
+        {
+            return Err(CharacterProgressionError::InvalidInput);
+        }
+        let recovery = authority
+            .record_for(self)
+            .map_err(|_| CharacterProgressionError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+        let node = node.clone();
+        let source = NpcRuleCatalog::runtime()
+            .and_then(|source| source.clone().with_native_bounds(&catalogue))
+            .map_err(|_| CharacterProgressionError::InvalidInput)?;
+        self.try_issue_semantic_pass()?.run(move |holder,deadline| Box::pin(async move {
+            let mut tx=begin_semantic_transaction(holder,deadline).await?;
+            assert_recovery_fence(&mut tx,&recovery).await?;
+            lock_admission_relations(&mut tx).await?;
+            let character=fence.character_id;
+            let q=request.quest_request();
+            lock_receipt_key(&mut tx,character,&q).await?;
+            match load(&mut tx,character,&request).await? {
+                Ok(Some(replay)) => {commit_semantic_transaction(tx,deadline).await?;return Ok(Ok(replay));}
+                Err(error) => return Ok(Err(error)),
+                Ok(None) => {}
+            }
+            let root=match assert_gameplay_fence(&mut tx,&fence,&node).await? {
+                Ok(root)=>root,Err(e)=>return Ok(Err(e)),
+            };
+            let id=character.as_bytes().as_slice();
+            let state=sqlx::query("SELECT character_revision::text,level,total_experience,profile_revision,ruleset_revision,content_revision,simulation_revision,evidence_revision,declaration_revision,policy_revision,reward_revision FROM game_character_progression_state WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid FOR UPDATE")
+                .bind(id).fetch_optional(&mut *tx).await?;
+            let Some(state)=state else {return Ok(Err(CharacterProgressionError::MissingProgressionState));};
+            if numeric_u64(&state,"character_revision")?!=root.revision {return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);}
+            if !state_matches_root(&state,&root) || state.try_get::<String,_>("content_revision")?!=catalogue.content_revision() {
+                return Ok(Err(CharacterProgressionError::ProgressionContextMismatch));
+            }
+            let pending:bool=sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM game_character_pending_respawns WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid)").bind(id).fetch_one(&mut *tx).await?;
+            if pending {return Ok(Err(CharacterProgressionError::RespawnPending));}
+            // Root lock serializes every Character writer; sorted bounded tracks include foreign guard reads.
+            let rows=sqlx::query("SELECT track_key,value FROM game_character_quest_tracks WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid ORDER BY track_key LIMIT $2 FOR UPDATE")
+                .bind(id).bind((QUESTSTATE0_RL_08_TRACKS+1) as i64).fetch_all(&mut *tx).await?;
+            if rows.len()>QUESTSTATE0_RL_08_TRACKS {return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);}
+            let mut tracks=BTreeMap::new();
+            for row in rows {tracks.insert(row.try_get::<String,_>("track_key")?,row.try_get::<i64,_>("value")?);}
+            let states=sqlx::query("SELECT quest_key,definition_hash FROM game_character_quest_states WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid ORDER BY quest_key LIMIT $2 FOR UPDATE")
+                .bind(id).bind((QUESTSTATE0_RL_08_STATES+1) as i64).fetch_all(&mut *tx).await?;
+            if states.len()>QUESTSTATE0_RL_08_STATES {return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);}
+            let mut effective_tracks=tracks.clone();
+            for state in states {
+                let quest:String=state.try_get("quest_key")?;
+                let stored:[u8;32]=state.try_get::<Vec<u8>,_>("definition_hash")?.try_into().map_err(|_|DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+                if let Some(defaults)=oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::npc_rules::legacy_defaults(&catalogue,&quest,&stored) {
+                    for (key,value) in defaults {effective_tracks.entry(key.clone()).or_insert(*value);}
+                }
+            }
+            let now:i64=sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT floor(extract(epoch FROM now()))::bigint").fetch_one(&mut *tx).await?;
+            let plan=match evaluate_source(&source,&request,&effective_tracks,&catalogue,now) {
+                Ok(Some(plan)) if plan.selected_rule_keys.first()==Some(&request.transition_key) && !plan.track_changes.is_empty()=>plan,
+                _=>return Ok(Ok(refused(QuestRefusal::StageMismatch))),
+            };
+            let quest=&plan.quest;
+            let hash=catalogue.definition_hash(quest).ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+            let stored_state=sqlx::query("SELECT pinned_content_revision,definition_hash,completed_character_revision IS NOT NULL AS completed FROM game_character_quest_states WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND quest_key=$2 FOR UPDATE").bind(id).bind(quest).fetch_optional(&mut *tx).await?;
+            let (pin,hash)=match &stored_state {
+                Some(row)=>{
+                    let stored:[u8;32]=row.try_get::<Vec<u8>,_>("definition_hash")?.try_into().map_err(|_|DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+                    if stored!=hash && !row.try_get::<bool,_>("completed")?
+                        && oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::npc_rules::legacy_defaults(&catalogue,quest,&stored).is_none()
+                    {return Ok(Ok(refused(QuestRefusal::RevisionMismatch)));}
+                    (row.try_get::<String,_>("pinned_content_revision")?,stored)
+                }
+                None=>{
+                    if !capacity::new_state_fits(&mut tx,character).await? {return Ok(Ok(refused(QuestRefusal::CapacityExceeded)));}
+                    (catalogue.content_revision().to_owned(),hash)
+                }
+            };
+            let keys=plan.track_changes.iter().map(|c|c.key.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+            let existing=keys.iter().filter(|k|tracks.contains_key(**k)).count();
+            if !capacity::tracks_fit(&mut tx,character,&keys,existing).await? {return Ok(Ok(refused(QuestRefusal::CapacityExceeded)));}
+            let revision=root.revision.checked_add(1).ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+            let original=root.revision.to_string();let committed=revision.to_string();
+            for query in ["UPDATE game_character_roots SET character_revision=$2::text::numeric WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND character_revision=$3::text::numeric", "UPDATE game_character_progression_state SET character_revision=$2::text::numeric WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND character_revision=$3::text::numeric"] {
+                if sqlx::query(query).bind(id).bind(&committed).bind(&original).execute(&mut *tx).await?.rows_affected()!=1 {return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);}
+            }
+            let before=plan.track_changes.iter().map(|c|c.before).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+            let after=plan.track_changes.iter().map(|c|c.after).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+            sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_character_quest_receipts(character_id,cause_kind,cause_id,cause_ordinal,transition_key,quest_key,request_binding,pinned_content_revision,definition_hash,completes,track_keys,values_before,values_after,original_character_revision,committed_character_revision,level_before,level_after,experience_before,experience_after,profile_revision,ruleset_revision,content_revision,simulation_revision,evidence_revision,declaration_revision,policy_revision,reward_revision,committed_at) SELECT s.character_id,'command',encode($2,'hex')::uuid,$3::text::numeric,$4,$5,$6,$7,$8,false,$9::text[],$10::bigint[],$11::bigint[],$12::text::numeric,$13::text::numeric,s.level,s.level,s.total_experience,s.total_experience,s.profile_revision,s.ruleset_revision,s.content_revision,s.simulation_revision,s.evidence_revision,s.declaration_revision,s.policy_revision,s.reward_revision,floor(extract(epoch FROM statement_timestamp())*1000)::bigint FROM game_character_progression_state s WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid")
+                .bind(id).bind(request.command.game_session_id().as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(request.command.command_id().get().to_string()).bind(&request.transition_key).bind(quest).bind(request.binding(character).as_slice()).bind(&pin).bind(hash.as_slice()).bind(&keys).bind(&before).bind(&after).bind(&original).bind(&committed).execute(&mut *tx).await?;
+            for change in &plan.track_changes {
+                sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_character_quest_tracks(character_id,track_key,quest_key,value,committed_character_revision) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,$2,$3,$4,$5::text::numeric) ON CONFLICT(character_id,track_key) DO UPDATE SET value=EXCLUDED.value,committed_character_revision=EXCLUDED.committed_character_revision")
+                    .bind(id).bind(&change.key).bind(quest).bind(change.after).bind(&committed).execute(&mut *tx).await?;
+            }
+            if stored_state.is_some() {
+                sqlx::query("UPDATE game_character_quest_states SET committed_character_revision=$3::text::numeric WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND quest_key=$2").bind(id).bind(quest).bind(&committed).execute(&mut *tx).await?;
+            } else {
+                sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_character_quest_states(character_id,quest_key,pinned_content_revision,definition_hash,started_character_revision,committed_character_revision) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,$2,$3,$4,$5::text::numeric,$5::text::numeric)").bind(id).bind(quest).bind(&pin).bind(hash.as_slice()).bind(&committed).execute(&mut *tx).await?;
+            }
+            sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_quest_npc_program_receipts(character_id,game_session_id,command_id,transition_key,reply,next_topic) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid,$3::text::numeric,$4,$5,$6)")
+                .bind(id).bind(request.command.game_session_id().as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(request.command.command_id().get().to_string()).bind(&request.transition_key).bind(&plan.reply).bind(plan.next_topic).execute(&mut *tx).await?;
+            let receipt=load_receipt(&mut tx,character,&q).await?.ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+            let receipt=decode_receipt(&receipt)?;
+            commit_semantic_transaction(tx,deadline).await?;
+            Ok(Ok(NpcProgramCommit{transition:QuestTransitionOutcome::Committed(receipt),reply:plan.reply,next_topic:plan.next_topic}))
+        })).await?
+    }
+}
+
+fn refused(refusal: QuestRefusal) -> NpcProgramCommit {
+    NpcProgramCommit {
+        transition: QuestTransitionOutcome::Refused(refusal),
+        reply: Vec::new(),
+        next_topic: 0,
+    }
+}
+
+async fn load(
+    tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Postgres>,
+    character: CharacterId,
+    request: &NpcProgramRequest,
+) -> StoredResult<Result<Option<NpcProgramCommit>>> {
+    let Some(row) = load_receipt(tx, character, &request.quest_request()).await? else {
+        return Ok(Ok(None));
+    };
+    if row.try_get::<Vec<u8>, _>("request_binding")? != request.binding(character) {
+        return Ok(Err(CharacterProgressionError::ConflictingOccurrence));
+    }
+    let response=sqlx::query("SELECT reply,next_topic FROM game_quest_npc_program_receipts WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND game_session_id=encode($2,'hex')::uuid AND command_id=$3::text::numeric AND transition_key=$4")
+        .bind(character.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(request.command.game_session_id().as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(request.command.command_id().get().to_string()).bind(&request.transition_key).fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?.ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    Ok(Ok(Some(NpcProgramCommit {
+        transition: QuestTransitionOutcome::AlreadyCommitted(decode_receipt(&row)?),
+        reply: response.try_get("reply")?,
+        next_topic: response.try_get("next_topic")?,
+    })))
+}

+impl super::DurabilityRoot {
+    /// Read a literal callback's quest gates under the live session and Character
+    /// root fences. This path never writes Quest state, receipts or revisions.
+    #[allow(
+        clippy::too_many_arguments,
+        reason = "the read owner requires the same authority and world context as callback writes"
+    )]
+    pub async fn check_character_world_callback(
+        &self,
+        authority: &super::character_authority::ReconciledCharacterAuthority<'_, '_>,
+        node: &super::runtime_scope_assignment::NodeIncarnationProof,
+        fence: CurrentCharacterGameplayFence,
+        callback_key: &str,
+        catalogue: std::sync::Arc<oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::QuestStateCatalogue>,
+        permit: &SpellLanePermit,
+        context: QuestWorldContext,
+    ) -> Result<bool, super::character_progression::CharacterProgressionError> {
+        use super::character_authority::assert_recovery_fence;
+        use super::character_progression::{CharacterProgressionError, assert_item_only_fence};
+        use super::db::{
+            begin_semantic_transaction, commit_semantic_transaction, lock_admission_relations,
+        };
+        let callback = oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::world_callbacks::load()
+            .map_err(|_| CharacterProgressionError::InvalidInput)?
+            .into_iter()
+            .find(|callback| callback.key == callback_key && callback.is_read_only())
+            .ok_or(CharacterProgressionError::InvalidInput)?;
+        let recovery = authority
+            .record_for(self)
+            .map_err(|_| CharacterProgressionError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+        let node = node.clone();
+        let mut context = (permit, context);
+        self.try_issue_semantic_pass()?.run_with_context(&mut context, move |holder, deadline, (permit, context)| {
+            Box::pin(async move {
+                let mut tx = begin_semantic_transaction(holder, deadline).await?;
+                assert_recovery_fence(&mut tx, &recovery).await?;
+                lock_admission_relations(&mut tx).await?;
+                if let Err(error) = assert_item_only_fence(&mut tx, &fence.item_fence(), &node).await? {
+                    return Ok(Err(error));
+                }
+                if !context_is_current(&mut tx, permit, fence, context).await? {
+                    return Ok(Ok(false));
+                }
+                let current: Option<String> = sqlx::query_scalar(
+                    "SELECT content_revision FROM game_character_progression_state \
+                     WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+                ).bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).fetch_optional(&mut *tx).await?;
+                if current.as_deref() != Some(catalogue.content_revision()) {
+                    return Ok(Err(CharacterProgressionError::ProgressionContextMismatch));
+                }
+                let pending: bool = sqlx::query_scalar(
+                    "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM game_character_pending_respawns \
+                     WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid)",
+                ).bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).fetch_one(&mut *tx).await?;
+                if pending {
+                    return Ok(Err(CharacterProgressionError::RespawnPending));
+                }
+                let pin = sqlx::query(
+                    "SELECT pinned_content_revision,definition_hash,completed_character_revision IS NOT NULL AS completed \
+                     FROM game_character_quest_states WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND quest_key=$2",
+                ).bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(&callback.quest)
+                    .fetch_optional(&mut *tx).await?;
+                if let Some(pin) = pin {
+                    let pin = super::quest_state::QuestStateRecord {
+                        pinned_content_revision: pin.try_get("pinned_content_revision")?,
+                        definition_hash: pin.try_get::<Vec<u8>, _>("definition_hash")?.try_into()
+                            .map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?,
+                        completed: pin.try_get("completed")?,
+                    };
+                    if !read_only_pin_allows(&pin, &catalogue, &callback.quest)? {
+                        return Ok(Err(CharacterProgressionError::ProgressionContextMismatch));
+                    }
+                }
+                for storage in &callback.storage {
+                    let Some(track) = catalogue.track(&storage.track).filter(|track| track.quest == callback.quest) else {
+                        return Ok(Err(CharacterProgressionError::InvalidInput));
+                    };
+                    let value: Option<i64> = sqlx::query_scalar(
+                        "SELECT value FROM game_character_quest_tracks \
+                         WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND track_key=$2 AND quest_key=$3",
+                    ).bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(&storage.track)
+                        .bind(&callback.quest).fetch_optional(&mut *tx).await?;
+                    if !(storage.minimum..=storage.maximum).contains(&value.unwrap_or(track.initial)) {
+                        return Ok(Ok(false));
+                    }
+                }
+                commit_semantic_transaction(tx, deadline).await?;
+                Ok(Ok(true))
+            })
+        }).await?
+    }
+}
+
+fn read_only_pin_allows(
+    stored: &super::quest_state::QuestStateRecord,
+    catalogue: &oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::QuestStateCatalogue,
+    quest: &str,
+) -> Result<bool, DurabilityError> {
+    let current_hash = catalogue
+        .definition_hash(quest)
+        .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    // QUEST-STATE-0 §6: unchanged definitions retain their historical content
+    // pin; completed quests keep access even when the definition changes.
+    Ok(stored.definition_hash == current_hash || stored.completed)
+}

+            "content-current",
+            vec![QuestTrack {
+                key: "oteryn:quest-progress/fixture/read-only".into(),
+                quest: quest.into(),
+                initial: -1,
+                min: -1,
+                max: 13,
+            }],
+            vec![],
+        )
+        .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
+        let mut stored = super::super::quest_state::QuestStateRecord {
+            pinned_content_revision: "content-previous".into(),
+            definition_hash: catalogue.definition_hash(quest).ok_or("hash")?,
+            completed: false,
+        };
+        assert_ne!(stored.pinned_content_revision, catalogue.content_revision());

+            read_only_pin_allows(&stored, &catalogue, quest)

+        );

+        assert!(
+            !read_only_pin_allows(&stored, &catalogue, quest)

+        );
+        stored.completed = true;
+        assert!(
+            read_only_pin_allows(&stored, &catalogue, quest)
+                .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
+        );
+        Ok(())
+    }

+//! Explicit source charges initialize the timed owner in the claim's mint.
+//! An absent state still means the definition default for legacy rewards.
+use super::*;
+use serde_json::{Value, json};
+
+pub(super) fn plan(request: &RewardClaimMintRequest) -> Value {
+    Value::Array(
+        std::iter::once((0, request.root_charges_remaining))
+            .chain(request.contents.iter().enumerate().map(|(index, child)| {
+                (index + 1, child.charges_remaining)
+            }))
+            .filter_map(|(ordinal, charges)| {
+                charges.map(|charges| json!({"ordinal": ordinal, "charges_remaining": charges}))
+            })
+            .collect(),
+    )
+}
+
+pub(super) fn validate(request: &RewardClaimMintRequest) -> Result<()> {
+    for (facts, quantity, key, text, fluid, charges) in std::iter::once((
+        &request.item,
+        request.quantity,
+        request.key_number,
+        request.root_text.as_ref(),
+        request.root_fluid_subtype,
+        request.root_charges_remaining,
+    ))
+    .chain(request.contents.iter().map(|child| {
+        (
+            &child.item,
+            child.quantity,
+            child.key_number,
+            child.text.as_ref(),
+            child.fluid_subtype,
+            child.charges_remaining,
+        )
+    })) {
+        if charges.is_some_and(|charges| {
+            charges != 1
+                || quantity != 1
+                || facts.stack != ItemStackClass::NonStackable
+                || facts.container_capacity.is_some()
+                || key.is_some()
+                || text.is_some()
+                || fluid.is_some()
+                || facts.definition.family != "Item"
+                || facts.definition.revision_ref != "definition-r1"
+                || !matches!(
+                    facts.definition.production_key.as_str(),
+                    "oteryn:item.tibia.i3048"
+                        | "oteryn:item.tibia.i3054"
+                        | "oteryn:item.tibia.i3081"
+                        | "oteryn:item.tibia.i3082"
+                        | "oteryn:item.tibia.i3083"
+                        | "oteryn:item.tibia.i3085"
+                )
+        }) {
+            return Err(RewardClaimMintError::InvalidInput);
+        }
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+pub(super) async fn apply(
+    tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Postgres>,
+    frozen: &FrozenClaim,
+    reservation: &Reservation,
+) -> std::result::Result<(), DurabilityError> {
+    for (ordinal, charges) in std::iter::once((0, frozen.request.root_charges_remaining)).chain(
+        frozen.request.contents.iter().enumerate().map(|(index, child)| {
+            (index + 1, child.charges_remaining)
+        }),
+    ) {
+        let Some(charges) = charges else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        let item = if ordinal == 0 {
+            frozen.item_instance_id
+        } else {
+            contents::child_id(&reservation.transaction_id, ordinal, 3)
+        };
+        // The initialization guard proves this exact fresh mint against its
+        // immutable charges_plan; subsequent writes use timed state revisions.
+        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_item_timed_states(item_instance_id,charges,remaining_ms,deadline_at,state_revision) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,$2,NULL,NULL,1)")
+            .bind(item.as_slice())
+            .bind(i64::from(charges))
+            .execute(&mut **tx)
+            .await?;
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}

+//! Falcon's durable cycle receipt wraps the existing atomic custody and audit owner.
+//! Public Content identity stays unchanged. `:cycle:N` is an INTERNAL operation
+//! identity, derived only here after the six-source repeat gate, never a new
+//! Content definition. Each cycle retains the original key and frozen draw.
+use super::*;
+use oteryn_gameplay_quests::quest::falcon::{REWARDS, claim_key, draw_index, repeat_ready};
+
+impl DurabilityRoot {
+    pub async fn claim_falcon_chest(
+        &self,
+        authority: &ReconciledCharacterAuthority<'_, '_>,
+        node: &NodeIncarnationProof,
+        fence: CurrentCharacterItemFence,
+        requests: [RewardClaimMintRequest; 7],
+        weights: &dyn super::super::item_transfer::ItemWeightLookup,
+    ) -> Result<RewardClaimMintOutcome> {
+        validate_pool(&requests, weights)?;
+        let public_key = requests[0].claim.production_key.clone();
+        let command = requests[0].command;
+        let recovery = authority
+            .record_for(self)
+            .map_err(|_| RewardClaimMintError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+        let node = node.clone();
+        let mut context = weights;
+        self.try_issue_semantic_pass()?.run_with_context(&mut context, move |holder, deadline, weights| Box::pin(async move {
+            let mut tx = begin_type2_transaction(holder, deadline).await?;
+            assert_recovery_fence(&mut tx, &recovery).await?;
+            lock_admission_relations(&mut tx).await?;
+            lock_cause(&mut tx, command).await?;
+            let (world, channel) = scope_of(&fence).map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+            if !character_item_fence_is_current(&mut tx, &node, &fence, command,
+                fence.character_id, *world.as_bytes(), *channel.as_bytes()).await? {
+                return Ok(Err(RewardClaimMintError::AuthorityRejected));
+            }
+            // Current session/lease/scope fences precede both receipt and draw replay.
+            if let Some(receipt) = load_receipt(&mut tx, command).await? {
+                let transaction: String = receipt.try_get("transaction_id")?;
+                let cycle = sqlx::query("SELECT cycle,selected_index,entropy FROM game_character_falcon_chest_cycles WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND chest_key=$2 AND transaction_id=$3::uuid")
+                    .bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(&public_key).bind(transaction)
+                    .fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?;
+                let Some(cycle) = cycle else { return Ok(Err(RewardClaimMintError::ConflictingCause)); };
+                let request = cycle_request(&requests, &cycle)?;
+                if receipt.try_get::<Vec<u8>,_>("intent_binding")? != intent_binding(&request, fence.character_id).map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)? {
+                    return Ok(Err(RewardClaimMintError::ConflictingCause));
+                }
+                let committed = decode_receipt(&receipt)?;
+                tx.commit(deadline).await?;
+                return Ok(Ok(RewardClaimMintOutcome::AlreadyCommitted(committed)));
+            }
+            if load_reservation(&mut tx, command).await?.is_some() { return Ok(Err(RewardClaimMintError::ConflictingCause)); }
+            let now: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT game_falcon_now_ms()").fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+            let previous = sqlx::query("SELECT cycle,selected_index,entropy,claimed_at FROM game_character_falcon_chest_cycles WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND chest_key=$2 ORDER BY cycle DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE")
+                .bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(&public_key).fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?;
+            let pending = previous.as_ref().is_some_and(|row| row.try_get::<Option<i64>,_>("claimed_at").is_ok_and(|at| at.is_none()));
+            if let Some(previous) = &previous
+                && let Some(claimed) = previous.try_get::<Option<i64>,_>("claimed_at")?
+                && !repeat_ready(claimed, now) {
+                return Ok(Err(RewardClaimMintError::Refused(RewardClaimRefusal::AlreadyClaimed)));
+            }
+            let cycle = if pending {
+                previous.ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?
+            } else {
+                let number = match previous { Some(row) => row.try_get::<i64,_>("cycle")?.checked_add(1).ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?, None => 1 };
+                let entropy: Vec<u8> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT sha256(uuid_send(gen_random_uuid()) || uuid_send(gen_random_uuid()))").fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+                let bytes: [u8;32] = entropy.as_slice().try_into().map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+                sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_character_falcon_chest_cycles(character_id,chest_key,cycle,entropy,selected_index,drawn_at) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6) RETURNING cycle,selected_index,entropy")
+                    .bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(&public_key).bind(number).bind(entropy)
+                    .bind(i16::try_from(draw_index(&bytes)).map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?).bind(now)
+                    .fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?
+            };
+            let request = cycle_request(&requests, &cycle)?;
+            let binding = intent_binding(&request, fence.character_id).map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+            let row = sqlx::query("SELECT game_character_uuid_v7()::text AS transaction_id,game_character_uuid_v7()::text AS event_id,game_character_uuid_v7()::text AS item_instance_id").fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+            let reservation = Reservation { character_id: fence.character_id, world_id: *world.as_bytes(), channel_id: *channel.as_bytes(),
+                transaction_id: uuid_text(row.try_get("transaction_id")?)?, event_id: uuid_text(row.try_get("event_id")?)?,
+                item_instance_id: uuid_text(row.try_get("item_instance_id")?)?, occurred_at_unix_ms: now, work_units_used: 0 };
+            let (destination, _) = match admit(&mut tx, &node, &fence, &request, &reservation).await? {
+                Ok(admitted) => admitted,
+                Err(error) => { tx.commit(deadline).await?; return Ok(Err(error)); }
+            };
+            if let Err(error) = contents::check_capacity(&mut tx, fence.character_id, reservation.world_id, &request, *weights).await? {
+                // A refusal freezes only the draw. No reservation, item or cooldown is written.
+                tx.commit(deadline).await?; return Ok(Err(error));
+            }
+            insert_reservation(&mut tx, &request, &binding, &reservation).await?;
+            sqlx::query("UPDATE game_reward_claim_mint_reservations SET work_units_used=1 WHERE game_session_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND command_id=$2::text::numeric(20,0)")
+                .bind(command.game_session_id().as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(command.command_id().get().to_string()).execute(&mut **tx).await?;
+            let frozen = FrozenClaim { request, character_id: fence.character_id, transaction_id: reservation.transaction_id,
+                event_id: reservation.event_id, item_instance_id: reservation.item_instance_id, occurred_at_unix_ms: now, intent_binding: binding };
+            let tuple = tx.tuple();
+            let message = claim_message(&frozen, &reservation, &fence, destination);
+            let envelope = audit::encode_reward_claim_mint_event(RewardClaimMintEventIdentity {
+                event_id: frozen.event_id, transaction_id: frozen.transaction_id, occurred_at_unix_ms: now,
+                channel_id: reservation.channel_id, server_build_id: SERVER_BUILD_ID }, message, tuple.tuple())
+                .map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+            let committed = apply_claim(&mut tx, &frozen, &reservation, destination, &envelope, tuple).await?;
+            let updated = sqlx::query("UPDATE game_character_falcon_chest_cycles SET claimed_at=$4,transaction_id=encode($5,'hex')::uuid WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND chest_key=$2 AND cycle=$3 AND claimed_at IS NULL")
+                .bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(public_key).bind(cycle.try_get::<i64,_>("cycle")?).bind(now).bind(frozen.transaction_id.as_slice()).execute(&mut **tx).await?;
+            if updated.rows_affected()!=1 { return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState); }
+            tx.commit(deadline).await?;
+            Ok(Ok(RewardClaimMintOutcome::Committed(committed)))
+        })).await?
+    }
+}
+
+fn cycle_request(
+    requests: &[RewardClaimMintRequest; 7],
+    row: &sqlx::postgres::PgRow,
+) -> std::result::Result<RewardClaimMintRequest, DurabilityError> {
+    let index = usize::try_from(row.try_get::<i16, _>("selected_index")?)
+        .map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    let entropy: Vec<u8> = row.try_get("entropy")?;
+    let entropy: [u8; 32] = entropy
+        .try_into()
+        .map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    if index != draw_index(&entropy) {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    }
+    let mut request = requests
+        .get(index)
+        .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?
+        .clone();
+    let cycle: i64 = row.try_get("cycle")?;
+    if cycle < 1 {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    }
+    request.claim.production_key = format!("{}:cycle:{cycle}", request.claim.production_key);
+    Ok(request)
+}
+
+fn validate_pool(
+    requests: &[RewardClaimMintRequest; 7],
+    weights: &dyn super::super::item_transfer::ItemWeightLookup,
+) -> Result<()> {
+    let first = &requests[0];
+    let chest = claim_key(&first.claim.production_key).ok_or(RewardClaimMintError::InvalidInput)?;
+    let pin = format!(
+        "sha256:{}",
+        weights
+            .generation_digest()
+            .iter()
+            .map(|b| format!("{b:02x}"))
+            .collect::<String>()
+    );
+    if first.claim.family != "RewardClaim"
+        || first.source_placement != chest.source()
+        || first.content_revision != pin
+        || first.achievement.is_some()
+        || first.quest_transition.is_some()
+        || first.key_number.is_some()
+        || !first.contents.is_empty()
+        || first.root_text.is_some()
+        || first.root_fluid_subtype.is_some()
+        || first.root_charges_remaining.is_some()
+    {
+        return Err(RewardClaimMintError::InvalidInput);
+    }
+    for (request, (id, count)) in requests.iter().zip(REWARDS) {
+        validate_request(request)?;
+        let mut expected = first.clone();
+        expected.item = request.item.clone();
+        expected.quantity = count;
+        if request != &expected
+            || request.item.definition.family != "Item"
+            || request.item.definition.production_key != format!("oteryn:item.tibia.i{id}")
+        {
+            return Err(RewardClaimMintError::InvalidInput);
+        }
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}

+//! Source-resolved fluid rewards initialize the existing fluid owner in the
+//! once-claim transaction. Empty rewards retain their legacy intent bytes.
+use super::*;
+use serde_json::{Value, json};
+
+pub(super) fn plan(request: &RewardClaimMintRequest) -> Value {
+    Value::Array(
+        std::iter::once((0, request.root_fluid_subtype))
+            .chain(
+                request
+                    .contents
+                    .iter()
+                    .enumerate()
+                    .map(|(index, child)| (index + 1, child.fluid_subtype)),
+            )
+            .filter_map(|(ordinal, subtype)| {
+                subtype.map(|subtype| json!({"ordinal": ordinal, "subtype": subtype}))
+            })
+            .collect(),
+    )
+}
+
+pub(super) fn validate(request: &RewardClaimMintRequest) -> Result<()> {
+    for (facts, quantity, key, text, subtype) in std::iter::once((
+        &request.item,
+        request.quantity,
+        request.key_number,
+        request.root_text.as_ref(),
+        request.root_fluid_subtype,
+    ))
+    .chain(request.contents.iter().map(|child| {
+        (
+            &child.item,
+            child.quantity,
+            child.key_number,
+            child.text.as_ref(),
+            child.fluid_subtype,
+        )
+    })) {
+        if subtype.is_some_and(|subtype| {
+            subtype == 0
+                || quantity != 1
+                || facts.stack != ItemStackClass::NonStackable
+                || facts.container_capacity.is_some()
+                || key.is_some()
+                || text.is_some()
+        }) {
+            return Err(RewardClaimMintError::InvalidInput);
+        }
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+pub(super) async fn apply(
+    tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Postgres>,
+    frozen: &FrozenClaim,
+    reservation: &Reservation,
+) -> std::result::Result<(), DurabilityError> {
+    for (ordinal, subtype) in std::iter::once((0, frozen.request.root_fluid_subtype)).chain(
+        frozen
+            .request
+            .contents
+            .iter()
+            .enumerate()
+            .map(|(index, child)| (index + 1, child.fluid_subtype)),
+    ) {
+        let Some(subtype) = subtype else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        let (item, transaction) = if ordinal == 0 {
+            (frozen.item_instance_id, reservation.transaction_id)
+        } else {
+            (
+                contents::child_id(&reservation.transaction_id, ordinal, 3),
+                contents::child_id(&reservation.transaction_id, ordinal, 1),
+            )
+        };
+        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_item_fluid_states(item_instance_id,world_id,fluid_subtype,last_transaction_id) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid,$3,encode($4,'hex')::uuid)")
+            .bind(item.as_slice()).bind(reservation.world_id.as_slice())
+            .bind(i16::from(subtype)).bind(transaction.as_slice()).execute(&mut **tx).await?;
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
