+//! Book of Wisdom repeat receipt, composed with the existing atomic reward MINT.
+use super::*;
+const CLAIM: &str = "oteryn:reward-claim.tutorial.viola-book";
+const SOURCE: &str = "oteryn:placement.tutorial.viola-book.32614-32419-7";
+
+impl DurabilityRoot {
+    pub async fn claim_viola_book(
+        &self,
+        authority: &ReconciledCharacterAuthority<'_, '_>,
+        node: &NodeIncarnationProof,
+        fence: CurrentCharacterItemFence,
+        request: RewardClaimMintRequest,
+        weights: &dyn super::super::item_transfer::ItemWeightLookup,
+    ) -> Result<RewardClaimMintOutcome> {
+        validate_book(&request, weights)?;
+        let command = request.command;
+        let recovery = authority
+            .record_for(self)
+            .map_err(|_| RewardClaimMintError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+        let node = node.clone();
+        let mut context = weights;
+        self.try_issue_semantic_pass()?.run_with_context(&mut context, move |holder, deadline, weights| Box::pin(async move {
+            let mut tx = begin_type2_transaction(holder, deadline).await?;
+            assert_recovery_fence(&mut tx, &recovery).await?;
+            lock_admission_relations(&mut tx).await?;
+            lock_cause(&mut tx, command).await?;
+            let (world, channel) = scope_of(&fence).map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+            if !character_item_fence_is_current(&mut tx, &node, &fence, command,
+                fence.character_id, *world.as_bytes(), *channel.as_bytes()).await? {
+                return Ok(Err(RewardClaimMintError::AuthorityRejected));
+            }
+            // Current session/lease/scope fences precede receipt replay.
+            if let Some(receipt) = load_receipt(&mut tx, command).await? {
+                let transaction: String = receipt.try_get("transaction_id")?;
+                let cycle: Option<i64> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT cycle FROM game_character_viola_book_cycles WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND transaction_id=$2::uuid")
+                    .bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(transaction)
+                    .fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?;
+                let Some(cycle) = cycle else { return Ok(Err(RewardClaimMintError::ConflictingCause)); };
+                let request = cycle_request(&request, cycle)?;
+                if receipt.try_get::<Vec<u8>,_>("intent_binding")? != intent_binding(&request, fence.character_id).map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)? {
+                    return Ok(Err(RewardClaimMintError::ConflictingCause));
+                }
+                let committed = decode_receipt(&receipt)?;
+                tx.commit(deadline).await?;
+                return Ok(Ok(RewardClaimMintOutcome::AlreadyCommitted(committed)));
+            }
+            if load_reservation(&mut tx, command).await?.is_some() { return Ok(Err(RewardClaimMintError::ConflictingCause)); }
+            let now: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT game_viola_book_now_ms()").fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+            let previous: Option<(i64,i64)> = sqlx::query_as("SELECT cycle,claimed_at FROM game_character_viola_book_cycles WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid ORDER BY cycle DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE")
+                .bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?;
+            if previous.is_some_and(|(_,at)| i128::from(now) < i128::from(at)+300_000) {
+                return Ok(Err(RewardClaimMintError::Refused(RewardClaimRefusal::AlreadyClaimed)));
+            }
+            let cycle = match previous { Some((number,_)) => number.checked_add(1).ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?, None => 1 };
+            let request = cycle_request(&request, cycle)?;
+            let binding = intent_binding(&request, fence.character_id).map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+            let row = sqlx::query("SELECT game_character_uuid_v7()::text AS transaction_id,game_character_uuid_v7()::text AS event_id,game_character_uuid_v7()::text AS item_instance_id").fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+            let reservation = Reservation { character_id: fence.character_id, world_id: *world.as_bytes(), channel_id: *channel.as_bytes(),
+                transaction_id: uuid_text(row.try_get("transaction_id")?)?, event_id: uuid_text(row.try_get("event_id")?)?,
+                item_instance_id: uuid_text(row.try_get("item_instance_id")?)?, occurred_at_unix_ms: now, work_units_used: 0 };
+            let (destination, _) = match admit(&mut tx, &node, &fence, &request, &reservation).await? {
+                Ok(admitted) => admitted,
+                Err(error) => return Ok(Err(error)),
+            };
+            if let Err(error) = contents::check_capacity(&mut tx, fence.character_id, reservation.world_id, &request, *weights).await? {
+                return Ok(Err(error));
+            }
+            insert_reservation(&mut tx, &request, &binding, &reservation).await?;
+            sqlx::query("UPDATE game_reward_claim_mint_reservations SET work_units_used=1 WHERE game_session_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND command_id=$2::text::numeric(20,0)")
+                .bind(command.game_session_id().as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(command.command_id().get().to_string()).execute(&mut **tx).await?;
+            let frozen = FrozenClaim { request, character_id: fence.character_id, transaction_id: reservation.transaction_id,
+                event_id: reservation.event_id, item_instance_id: reservation.item_instance_id, occurred_at_unix_ms: now, intent_binding: binding };
+            let tuple = tx.tuple();
+            let message = claim_message(&frozen, &reservation, &fence, destination);
+            let envelope = audit::encode_reward_claim_mint_event(RewardClaimMintEventIdentity {
+                event_id: frozen.event_id, transaction_id: frozen.transaction_id, occurred_at_unix_ms: now,
+                channel_id: reservation.channel_id, server_build_id: SERVER_BUILD_ID }, message, tuple.tuple())
+                .map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+            let committed = apply_claim(&mut tx, &frozen, &reservation, destination, &envelope, tuple).await?;
+            sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_character_viola_book_cycles(character_id,cycle,claimed_at,transaction_id) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,$2,$3,encode($4,'hex')::uuid)")
+                .bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(cycle).bind(now).bind(frozen.transaction_id.as_slice()).execute(&mut **tx).await?;
+            tx.commit(deadline).await?;
+            Ok(Ok(RewardClaimMintOutcome::Committed(committed)))
+        })).await?
+    }
+}
+
+fn cycle_request(
+    request: &RewardClaimMintRequest,
+    cycle: i64,
+) -> std::result::Result<RewardClaimMintRequest, DurabilityError> {
+    if cycle < 1 {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    }
+    let mut request = request.clone();
+    request.claim.production_key = format!("{}:cycle:{cycle}", request.claim.production_key);
+    Ok(request)
+}
+
+fn validate_book(
+    request: &RewardClaimMintRequest,
+    weights: &dyn super::super::item_transfer::ItemWeightLookup,
+) -> Result<()> {
+    validate_request(request)?;
+    let pin = format!(
+        "sha256:{}",
+        weights
+            .generation_digest()
+            .iter()
+            .map(|b| format!("{b:02x}"))
+            .collect::<String>()
+    );
+    if request.claim.family != "RewardClaim"
+        || request.claim.production_key != CLAIM
+        || request.claim.revision_ref != "reward-claim-r1"
+        || request.source_placement != SOURCE
+        || request.item.definition.family != "Item"
+        || request.item.definition.production_key != "oteryn:item.tibia.i51977"
+        || request.item.definition.revision_ref != "definition-r1"
+        || request.quantity != 1
+        || request.content_revision != pin
+        || request.achievement.is_some()
+        || request.quest_transition.is_some()
+        || request.key_number.is_some()
+        || !request.contents.is_empty()
+        || request.root_text.is_some()
+        || request.root_fluid_subtype.is_some()
+        || request.root_charges_remaining.is_some()
+    {
+        return Err(RewardClaimMintError::InvalidInput);
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}

+/// Trusted preparation only. Preparing this value never consumes an entitlement.

+impl TutorialCorruptorClaim {
+    /// The exact vocation gear selected under the current Character fence.
+    pub fn items(&self) -> &[u32] {
+        &self.items
+    }
+
+    /// Bind guaranteed outputs to distinct first-roll planner source ordinals.
+    pub fn bind_draw_ordinals(mut self, first: u32) -> Option<Self> {
+        self.draw_ordinals = (0..self.items.len())
+            .map(|offset| first.checked_add(u32::try_from(offset).ok()?))
+            .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
+        Some(self)
+    }
+
+    /// Selected Item IDs paired with their distinct first-roll planner sources.
+    pub fn source_outputs(&self) -> impl ExactSizeIterator<Item = (u32, u32)> + '_ {
+        self.items
+            .iter()
+            .copied()
+            .zip(self.draw_ordinals.iter().copied())
+    }
+}

-            if !defined.is_empty() && defined!=corruptor_items(build.vocation()) {return Ok(Err(CharacterProgressionError::InvalidInput));}
-            let ids=defined.iter().map(|id|i32::try_from(*id)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map_err(|_|super::DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;

-            commit_semantic_transaction(tx,deadline).await?;
-            Ok(Ok(defined))
-        })).await?
+        self.try_issue_semantic_pass()?
+            .run(move |holder, deadline| {
+                Box::pin(async move {
+                    let mut tx = begin_semantic_transaction(holder, deadline).await?;
+                    assert_recovery_fence(&mut tx, &recovery).await?;
+                    lock_admission_relations(&mut tx).await?;
+                    if let Err(error) = assert_gameplay_fence(&mut tx, &fence, &node).await? {
+                        return Ok(Err(error));
+                    }
+                    let prior = prior_claim(&mut tx, fence, death).await?;
+                    let items = if let Some(items) = prior {
+                        items
+                    } else {
+                        let TutorialProgressOutcome::Read(Some(stored)) =
+                            tutorial_progress_in_transaction(&mut tx, fence.character_id, None)
+                                .await?
+                        else {
+                            return Ok(Ok(None));
+                        };
+                        if !stored.progress.corruptor {
+                            return Ok(Ok(None));
+                        }
+                        let build =
+                            super::character_death::load_death_build(&mut tx, fence.character_id)
+                                .await?;
+                        corruptor_items(build.vocation()).to_vec()
+                    };
+                    commit_semantic_transaction(tx, deadline).await?;
+                    Ok(Ok((!items.is_empty()).then_some(TutorialCorruptorClaim {
+                        fence,
+                        death,
+                        items,
+                        draw_ordinals: Vec::new(),
+                    })))
+                })
+            })
+            .await?
+    }
+}
+
+async fn prior_claim(
+    tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Postgres>,
+    fence: CurrentCharacterGameplayFence,
+    death: CreatureDeathOccurrenceKey,
+) -> Result<Option<Vec<u32>>, DurabilityError> {
+    let channel = death
+        .channel_id()
+        .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    let prior: Option<Vec<i32>> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT item_ids FROM game_tutorial_corruptor_claims WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND world_id=encode($2,'hex')::uuid AND channel_id=encode($3,'hex')::uuid AND scope_generation=$4::text::numeric(20,0) AND actor_id=$5 AND actor_generation=$6::text::numeric(20,0)")
+        .bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(death.world_id().as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(channel.as_bytes().as_slice())
+        .bind(death.scope_ownership_generation().get().to_string()).bind(i64::from(death.actor_local_id())).bind(death.actor_local_generation().to_string())
+        .fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?;
+    prior
+        .map(|ids| {
+            ids.into_iter()
+                .map(|id| u32::try_from(id).map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState))
+                .collect()
+        })
+        .transpose()
+}
+
+/// Called only inside the same root-locked transaction that inserted the corpse
+/// and its entire frozen gear set. Any subsequent MINT failure rolls this back too.
+pub(super) async fn consume_in_transaction(
+    tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Postgres>,
+    node: &NodeIncarnationProof,
+    claim: &TutorialCorruptorClaim,
+) -> Result<(), DurabilityError> {
+    let fence = claim.fence;
+    assert_gameplay_fence(tx, &fence, node)
+        .await?
+        .map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    if let Some(prior) = prior_claim(tx, fence, claim.death).await? {
+        if prior != claim.items {
+            return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+        }
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    let build = super::character_death::load_death_build(tx, fence.character_id).await?;
+    if claim.items.is_empty() || claim.items != corruptor_items(build.vocation()) {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
     }
+    let TutorialProgressOutcome::Read(Some(stored)) =
+        tutorial_progress_in_transaction(tx, fence.character_id, None).await?
+    else {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    };
+    let next = tutorial_transition(
+        true,
+        Some(stored.progress),
+        TutorialEvent::Corruptor,
+        "none",
+        false,
+    )
+    .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    let death = claim.death;
+    let channel = death
+        .channel_id()
+        .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    let ids: Vec<i32> = claim
+        .items
+        .iter()
+        .map(|id| i32::try_from(*id).map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState))
+        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
+    sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_tutorial_corruptor_claims(character_id,world_id,channel_id,scope_generation,actor_id,actor_generation,item_ids) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid,encode($3,'hex')::uuid,$4::text::numeric(20,0),$5,$6::text::numeric(20,0),$7)")
+        .bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(death.world_id().as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(channel.as_bytes().as_slice())
+        .bind(death.scope_ownership_generation().get().to_string()).bind(i64::from(death.actor_local_id())).bind(death.actor_local_generation().to_string())
+        .bind(ids).execute(&mut **tx).await?;
+    tutorial_progress_in_transaction(
+        tx,
+        fence.character_id,
+        Some((stored.version, next.progress)),
+    )
+    .await?;
+    Ok(())
 }

+//! Lost Wisdom: source NPC state, exact item hand-in and native XP in one transaction.
+use super::character_authority::{ReconciledCharacterAuthority, assert_recovery_fence};
+use super::character_progression::{
+    CharacterProgressionError, CurrentCharacterGameplayFence, ExperienceAwardRequest,
+    ExperienceRewardOccurrence, assert_gameplay_fence,
+};
+use super::db::{begin_type2_transaction, lock_admission_relations};
+use super::item_craft::{ItemCraftRequest, audit};
+use super::item_loop_move::{ItemLoopContent, carried_weight};
+use super::item_mint::TypedDefinitionRef;
+use super::item_transfer::{read_derived_transfer_capacity_centi_oz, scope_of};
+use super::runtime_scope_assignment::NodeIncarnationProof;
+use super::{DurabilityError, DurabilityRoot};
+use crate::domain::CharacterId;

+#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum ViolaAction {
+    Mission,
+    Accept,
+}
+impl ViolaAction {
+    fn key(self) -> i16 {
+        match self {
+            Self::Mission => 1,
+            Self::Accept => 2,
+        }
+    }
+}
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum ViolaReply {
+    Offer,
+    Accepted,
+    MissingBook,
+    Completed,
+    AlreadyCompleted,
+    NoRoom,
+    ReturnOffer,
+}
+impl ViolaReply {
+    fn code(self) -> i16 {
+        match self {
+            Self::Offer => 0,
+            Self::Accepted => 1,
+            Self::MissingBook => 2,
+            Self::Completed => 3,
+            Self::AlreadyCompleted => 4,
+            Self::NoRoom => 5,
+            Self::ReturnOffer => 6,
+        }
+    }
+    fn stored(code: i16) -> Result<Self, DurabilityError> {
+        Ok(match code {
+            0 => Self::Offer,
+            1 => Self::Accepted,
+            2 => Self::MissingBook,
+            3 => Self::Completed,
+            4 => Self::AlreadyCompleted,
+            5 => Self::NoRoom,
+            6 => Self::ReturnOffer,
+            _ => return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+impl DurabilityRoot {
+    /// A historical response never grants a new write or chooses a new book.
+    pub async fn reconcile_viola(
+        &self,
+        authority: &ReconciledCharacterAuthority<'_, '_>,
+        character: CharacterId,
+        command: CommandRef,
+    ) -> Result<Option<ViolaReply>, CharacterProgressionError> {
+        let recovery = authority
+            .record_for(self)
+            .map_err(|_| CharacterProgressionError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+        self.try_issue_semantic_pass()?
+            .run(move |holder, deadline| {
+                Box::pin(async move {
+                    let mut tx = super::db::begin_semantic_transaction(holder, deadline).await?;
+                    assert_recovery_fence(&mut tx, &recovery).await?;
+                    let result = load(&mut tx, character, command, None).await?;
+                    super::db::commit_semantic_transaction(tx, deadline).await?;
+                    Ok(result)
+                })
+            })
+            .await
+            .map_err(Into::into)
+    }
+
+    /// Called under the Character revision slot after exact placed-NPC authorization.
+    #[allow(
+        clippy::too_many_arguments,
+        reason = "the existing recovery, node, revision, item and progression owners share one NPC transaction"
+    )]
+    pub async fn commit_viola<C: ItemLoopContent, const N: usize>(
+        &self,
+        authority: &ReconciledCharacterAuthority<'_, '_>,
+        node: &NodeIncarnationProof,
+        fence: CurrentCharacterGameplayFence,
+        command: CommandRef,
+        action: ViolaAction,
+        policy: FiniteProgressionPolicy<String, N>,
+        content: &C,
+        output: TypedDefinitionRef,
+        digest: [u8; 32],
+    ) -> Result<ViolaReply, CharacterProgressionError> {
+        if command.game_session_id() != fence.game_session_id
+            || content.loop_content_digest() != digest
+            || output.family != "Item"
+            || output.production_key != "oteryn:item.tibia.i3572"
+        {
+            return Err(CharacterProgressionError::InvalidInput);
+        }
+        let recovery = authority
+            .record_for(self)
+            .map_err(|_| CharacterProgressionError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+        let node = node.clone();
+        let mut context = content;
+        self.try_issue_semantic_pass()?.run_with_context(&mut context,move |holder,deadline,content|Box::pin(async move {
+            let mut tx=begin_type2_transaction(holder,deadline).await?;
+            assert_recovery_fence(&mut tx,&recovery).await?;
+            lock_admission_relations(&mut tx).await?;
+            sqlx::query("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('oteryn:viola:'||encode($1,'hex'),0))").bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).execute(&mut **tx).await?;
+            if let Some(reply)=load(&mut tx,fence.character_id,command,Some(action)).await? {tx.commit(deadline).await?;return Ok(Ok(reply));}
+            if let Err(e)=assert_gameplay_fence(&mut tx,&fence,&node).await? {return Ok(Err(e));}
+            let stage:i16=sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT coalesce((SELECT stage FROM game_character_viola WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid),0::smallint)").bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+            let mut craft=None;
+            let mut xp=None;
+            let reply=match (stage,action) {
+                (2,_)=>ViolaReply::AlreadyCompleted,
+                (0,ViolaAction::Mission)=>ViolaReply::Offer,
+                (0,ViolaAction::Accept)=>{sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_character_viola(character_id,stage) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,1)").bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).execute(&mut **tx).await?;ViolaReply::Accepted},
+                (1,ViolaAction::Mission)=>ViolaReply::ReturnOffer,
+                (1,ViolaAction::Accept)=>{
+                    match super::character_equipment::assert_current_equipment_in_transaction(&mut tx,&recovery,&node,&fence.item_fence(),Some(command),digest).await {
+                        Ok(_)=>{},
+                        Err(super::character_equipment::EquipmentError::Rejected(_))=>return Ok(Err(CharacterProgressionError::AuthorityRejected)),
+                        Err(super::character_equipment::EquipmentError::Durability(e))=>return Err(e),
+                        Err(super::character_equipment::EquipmentError::Database(e))=>return Err(e.into()),
+                    }
+                    match exchange(&mut tx,fence,command,*content,&output,digest).await? {
+                        Exchange::Missing=>ViolaReply::MissingBook,
+                        Exchange::NoRoom=>ViolaReply::NoRoom,
+                        Exchange::Done(transaction)=>{
+                            let bytes:Vec<u8>=sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT uuid_send(game_character_uuid_v7())").fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+                            let occurrence=ExperienceRewardOccurrence::from_bytes(bytes.try_into().map_err(|_|DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?).map_err(|_|DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+                            let award=ExperienceAwardRequest {occurrence,amount:oteryn_simulation_determinism::ExactI64::new(800),context:policy.context.clone(),policy_revision:policy.policy_revision.clone(),reward_revision:policy.reward_revision.clone(),policy:policy.clone()};
+                            if let Err(e)=super::character_experience_transaction::commit_in_transaction(&mut tx,&node,fence,&award,false,None).await? {return Ok(Err(e));}
+                            sqlx::query("UPDATE game_character_viola SET stage=2 WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid").bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).execute(&mut **tx).await?;
+                            craft=Some(transaction);xp=Some(occurrence.as_bytes().to_vec());ViolaReply::Completed
+                        }
+                    }
+                },
+                _=>return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState),
+            };
+            sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_viola_receipts(character_id,game_session_id,command_id,action,reply,craft_transaction_id,xp_occurrence_id) VALUES(encode($1,'hex')::uuid,encode($2,'hex')::uuid,$3::text::numeric,$4,$5,$6::uuid,encode($7,'hex')::uuid)")
+                .bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(command.game_session_id().as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(command.command_id().get().to_string()).bind(action.key()).bind(reply.code()).bind(craft).bind(xp).execute(&mut **tx).await?;
+            tx.commit(deadline).await?;Ok(Ok(reply))
+        })).await?
+    }
+}
+
+async fn load(
+    tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Postgres>,
+    character: CharacterId,
+    command: CommandRef,
+    action: Option<ViolaAction>,
+) -> Result<Option<ViolaReply>, DurabilityError> {
+    let row:Option<(i16,i16)>=sqlx::query_as("SELECT action,reply FROM game_viola_receipts WHERE character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND game_session_id=encode($2,'hex')::uuid AND command_id=$3::text::numeric").bind(character.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(command.game_session_id().as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(command.command_id().get().to_string()).fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?;
+    row.map(|(stored, reply)| {
+        if action.is_some_and(|a| a.key() != stored) {
+            Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)
+        } else {
+            ViolaReply::stored(reply)
+        }
+    })
+    .transpose()
+}
+
+enum Exchange {
+    Missing,
+    NoRoom,
+    Done(String),
+}
+async fn exchange<C: ItemLoopContent>(
+    tx: &mut super::item_mint_audit::Type2Transaction<'_>,
+    fence: CurrentCharacterGameplayFence,
+    command: CommandRef,
+    content: &C,
+    output: &TypedDefinitionRef,
+    digest: [u8; 32],
+) -> Result<Exchange, DurabilityError> {
+    let (world, _) =
+        scope_of(&fence.item_fence()).map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    let source=sqlx::query("SELECT to_jsonb(i) AS before,uuid_send(i.item_instance_id) AS source,uuid_send(b.item_instance_id) AS parent,coalesce(e.placement_ordinal,n.placement_ordinal)::text AS ordinal,CASE WHEN e.item_instance_id IS NULL THEN to_jsonb(n) ELSE NULL END AS nested_source FROM game_item_container_slots b JOIN game_item_instances i ON i.world_id=b.world_id LEFT JOIN game_item_container_entries e ON e.item_instance_id=i.item_instance_id AND e.character_id=b.character_id AND e.parent_item_instance_id=b.item_instance_id AND e.world_id=b.world_id LEFT JOIN game_item_nested_entries n ON n.item_instance_id=i.item_instance_id AND n.world_id=b.world_id LEFT JOIN LATERAL game_item_loop_owner(n.parent_item_instance_id) owner ON n.item_instance_id IS NOT NULL WHERE b.character_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND i.world_id=encode($2,'hex')::uuid AND i.lifecycle=1 AND i.definition_family='Item' AND i.definition_production_key='oteryn:item.tibia.i51977' AND (e.item_instance_id IS NOT NULL OR (owner.owner_character=b.character_id AND owner.depth BETWEEN 1 AND 7)) ORDER BY e.placement_ordinal NULLS LAST,n.placement_ordinal,i.item_instance_id LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE OF i")
+        .bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(world.as_bytes().as_slice()).fetch_optional(&mut ***tx).await?;
+    let Some(source) = source else {
+        return Ok(Exchange::Missing);
+    };
+    let before: Value = source.try_get("before")?;
+    let parent: Vec<u8> = source.try_get("parent")?;
+    let nested: Option<Value> = source.try_get("nested_source")?;
+    let source_id: [u8; 16] = source
+        .try_get::<Vec<u8>, _>("source")?
+        .try_into()
+        .map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    let definition = TypedDefinitionRef {
+        family: "Item".into(),
+        production_key: "oteryn:item.tibia.i51977".into(),
+        revision_ref: before["definition_revision_ref"]
+            .as_str()
+            .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?
+            .into(),
+    };
+    let Some(book_weight) = content.loop_facts(&definition).and_then(|f| f.weight) else {
+        return Ok(Exchange::NoRoom);
+    };
+    let Some(scarf_weight) = content.loop_facts(output).and_then(|f| f.weight) else {
+        return Ok(Exchange::NoRoom);
+    };
+    let carried = carried_weight(tx, fence.character_id, *world.as_bytes(), content)
+        .await
+        .map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    let capacity = read_derived_transfer_capacity_centi_oz(tx, fence.character_id, content).await?;
+    if carried
+        .checked_sub(u64::from(book_weight))
+        .and_then(|v| v.checked_add(u64::from(scarf_weight)))
+        .is_none_or(|v| v > capacity)
+    {
+        return Ok(Exchange::NoRoom);
+    }
+    let quantity = before["quantity"]
+        .as_i64()
+        .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    let (occupied,highest):(i64,String)=sqlx::query_as("SELECT count(*),coalesce(max(placement_ordinal),0)::text FROM game_item_container_entries WHERE parent_item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid").bind(&parent).fetch_one(&mut ***tx).await?;
+    let bag:Value=sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT to_jsonb(i) FROM game_item_instances i WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid FOR UPDATE").bind(&parent).fetch_one(&mut ***tx).await?;
+    let bag = TypedDefinitionRef {
+        family: bag["definition_family"]
+            .as_str()
+            .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?
+            .into(),
+        production_key: bag["definition_production_key"]
+            .as_str()
+            .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?
+            .into(),
+        revision_ref: bag["definition_revision_ref"]
+            .as_str()
+            .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?
+            .into(),
+    };
+    if occupied + 1 - i64::from(quantity == 1 && nested.is_none())
+        > i64::from(
+            content
+                .loop_facts(&bag)
+                .and_then(|f| f.container_capacity)
+                .unwrap_or(0)
+                .min(20),
+        )
+    {
+        return Ok(Exchange::NoRoom);
+    }
+    let ordinal = if quantity == 1 && nested.is_none() {
+        source.try_get::<String, _>("ordinal")?
+    } else {
+        highest
+            .parse::<u64>()
+            .ok()
+            .and_then(|v| v.checked_add(1))
+            .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?
+            .to_string()
+    };
+    let (item,transaction,event,occurred):(String,String,String,i64)=sqlx::query_as("SELECT game_character_uuid_v7()::text,game_character_uuid_v7()::text,game_character_uuid_v7()::text,(extract(epoch FROM clock_timestamp())*1000)::bigint").fetch_one(&mut ***tx).await?;
+    let outputs = vec![
+        json!({"item":item,"key":output.production_key,"revision":output.revision_ref,"count":1,"ordinal":ordinal}),
+    ];
+    let request = ItemCraftRequest {
+        command,
+        source: source_id,
+        tool: None,
+        ingredient: None,
+        station: None,
+        intent: b"oteryn:npc.viola/mission".to_vec(),
+        gathering: None,
+        enchanting: None,
+    };
+    let envelope = audit::encode(
+        fence.item_fence(),
+        &request,
+        "oteryn:npc-handoff.viola",
+        digest,
+        &before,
+        &outputs,
+        &transaction,
+        &event,
+        occurred,
+        tx.tuple().tuple(),
+    )?;
+    sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_item_craft_records(transaction_id,event_id,game_session_id,command_id,character_id,world_id,intent,recipe,source_item_instance_id,parent_item_instance_id,source_ordinal,before_item,outputs,occurred_at,envelope_sha256,event_source_location) VALUES($1::uuid,$2::uuid,encode($3,'hex')::uuid,$4::text::numeric,encode($5,'hex')::uuid,encode($6,'hex')::uuid,$7,'oteryn:npc-handoff.viola',encode($8,'hex')::uuid,encode($9,'hex')::uuid,$10::text::numeric,$11,$12,$13,sha256($14),$15)")
+        .bind(&transaction).bind(&event).bind(command.game_session_id().as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(command.command_id().get().to_string()).bind(fence.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(world.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(&request.intent).bind(source_id.as_slice()).bind(&parent).bind(source.try_get::<String,_>("ordinal")?).bind(&before).bind(json!(outputs)).bind(occurred).bind(&envelope).bind(&nested).execute(&mut ***tx).await?;
+    sqlx::query("UPDATE game_item_instances SET quantity=quantity-1,lifecycle=CASE WHEN quantity=1 THEN 2 ELSE 1 END,last_transaction_id=$2::uuid WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid").bind(source_id.as_slice()).bind(&transaction).execute(&mut ***tx).await?;
+    if quantity == 1 {
+        let remove = if nested.is_some() {
+            "DELETE FROM game_item_nested_entries WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid"
+        } else {
+            "DELETE FROM game_item_container_entries WHERE item_instance_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid"
+        };

+        }

+        assert!(book.get("oteryn:npc.spectulus").is_none());

+//! Falcon Bastion's six accepted 24-hour random reward sources.
+use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
+
+pub const COOLDOWN_MS: i64 = 86_400_000;
+pub const CLAIM_PREFIX: &str =
+    "oteryn:reward-claim.quest.u11_80.the_secret_library.falcon_bastion.chests_timer.";
+pub const REWARDS: [(u16, u32); 7] = [
+    (9081, 1),
+    (28821, 1),
+    (28823, 1),
+    (9058, 1),
+    (6299, 1),
+    (3052, 1),
+    (3035, 10),
+];
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub struct Chest {
+    pub suffix: &'static str,
+    pub unique: u16,
+    pub appearance: u16,
+    pub position: (u16, u16, i8),
+}
+impl Chest {
+    pub fn key(self) -> String {
+        format!("{CLAIM_PREFIX}{}", self.suffix)
+    }
+    pub fn source(self) -> String {
+        // The existing native RewardClaim binder uses this same stable key.
+        format!(
+            "oteryn:placement/{}/0",
+            self.key().trim_start_matches("oteryn:")
+        )
+    }
+}
+
+// Crystal 201189f:chest.lua:1383-1429, accepted 24h repeat ruling PR2770 Q3/A.
+pub const CHESTS: [Chest; 6] = [
+    Chest {
+        suffix: "coast",
+        unique: 6105,
+        appearance: 23741,
+        position: (33352, 31318, -7),
+    },
+    Chest {
+        suffix: "island",
+        unique: 6106,
+        appearance: 23740,
+        position: (33384, 31285, -7),
+    },
+    Chest {
+        suffix: "throne_hall",
+        unique: 6107,
+        appearance: 23741,
+        position: (33366, 31323, -5),
+    },
+    Chest {
+        suffix: "shortcut",
+        unique: 6108,
+        appearance: 23741,
+        position: (33374, 31340, -4),
+    },
+    Chest {
+        suffix: "lower_bastion",
+        unique: 6109,
+        appearance: 23740,
+        position: (33324, 31268, -8),
+    },
+    Chest {
+        suffix: "underground_bastion",
+        unique: 6110,
+        appearance: 23740,
+        position: (33308, 31304, -9),
+    },
+];
+
+pub fn claim_key(key: &str) -> Option<Chest> {
+    CHESTS.into_iter().find(|chest| chest.key() == key)
+}
+
+/// The accepted cooldown starts at successful delivery, not the first draw.
+pub fn repeat_ready(claimed_at: i64, now: i64) -> bool {
+    claimed_at
+        .checked_add(COOLDOWN_MS)
+        .is_some_and(|next| now >= next)
+}
+
+/// Uniform one-of-seven selection. Rejection sampling avoids modulo bias; hashing
+/// a rejected block makes the frozen 32-byte entropy a complete replay recipe.
+pub fn draw_index(entropy: &[u8; 32]) -> usize {
+    let mut block = *entropy;
+    loop {
+        if let Some(byte) = block.iter().find(|byte| **byte < 252) {
+            return usize::from(*byte % 7);
+        }
+        block = Sha256::digest(block).into();
+    }
+}
+
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod tests {
+    #[test]
+    fn repeat_opens_at_exactly_twenty_four_hours() {
+        let claimed = 10_000;
+        assert!(!super::repeat_ready(
+            claimed,
+            claimed + super::COOLDOWN_MS - 1
+        ));
+        assert!(super::repeat_ready(claimed, claimed + super::COOLDOWN_MS));
+        assert!(super::repeat_ready(
+            claimed,
+            claimed + super::COOLDOWN_MS + 1
+        ));
+        assert!(!super::repeat_ready(i64::MAX, i64::MAX));
+    }
+    #[test]
+    fn seven_entry_draw_rejects_modulo_bias() {
+        let mut counts = [0; 7];
+        for byte in 0..252 {
+            counts[super::draw_index(&[byte; 32])] += 1;
+        }
+        assert_eq!(counts, [36; 7]);
+        assert!(super::draw_index(&[255; 32]) < 7);
+    }
+    #[test]
+    fn six_chests_have_the_exact_seven_entry_pool() {
+        assert_eq!(super::CHESTS.len(), 6);
+        assert_eq!(
+            super::REWARDS,
+            [
+                (9081, 1),
+                (28821, 1),
+                (28823, 1),
+                (9058, 1),
+                (6299, 1),
+                (3052, 1),
+                (3035, 10)
+            ]
+        );
+    }
+}

         super::rotten_blood::ACCESS_DOOR_TRACK.into(),

     );
+    lowered.catalogue = super::npc_rules::extend(lowered.catalogue, &lowered.source_tracks)
+        .map_err(QuestLoadError::Json)?;
+    // Preserve source captures and their counts; only the production catalogue
+    // and its executable bindings are filtered by the owner's target scope.
+    let npc_legacy = std::mem::take(&mut lowered.catalogue.npc_legacy);
+    lowered.catalogue = QuestStateCatalogue::new(
+        content_revision,
+        lowered
+            .catalogue
+            .tracks
+            .values()
+            .filter(|track| super::in_runtime_scope(&track.quest))
+            .cloned()
+            .collect(),
+        lowered
+            .catalogue
+            .transitions
+            .values()
+            .filter(|transition| super::in_runtime_scope(&transition.quest))
+            .cloned()
+            .collect(),
+    )
+    .map_err(QuestLoadError::Catalogue)?;
+    lowered.catalogue.npc_legacy = npc_legacy;
+    lowered
+        .source_tracks
+        .retain(|key, _| lowered.catalogue.track(key).is_some());
+    lowered
+        .requested_by
+        .retain(|key, _| lowered.catalogue.transition(key).is_some());
     Ok(lowered)

+    /// Preserve the source's absent value while accepting the complete native
+    /// track range shared with other quest owners. Source bytes keep their digest.
+    pub fn with_native_bounds(
+        mut self,
+        native: &super::QuestStateCatalogue,
+    ) -> Result<Self, NpcRuleError> {
+        for program in &mut self.programs {
+            for track in &mut program.document.tracks {
+                let definition = native
+                    .track(&track.key)
+                    .ok_or_else(|| NpcRuleError::MissingTrack(track.key.clone()))?;
+                if definition.quest != track.quest
+                    || (definition.initial != track.initial
+                        && !(definition.initial == 0
+                            && track.initial == -1
+                            && native
+                                .npc_legacy
+                                .get(&track.quest)
+                                .is_some_and(|(_, defaults)| defaults.get(&track.key) == Some(&0))))
+                    || definition.min > track.min
+                    || definition.max < track.max
+                {
+                    return Err(NpcRuleError::Invalid(format!(
+                        "native NPC track refinement: {}",
+                        track.key
+                    )));
+                }
+                track.min = definition.min;
+                track.max = definition.max;
+            }
+        }
+        Ok(self)
+    }
+

+    /// Parse and validate immutable source content once before owner transactions.
+    pub fn embedded() -> Result<&'static Self, NpcRuleError> {
+        static SOURCE: std::sync::OnceLock<Result<NpcRuleCatalog, NpcRuleError>> =
+            std::sync::OnceLock::new();
+        SOURCE
+            .get_or_init(|| Self::from_json_documents(NPC_RULE_PROGRAMS))
+            .as_ref()
+            .map_err(Clone::clone)
+    }

+    /// A..M programs with read-compatible legacy definitions, plus the already active Stag.
+    pub fn runtime() -> Result<&'static Self, NpcRuleError> {
+        static RUNTIME: std::sync::OnceLock<Result<NpcRuleCatalog, NpcRuleError>> =
+            std::sync::OnceLock::new();
+        RUNTIME
+            .get_or_init(|| {
+                let mut catalogue = Self::load_embedded()?;

+                        p.document.quest.as_str(),
+                        "oteryn:quest.a_father_s_burden_quest"

+                            | "oteryn:quest.the_order_of_the_stag_quest"
+                    )
+                });
+                Ok(catalogue)
+            })
+            .as_ref()
+            .map_err(Clone::clone)

+/// Refine only original source-qualified tracks; preserve every wider native bound.
+pub(super) fn extend(
+    catalogue: super::QuestStateCatalogue,
+    source_tracks: &BTreeMap<String, String>,
+) -> Result<super::QuestStateCatalogue, String> {
+    let source = NpcRuleCatalog::runtime().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+    let mut legacy = catalogue.npc_legacy.clone();
+    for program in source.programs() {
+        let quest = &program.document.quest;
+        if quest != "oteryn:quest.the_order_of_the_stag_quest"
+            && let Some(hash) = catalogue.definition_hash(quest)
+        {
+            legacy.insert(
+                quest.clone(),
+                (
+                    hash,
+                    catalogue
+                        .tracks
+                        .values()
+                        .filter(|track| &track.quest == quest)
+                        .map(|track| (track.key.clone(), track.initial))
+                        .collect(),
+                ),
+            );
+        }
     }
+    let mut tracks = catalogue.tracks.clone();
+    let mut transitions = catalogue.transitions.clone();
+    for program in source.programs() {
+        for track in &program.document.tracks {
+            if let Some(native) = tracks.get_mut(&track.key) {
+                let original = track
+                    .key
+                    .strip_prefix("oteryn:")
+                    .map(|tail| format!("canary:{tail}"));
+                if native.quest != track.quest
+                    || (native.initial != track.initial
+                        && !(native.initial == 0
+                            && source_tracks.get(&track.key) == original.as_ref()))
+                {
+                    return Err(format!("NPC source track conflict: {}", track.key));
+                }
+                // Keep the old catalogue's absent value for every existing native
+                // consumer. Only source NPC planning uses its donor absent value.
+                native.min = native.min.min(track.min);
+                native.max = native.max.max(track.max);
+            } else {
+                tracks.insert(
+                    track.key.clone(),
+                    super::QuestTrack {
+                        key: track.key.clone(),
+                        quest: track.quest.clone(),
+                        initial: track.initial,
+                        min: track.min,
+                        max: track.max,
+                    },
+                );
+            }
+        }
+        // An unexecutable definition marker binds every guard, reply, effect and
+        // source reference into the existing quest definition hash.
+        let first = program
+            .document
+            .tracks
+            .first()
+            .ok_or("NPC program has no tracks")?;
+        let digest = program
+            .digest
+            .iter()
+            .map(|byte| format!("{byte:02x}"))
+            .collect::<String>();
+        let key = format!("oteryn:quest-npc-pin/{digest}");
+        if transitions
+            .insert(
+                key.clone(),
+                super::QuestTransition {
+                    key,
+                    quest: program.document.quest.clone(),
+                    effects: vec![super::QuestEffect {
+                        track: first.key.clone(),
+                        from: super::QuestComparison::Any,
+                        effect: super::QuestEffectKind::Computed,
+                    }],
+                    completes: false,
+                    experience: None,
+                },
+            )
+            .is_some()
+        {
+            return Err("duplicate NPC source pin".into());
+        }
+    }
+    let mut active = super::QuestStateCatalogue::new(
+        catalogue.content_revision,
+        tracks.into_values().collect(),
+        transitions.into_values().collect(),
+    )
+    .map_err(|e| format!("{e:?}"))?;
+    active.npc_legacy = legacy;
+    Ok(active)
+}
+
+/// Only an exact pre-activation definition can retain its old absent-track values.
+/// Existing rows, their values, pins and receipts are never rewritten.
+pub fn legacy_defaults<'a>(
+    catalogue: &'a super::QuestStateCatalogue,
+    quest: &str,
+    stored_hash: &[u8; 32],
+) -> Option<&'a BTreeMap<String, i64>> {
+    catalogue
+        .npc_legacy
+        .get(quest)
+        .filter(|(hash, _)| hash == stored_hash)
+        .map(|(_, defaults)| defaults)
 }

+#[test]
+fn npc_activation_keeps_exact_legacy_hashes_defaults_and_rejects_incompatible_rollback()
+-> Result<(), String> {
+    let text = include_str!("../../../../../../content/quests/missions/quest-state.json");
+    let old = super::super::loader::parse_quest_state(text, "npc-legacy")
+        .map_err(|e| format!("{e:?}"))?;
+    let document: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(text).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+    let mut bindings = BTreeMap::new();
+    for quest in document["quests"].as_array().ok_or("quests")? {
+        for track in quest["tracks"].as_array().ok_or("tracks")? {
+            bindings.insert(
+                track["key"].as_str().ok_or("key")?.to_owned(),
+                track["source_key"].as_str().ok_or("source")?.to_owned(),
+            );
+        }
+    }
+    let active = extend(old.catalogue().clone(), &bindings)?;
+    let runtime = NpcRuleCatalog::runtime().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+    assert_eq!(runtime.programs().len(), 7);
+    assert_eq!(active.npc_legacy.len(), 6);
+    let mut defaults = BTreeMap::new();
+    for track in NpcRuleCatalog::embedded()
+        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
+        .programs()
+        .iter()
+        .flat_map(|program| &program.document.tracks)
+    {
+        *defaults.entry(track.initial).or_insert(0usize) += 1;
+    }
+    assert_eq!(defaults, BTreeMap::from([(-1, 180), (0, 50)]));
+    for program in NpcRuleCatalog::embedded()
+        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
+        .programs()
+    {
+        let quest = &program.document.quest;
+        if let Some((hash, defaults)) = active.npc_legacy.get(quest) {
+            assert_eq!(Some(*hash), old.catalogue().definition_hash(quest));
+            assert_ne!(Some(*hash), active.definition_hash(quest));
+            assert_eq!(legacy_defaults(&active, quest, hash), Some(defaults));
+            assert!(legacy_defaults(&active, quest, &[0; 32]).is_none());
+            // Old artifacts cannot accept a new pin: rollback requires this verified
+            // compatibility adapter, never activation of an incompatible old binary.
+            assert!(
+                legacy_defaults(
+                    old.catalogue(),
+                    quest,
+                    &active.definition_hash(quest).ok_or("hash")?
+                )
+                .is_none()
+            );
+            for track in old
+                .catalogue()
+                .tracks
+                .values()
+                .filter(|t| &t.quest == quest)
+            {
+                assert_eq!(defaults.get(&track.key), Some(&track.initial));
+                let new = active.track(&track.key).ok_or("track")?;
+                assert_eq!(
+                    new.initial, track.initial,
+                    "all existing native consumers keep their absent value"
+                );
+                assert!(new.min <= track.min && new.max >= track.max);
+            }
+            for transition in old
+                .catalogue()
+                .transitions
+                .values()
+                .filter(|t| &t.quest == quest)
+            {
+                assert_eq!(active.transition(&transition.key), Some(transition));
+            }
+        } else if runtime.program(quest).is_none() {
+            assert_eq!(
+                active.definition_hash(quest),
+                old.catalogue().definition_hash(quest)
+            );
+        }
+    }
+    runtime
+        .clone()
+        .with_native_bounds(&active)
+        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+    let loaded = super::super::loader::load_embedded_quest_state("npc-legacy")
+        .map_err(|e| format!("{e:?}"))?;
+    assert_eq!(loaded.catalogue().npc_legacy.len(), 6);
+    Ok(())
+}

+"""Bound Newhaven item overlay; historical starter definitions remain independent.
+
+Current Wiki item identities and portable/equipment facts win over Crystal's
+older server IDs. Wiki revision IDs below are the exact source cuts selected by
+QUESTPC. The sole appearance translation is explicit: current Wiki Simple Jo
+Staff Wiki Item51166 names current15.30 appearance51119, `simple jo staff`,
+take/clothes, sprite239746. Crystal201189f8 independently agrees on appearance
+51119 (sprite238626). Appearance51166 is an unrelated immovable object. Existing
+Game identity and presentation bindings retain i51119; Wiki external ID51166
+is a separate namespace.
+
+Viola: Newhaven Quest/Spoiler111294/revid1205117 selects Book51977 -> Scarf3572
+and800 XP. This module supplies item facts only, never a claim or starter grant.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import copy
+
+CLIENT_SHA256 = '2dfa943b548472a1ddc7bc5afe97945bc75e14f1f41d74f728f8e622f5dae7e2'
+
+CORRUPTOR_ITEMS = {
+    'knight': (3359, 3285, 3375), 'paladin': (3571, 51760),
+    'sorcerer': (3074, 7991), 'druid': (3066, 7991),
+    'monk': (50195, 51119),
+}
+APPEARANCE_TRANSLATIONS = {51166: 51119}
+# Crystal201189f8 items.xml:87366-84: source48 on the first three
+# containers. Owner-approved native30 projection; final stage has no container.
+CORPSE_CHAIN = (52113, 52114, 52115, 52116)
+CORPSE_SOURCE_CAPACITY = 48
+CORPSE_NATIVE_CAPACITY = 30
+# Already-admitted canonical ordinary loot needs an exact native carrying policy
+# too. Preserve all its source semantics, including stack100 and food108s.
+CANONICAL_LOOT_ITEMS = {3723: 'white mushroom'}
+# id: name, weight in hundredths of an ounce, primary slot, vocations,
+# minimum level (None = source did not specify), Wiki page, revision.
+# Crystal corpus9f5a72c6 items.xml SHA256 c847293e980b40ec146e2b7f68a62366513a1c0566d16b7c3a011136087021eb
+# declares no level gate on these armor/helmet/cloak/robe/scarf moveevents,
+# and no vocation gate on armor/helmet/sword/bow/scarf. Empty means unrestricted;
+# preserve the current Wiki's explicit level0 choice for the Monk items.
+SPEC = {
+    3359: ('brass armor', 8000, 'TORSO', (), 0, 1615, 1186985),
+    3285: ('longsword', 4200, 'WEAPON', (), 0, 2404, 1186983),
+    3375: ('soldier helmet', 3200, 'HEAD', (), 0, 2304, 1147311),
+    3571: ("ranger's cloak", 4800, 'TORSO', ('PALADIN',), 0, 25353, 1186981),
+    51760: ('refined bow', 3250, 'WEAPON', (), 0, 109332, 1148726),
+    3074: ('wand of vortex', 1900, 'WEAPON', ('SORCERER',), 6, 4939, 1186984),
+    7991: ("magician's robe", 2500, 'TORSO', ('DRUID', 'SORCERER'), 0, 25343, 1186986),
+    3066: ('snakebite rod', 1900, 'WEAPON', ('DRUID',), 6, 4937, 1147165),
+    50195: ('harmony amulet', 510, 'AMULET', ('MONK',), 0, 108150, 1194222),
+    51119: ('simple jo staff', 1800, 'WEAPON', ('MONK',), 0, 108393, 1186982),
+    51977: ('book of wisdom', 100, None, (), None, 109842, 1143205),
+    3572: ('scarf', 200, 'AMULET', (), 0, 2473, 1194228),
+}
+UNKNOWN_GROUPS = ('classification', 'container', 'equipment', 'weapon', 'protection',
+                  'skill_modifiers', 'charges', 'imbuement', 'temporal', 'use_transform',
+                  'trade_restrictions', 'fluid', 'readable_writeable')
+
+
+def known(value):
+    return {'state': 'KNOWN', 'value': value}
+
+
+def unknown():
+    return {'state': 'UNKNOWN'}
+
+
+def apply(definition):
+    """Apply one named Item overlay; fail before mutation on identity conflicts."""
+    identity = definition.get('identity', {})
+    key = identity.get('key', '')
+    if identity.get('family') != 'Item' or not key.startswith('oteryn:item.tibia.i'):
+        return False
+    try:
+        item = int(key.removeprefix('oteryn:item.tibia.i'))
+    except ValueError:
+        return False
+    if item in CORPSE_CHAIN:
+        name, weight, slot, vocations, level = 'dead the corruptor', None, None, (), None
+    elif item in SPEC:
+        name, weight, slot, vocations, level, _, _ = SPEC[item]
+    else:
+        return False
+    semantics = definition.get('semantics', {})
+    previous_name = semantics.get('presentation', {}).get('value', {}).get('name', {})
+    if previous_name.get('state') == 'KNOWN' and previous_name.get('value', '').lower() != name:
+        raise ValueError(f'NEWHAVEN_ITEM_NAME:{item}')
+    replacement = copy.deepcopy(definition)
+    semantics = replacement.setdefault('semantics', {})
+    for group in UNKNOWN_GROUPS:
+        semantics.setdefault(group, unknown())
+    semantics['physical'] = known({'weight': known(weight) if weight is not None else unknown(),
+        'movable': known(item not in CORPSE_CHAIN), 'pickupable': known(item not in CORPSE_CHAIN)})
+    if item in CORPSE_CHAIN:
+        semantics['container'] = (known({'capacity': known(CORPSE_NATIVE_CAPACITY)})
+                                  if item != CORPSE_CHAIN[-1] else {'state': 'NOT_APPLICABLE'})
+    else:
+        # The exact served client's flags are checked by verify_client below.
+        semantics['container'] = {'state': 'NOT_APPLICABLE'}
+    semantics['stack'] = known({'stackable': known(False), 'stack_max': {'state': 'NOT_APPLICABLE'}})
+    semantics['presentation'] = known({'name': known(name),
+        'description': semantics.get('presentation', {}).get('value', {}).get('description', unknown())})
+    if slot:
+        # Bow permits a quiver in the other hand; a jo staff reserves both hands.
+        semantics['equipment'] = known({'patterns': known([{
+            'pattern_id': 1, 'primary_slot': known(slot),
+            'additional_reserved_slots': known(['SHIELD'] if item == 51119 else []),
+            'mutually_exclusive_groups': known(['oteryn:equipment-group.non_quiver_left_hand']
+                                              if item == 51760 else []),
+            'vocations': known(list(vocations)),
+            'level': known(level) if level is not None else unknown(),
+            'compatibility_rule': unknown(),
+        }])})
+    if item == 51119:
+        weapon = {field: unknown() for field in ('ammunition', 'elemental', 'extra_defense',
+            'hit_chance', 'max_hit_chance', 'range')}
+        weapon.update({'weapon_type': known('FIST'), 'attack': known(12), 'defense': known(8)})
+        semantics['weapon'] = known(weapon)
+        semantics['skill_modifiers'] = known({'modifiers': known([{
+            'kind': 'SKILL_FIST', 'parameter': known({'kind': 'SIGNED_POINTS', 'value': 1}),
+            'evaluation_phase': unknown(), 'priority': unknown(), 'target_domain': unknown(),
+        }])})
+    replacement['materializable'] = True
+    replacement['stack_class'] = 'NonStackable'
+    definition.clear()
+    definition.update(replacement)
+    return True
+
+
+def verify_client(root):
+    """Prove each selected Item's portability and name on the exact served client."""
+    import hashlib
+    import sys
+
+    path = root / f'content/assets/files/appearances-{CLIENT_SHA256}.dat'
+    raw = path.read_bytes()
+    if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != CLIENT_SHA256:
+        raise ValueError('NEWHAVEN_CLIENT_PIN')
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(root / 'tools/content-schema/item-authoring'))
+    from engine_items import load_appearance_objects
+    objects = load_appearance_objects(raw)
+    names = {item: spec[0] for item, spec in SPEC.items()}
+    names.update(CANONICAL_LOOT_ITEMS)
+    for item, name in names.items():
+        record = objects.get(item, {})
+        flags = record.get('flags', {})
+        if (record.get('name', '').lower() != name
+                or flags.get('flags.take') is not True
+                or flags.get('flags.unmove', False)
+                or flags.get('flags.container', False)):
+            raise ValueError(f'NEWHAVEN_CLIENT_IDENTITY:{item}')
+    for item in CORPSE_CHAIN:
+        flags = objects.get(item, {}).get('flags', {})
+        if (flags.get('flags.corpse') is not True
+                or bool(flags.get('flags.container')) != (item != CORPSE_CHAIN[-1])
+                or (item == CORPSE_CHAIN[0] and flags.get('flags.unmove') is not True)):
+            raise ValueError(f'NEWHAVEN_CLIENT_CORPSE:{item}')
+
+
+def apply_native(root, generated, profile_path, manifest_path, provider_order, canonical_bytes):
+    """Mount exact named Newhaven Items in the existing native provider document."""
+    import hashlib
+    import json
+
+    verify_client(root)
+    canonical = {}
+    for path in sorted((root / 'content/items/definitions').glob('items-*.json')):
+        for row in json.loads(path.read_bytes())['records']:
+            definition = row['definition']
+            key = definition['identity']['key']
+            if key in {f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}' for item in (*SPEC, *CORPSE_CHAIN, *CANONICAL_LOOT_ITEMS)}:
+                if key in canonical:
+                    raise ValueError('NEWHAVEN_DUPLICATE_ITEM')
+                canonical[key] = copy.deepcopy(definition)
+    bindings = {int(row['external_id']): row for row in json.loads(
+        (root / 'imports/crystalserver/bindings/items.json').read_bytes())['bindings']}
+    taxonomy = {row['target']['key']: row['source_taxonomy'] for row in json.loads(
+        (root / 'content/items/taxonomy/items.json').read_bytes())['records']}
+    document = json.loads(generated[profile_path])
+    indexed = {row['production_definition']['production_key']: row for row in document['records']}
+    for item in sorted((*SPEC, *CORPSE_CHAIN, *CANONICAL_LOOT_ITEMS)):
+        key = f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}'
+        definition = canonical[key]
+        if item in CANONICAL_LOOT_ITEMS:
+            if definition['materializable'] is not True:
+                raise ValueError(f'NEWHAVEN_CANONICAL_LOOT_INACTIVE:{item}')
+        else:
+            apply(definition)
+        external = item
+        binding = copy.deepcopy(bindings[external])
+        if binding['target'] != definition['identity']:
+            raise ValueError('NEWHAVEN_ITEM_BINDING')
+        previous = indexed.get(key)
+        row = copy.deepcopy(previous) if previous else {
+            'authoring': {'item': copy.deepcopy(definition['identity'])},
+            'attributes': {'speed_bonus': 0},
+            'production_binding': binding, 'production_binding_qualification': None,
+            'production_definition': {'family': 'Item', 'production_key': key,
+                                      'revision_ref': definition['identity']['revision']},
+        }
+        row['semantics'] = copy.deepcopy(definition['semantics'])
+        row['admission'] = {'materializable': True, 'stack_class': definition['stack_class'],
+            'legal_destinations': ['Ground'] if item in CORPSE_CHAIN else ['CharacterInventory', 'Ground']}
+        if key in taxonomy:
+            row['authoring']['taxonomy'] = copy.deepcopy(taxonomy[key])
+        if item == 51119:
+            row['authoring']['taxonomy'] = {'primary': 'Fist Fighting Weapons'}
+        indexed[key] = row
+    document['records'] = sorted(indexed.values(), key=provider_order)
+    raw = (json.dumps(document, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n').encode()
+    generated[profile_path] = raw
+    manifest = json.loads(generated[manifest_path])
+    manifest['item_profiles']['sha256'] = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
+    generated[manifest_path] = canonical_bytes(manifest)

     # Keep an existing admission (notably i3081); its reward subtype stays explicitly held.
     if not facts["holds"]:
         definition["materializable"] = True
+    elif (facts['holds'] == ['INSTANCE_CHARGE_ATTRIBUTE_LOWERING_MISSING']
+          and not facts['stackable'] and facts['capacity'] is None
+          and facts['charges'] == NATIVE_FULL_CHARGE_DEFAULTS.get(definition['identity']['key'])):

+        definition['materializable'] = True
+    elif (facts['holds'] == ['INSTANCE_FLUID_SUBTYPE_LOWERING_MISSING']
+          and not facts['stackable'] and facts['capacity'] is None
+          and facts['charges'] is None
+          and definition['identity']['key'] in NATIVE_REWARD_FLUID_ITEMS):
+        put_group(definition, 'fluid', {'fluid_type': {'state': 'NOT_APPLICABLE'}})

+"""Newhaven item identity and compatibility regression tests."""
+import copy
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+import unittest
+
+import quest_finish_newhaven_items as newhaven
+
+
+class NewhavenItemTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_exact_current_client_proves_all_selected_portable_names(self):
+        newhaven.verify_client(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2])
+
+    def test_native_mount_preserves_existing_ranged_authoring(self):
+        root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+        profiles = 'content/items/definitions/spell-native-profiles.json'
+        manifest = 'content/spells.manifest.json'
+        generated = {path: (root / path).read_bytes() for path in (profiles, manifest)}
+        before = {row['production_definition']['production_key']: row for row in
+                  json.loads(generated[profiles])['records']}
+        newhaven.apply_native(root, generated, profiles, manifest,
+                              lambda row: row['production_definition']['production_key'],
+                              lambda value: (json.dumps(value) + '\n').encode())
+        after = {row['production_definition']['production_key']: row for row in
+                 json.loads(generated[profiles])['records']}
+        for item in newhaven.SPEC:
+            key = f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}'
+            self.assertTrue(after[key]['admission']['materializable'])
+            self.assertEqual(after[key]['production_binding']['target']['key'], key)
+            equipment = after[key]['semantics']['equipment']
+            if equipment['state'] == 'KNOWN':
+                for pattern in equipment['value']['patterns']['value']:
+                    vocations = pattern['vocations']
+                    if vocations['state'] == 'KNOWN':
+                        # ReferenceBaseVocation discriminants define loader order.
+                        order = ['DRUID', 'KNIGHT', 'MONK', 'PALADIN', 'SORCERER', 'NONE']
+                        self.assertEqual(vocations['value'], sorted(set(vocations['value']), key=order.index))
+        key = 'oteryn:item.tibia.i3066'
+        self.assertEqual(after[key]['authoring']['ranged'], before[key]['authoring']['ranged'])
+        for item in newhaven.CORPSE_CHAIN:
+            key = f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}'
+            self.assertEqual(after[key]['admission'], {
+                'materializable': True, 'stack_class': 'NonStackable',
+                'legal_destinations': ['Ground'],
+            })
+            self.assertEqual(after[key]['production_binding']['target']['key'], key)
+
+    def test_five_vocations_use_current_named_wiki_identities(self):
+        self.assertEqual(newhaven.CORRUPTOR_ITEMS, {
+            'knight': (3359, 3285, 3375), 'paladin': (3571, 51760),
+            'sorcerer': (3074, 7991), 'druid': (3066, 7991),
+            'monk': (50195, 51119),
+        })
+
+    def test_guaranteed_gear_has_complete_plain_equipment_requirements(self):
+        for item in {item for items in newhaven.CORRUPTOR_ITEMS.values() for item in items}:
+            with self.subTest(item=item):
+                definition = {'identity': {'family': 'Item', 'key': f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}'}}
+                self.assertTrue(newhaven.apply(definition))
+                semantics = definition['semantics']
+                self.assertEqual(semantics['container'], {'state': 'NOT_APPLICABLE'})
+                pattern, = semantics['equipment']['value']['patterns']['value']
+                self.assertEqual(pattern['level'], newhaven.known(6 if item in (3066, 3074) else 0))
+                self.assertEqual(pattern['vocations'], newhaven.known(list(newhaven.SPEC[item][3])))
+
+    def test_ordinary_mushroom_mount_preserves_canonical_stack_food_and_weight(self):
+        root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+        key = 'oteryn:item.tibia.i3723'
+        canonical = next(row['definition']
+            for path in (root / 'content/items/definitions').glob('items-*.json')
+            for row in json.loads(path.read_bytes())['records']
+            if row['definition']['identity']['key'] == key)
+        profiles = 'content/items/definitions/spell-native-profiles.json'
+        manifest = 'content/spells.manifest.json'
+        generated = {path: (root / path).read_bytes() for path in (profiles, manifest)}
+        newhaven.apply_native(root, generated, profiles, manifest,
+            lambda row: row['production_definition']['production_key'],
+            lambda value: (json.dumps(value) + '\n').encode())
+        selected = [row for row in json.loads(generated[profiles])['records']
+                    if row['production_definition']['production_key'] == key]
+        self.assertEqual(len(selected), 1)
+        self.assertEqual(selected[0]['semantics'], canonical['semantics'])
+        self.assertEqual(selected[0]['production_binding']['target'], canonical['identity'])
+        self.assertEqual(selected[0]['admission'], {'materializable': True,
+            'stack_class': 'StackCapable', 'legal_destinations': ['CharacterInventory', 'Ground']})
+        self.assertEqual(selected[0]['semantics']['stack']['value']['stack_max'], newhaven.known(100))
+        self.assertEqual(selected[0]['semantics']['physical']['value']['weight'], newhaven.known(40))
+
+    def test_complete_corruptor_chain_projects_source48_to_native30(self):
+        self.assertEqual(newhaven.CORPSE_SOURCE_CAPACITY, 48)
+        for item in (52113, 52114, 52115, 52116):
+            definition = {'identity': {'family': 'Item', 'key': f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}'}}
+            self.assertTrue(newhaven.apply(definition))
+            sem = definition['semantics']
+            self.assertEqual(sem['physical']['value']['movable'], newhaven.known(False))
+            self.assertEqual(sem['physical']['value']['pickupable'], newhaven.known(False))
+            self.assertTrue(definition['materializable'])
+            if item != 52116:
+                self.assertEqual(sem['container']['value']['capacity'], newhaven.known(30))
+            else:
+                self.assertEqual(sem['container'], {'state': 'NOT_APPLICABLE'})
+
+    def test_staff_appearance_is_explicitly_a_separate_donor_namespace(self):
+        self.assertEqual(newhaven.APPEARANCE_TRANSLATIONS[51166], 51119)
+        staff = {'identity': {'family': 'Item', 'key': 'oteryn:item.tibia.i51119'}}
+        newhaven.apply(staff)
+        self.assertEqual(staff['identity']['key'], 'oteryn:item.tibia.i51119')
+        self.assertTrue(staff['materializable'])
+        self.assertEqual(staff['semantics']['equipment']['value']['patterns']['value'][0]
+                         ['additional_reserved_slots']['value'], ['SHIELD'])
+
+    def test_unrelated_historical_starter_item_is_preserved(self):
+        original = {'identity': {'family': 'Item', 'key': 'oteryn:item.tibia.i50166'},
+                    'materializable': False, 'historical': ['unchanged']}
+        definition = copy.deepcopy(original)
+        self.assertFalse(newhaven.apply(definition))
+        self.assertEqual(definition, original)
+
+    def test_wrong_named_identity_is_rejected_before_any_mutation(self):
+        definition = {'identity': {'family': 'Item', 'key': 'oteryn:item.tibia.i3571'},
+                      'semantics': {'presentation': newhaven.known({'name': newhaven.known('mammoth tusk')})}}
+        original = copy.deepcopy(definition)
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'NEWHAVEN_ITEM_NAME'):
+            newhaven.apply(definition)
+        self.assertEqual(definition, original)
+
+    def test_apply_is_idempotent_and_preserves_other_semantic_groups(self):
+        definition = {'identity': {'family': 'Item', 'key': 'oteryn:item.tibia.i3359'},
+                      'semantics': {'protection': {'custom': 'preserve'}}}
+        self.assertTrue(newhaven.apply(definition))
+        original = copy.deepcopy(definition)
+        self.assertTrue(newhaven.apply(definition))
+        self.assertEqual(definition, original)
+        self.assertEqual(definition['semantics']['protection'], {'custom': 'preserve'})
+        self.assertEqual(definition['stack_class'], 'NonStackable')
+        self.assertTrue(definition['materializable'])
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    unittest.main()

+def corruptor_loot_projection(tables: dict, facts: dict, item_definitions: Path) -> list[dict]:
+    """Current named drops and the owner-approved native30 corpse projection.
+
+    Crystal201189f8 the_corruptor.lua:68-77 supplies chance/count fields; current
+    Wiki names resolve its four stale Item IDs (QUESTPC newhaven proposal).
+    Reference tables and their source48 corpse admission remain unchanged.
+    """
+    key = 'oteryn:loot.creature.the_corruptor'
+    table = copy.deepcopy(tables[key])
+    expected = {3359, 8820, 3375, 3074, 3723, 3338, 7618, 7620, 3031}
+    if ({entry['item']['key'] for entry in table['entries']}
+            != {f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}' for item in expected}
+            or len(table['entries']) != len(expected)):
+        raise ValueError('Corruptor source loot changed; reconcile its native projection')
+    for entry in table['entries']:
+        item = {7618: 266, 7620: 268, 8820: 7991}.get(
+            int(entry['item']['key'].removeprefix('oteryn:item.tibia.i')))
+        if item is not None:
+            entry['item'] = facts[f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}']['item']
+    # The original importer omitted unresolved source44773. Its named refined
+    # bow is current51760; absent source min/max uses the established1..1 default.
+    table['entries'].append({'item': facts['oteryn:item.tibia.i51760']['item'],
+                             'min_count': 1, 'max_count': 1, 'probability_ppm': 47600})
+    tables[key] = table
+    _, profiles = read(item_definitions / 'spell-native-profiles.json')
+    # Include every guaranteed-vocation item and each durable decay stage, even
+    # when the ordinary table cannot roll that item.
+    selected = (3359, 3285, 3375, 3571, 51760, 3074, 7991, 3066, 50195, 51119, 3338,
+                52113, 52114, 52115, 52116)
+    for item in selected:
+        fact = facts[f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}']
+        ref = fact['item']
+        exact = {'family': ref['family'], 'production_key': ref['key'],
+                 'revision_ref': ref['revision']}
+        rows = [row for row in profiles['records'] if row['production_definition'] == exact]
+        if len(rows) != 1 or rows[0]['admission']['materializable'] is not True:
+            raise ValueError(f'Corruptor requires one admitted exact native Item: {item}')
+        container = rows[0]['semantics'].get('container', {})
+        capacity = container.get('value', {}).get('capacity', {}) if container.get('state') == 'KNOWN' else {}
+        fact['materializable'] = True
+        fact['container_capacity'] = capacity['value'] if capacity.get('state') == 'KNOWN' else None
+        if item in (52113, 52114, 52115) and fact['container_capacity'] != 30:
+            raise ValueError('Corruptor corpse requires the explicit native30 projection')
+        if item == 52116 and fact['container_capacity'] is not None:
+            raise ValueError('Corruptor final decay stage is not a container')
+    return [facts[f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}']['item'] for item in selected]
+
+

+    def test_corruptor_projects_current_items_and_native_corpse_without_changing_source(self):
+        _, creatures = full.read(full.CREATURE_PROFILES)

+            self.assertTrue(items[f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}']['materializable'])
+            self.assertEqual(30, items[f'oteryn:item.tibia.i{item}']['container_capacity'])
+        self.assertIsNone(items['oteryn:item.tibia.i52116']['container_capacity'])
+        # Reference shards retain their identities and source admission. Only
+        # the native projection supplies these production reward facts.
+        source = full.item_facts()
+        self.assertFalse(source['oteryn:item.tibia.i52113']['materializable'])
+        for path in full.LOOT_DEFINITIONS.glob('loot-*.json'):
+            _, shard = full.read(path)
+            for row in shard['records']:
+                if row['definition']['identity'] == table['identity']:
+                    self.assertEqual(9, len(row['definition']['entries']))
+                    self.assertIn('oteryn:item.tibia.i8820',
+                                  {entry['item']['key'] for entry in row['definition']['entries']})
